# The Final Countdown — A Bible Handbook

_A topical compilation of Bible studies on the last-day events, in the style of
Stephen N. Haskell's "Bible Handbook": suggestive texts grouped under titled
separators, each with a short explanation._

These studies were compiled from the **Amazing Discoveries** video series
"The Final Countdown" and the "Judgment Day" ten-week Bible study. Each scripture
reference and Ellen G. White citation is drawn from what was taught and shown on
screen in the studies. They are not exhaustive; they present the line of texts
each study walked through, so the reader can take Bible in hand and follow.

> "The Bible Handbook is not an exhaustive study; but contains suggestive texts
> on important lines of thought." — S. N. Haskell

---

**Abbreviations for E. G. White references used in these studies**

- **GC** — The Great Controversy
- **DA** — The Desire of Ages
- **PP** — Patriarchs and Prophets
- **PK** — Prophets and Kings
- **AA** — The Acts of the Apostles
- **EW** — Early Writings
- **CL** — Country Living
- **T., v. _, p. _** — Testimonies for the Church (volume, page)
- **MH** — The Ministry of Healing
- **COL** — Christ's Object Lessons
- **CCh / 1888 / Mar** — other compilations as cited on screen

# The Final Countdown

## End-Time Events Timeline: The Beginning of the End — The Final Countdown, Study 1

### The Reward of the Wicked

> "Only with thine eyes shalt thou behold and see the reward of the wicked." This series traces every event from now until Christ's coming and beyond.

- **Ps. 91:8** — Only with our eyes will we behold the reward of the wicked; the faithful do not receive it, but Scripture warns that such a reward is real.
- **Dan. 12:1** — A time of trouble such as never was is coming, yet God's people will be delivered.
- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — Those who worship the beast and his image and receive his mark in hand or forehead shall drink the wine of the wrath of God.
- **Rev. 16:1, 2** — The vials of God's wrath are poured out on those who have the mark of the beast and worship his image.

### Two Books, One Prophecy

> "The books of Daniel and the Revelation are one. One is a prophecy, the other a revelation; one a book sealed, the other a book opened." — CTr 344.4

- **Dan. 12:4** — Daniel is told to shut up the words and seal the book to the time of the end, when many shall run to and fro and knowledge be increased; the time of the end is 1798.
- **Rev. 10:2** — In the angel's hand is a little book open, his right foot on the sea and his left on the earth — the companion open book to Daniel's sealed one.

### The Prophetic Scope of Daniel and Revelation

> An overview of where each chapter lands in history, so symbols in one book can be compared with the other.

- **Dan. 2** — The dream image of gold, silver, brass, and iron traces world kingdoms from Babylon to the end of time.
- **Dan. 7** — The lion, bear, leopard, and dreadful beast with ten horns and a little horn give closer focus on these kingdoms, especially until 1798.
- **Dan. 8** — The ram and he-goat carry the history from Medo-Persia through Greece and beyond.
- **Dan. 9** — The seventy weeks prophecy concerning Jesus and the sanctuary.
- **Dan. 11** — Beginning again at Medo-Persia ("there are three kings yet in Persia"), tracing kings through history.
- **Dan. 12** — Michael stands up, probation closes, and the time of trouble follows, with the time prophecies recapped.
- **Rev. 2-3** — The seven churches portray church history all the way to Christ's coming.
- **Rev. 5-8** — The seven seals, including the four horsemen of the apocalypse.
- **Rev. 8-9** — The seven trumpets.
- **Rev. 10-11** — History from around 1798 to 1844.
- **Rev. 12** — The history of pagan Rome.
- **Rev. 13** — Papal Rome and America enter the prophetic stage.
- **Rev. 14** — The 144,000 and the three final (three angels') messages; the 144,000 also appear in Rev. 7.
- **Rev. 15-18** — The judgment of the beast — a whole block of chapters on this last-day subject.
- **Rev. 20** — The thousand years, or millennium.
- **Rev. 21-22** — The new heaven and the new earth.

### Why and How We Study Prophecy

- **John 16:13** — The Spirit of truth guides into all truth and shows things to come; prophecy is for everyone willing to be led.
- **Dan. 12:10** — Many shall be purified and made white; the wicked shall not understand, but the wise shall understand.
- **James 1:5** — If any lack wisdom, let him ask of God, who gives liberally, and it shall be given him.
- **Rev. 1:1-3** — The Revelation of Jesus Christ shows His servants things which must shortly come to pass; blessed are they that hear and _keep_ — prophecy demands action. 8T 301
- **2 Tim. 3:16** — All Scripture is given by inspiration and is profitable for doctrine; all of it helps us understand prophecy. (Review and Herald, Nov 25, 1884, endorses Father Miller's Bible rules.)
- **Isa. 28:10** — Precept upon precept, line upon line, here a little and there a little — let the Bible explain the Bible.

### Bible Words and Their Meaning

> A foundational exercise: let Scripture define its own symbols. By Miller's Rule 11, a word is figurative when a literal reading would do violence to the laws of nature.

- **Dan. 7:17, 23** — The four great beasts are four kings, and the fourth beast is the fourth kingdom; a beast represents a kingdom.
- **Dan. 7:24** — The ten horns out of this kingdom are ten kings; horns are kings or divisions of a kingdom.
- **Rev. 17:15** — The waters where the harlot sits are peoples, multitudes, nations, and tongues; sea and waters mean peoples — so earth (dry ground) means an unpopulated area.
- **Jer. 6:2** — The daughter of Zion is likened to a comely and delicate woman; a woman represents God's church — pure or impure.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — God appoints each day for a year; a prophetic day equals a year, so 1260 days equals 1260 years.
- **Num. 14:34** — "Each day for a year" — the same day-for-a-year principle confirmed.
- **Isa. 28:7** — The priest and prophet err through wine and stumble in judgment; the wine of Babylon means false doctrine that corrupts one's view of prophecy. Drink instead the pure, unfermented wine of God's truth.

### A Preview of the Final Events

> Each event below is anchored to one key text; later studies will define the beast and prove each point from the Bible.

- **Rev. 13:15** — The image of the beast is given life, speaks, and causes that as many as would not worship it should be killed — the image of the beast and the death decree.
- **Rev. 13:16** — He causes all, small and great, rich and poor, free and bond, to receive a mark in their right hand or in their foreheads — the mark of the beast.
- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell except he that has the mark, the name of the beast, or the number of his name — the no-buy-no-sell crisis.
- **Dan. 12:1** — A time of trouble such as never was, after which God's people are delivered — the time of trouble.
- **Jer. 30:7** — That day is great, none is like it; it is the time of Jacob's trouble, but he shall be saved out of it.
- **Rev. 7:1-4** — Four angels hold the four winds until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads — 144,000 sealed; prophetic events are held back for the sealing.
- **Dan. 12:1; Matt. 25:10; Gen. 7:16** — When Michael (Jesus) stands up, probation closes; like the shut door at the wedding and the Lord shutting Noah in the ark, no one can then enter — the close of probation.
- **Rev. 15:1** — Seven angels have the seven last plagues, in which is filled up the wrath of God — the seven last plagues.
- **Rev. 17:12** — The ten horns are ten kings who receive no kingdom as yet but receive power as kings one hour with the beast — the coming new world order.

### A Diligent Study to Avoid the Mark

> "When God sends to men warnings... He requires every person endowed with reasoning powers to heed the message." — DD 37

- **Rev. 14:9-11** — The fearful judgments against worshiping the beast and his image should lead all to a diligent study of the prophecies, to learn what the mark of the beast is and how to avoid receiving it. DD 37
- **2 Tim. 4:3** — The time has fully come when men will not endure sound doctrine; the multitudes reject Bible truth because it interferes with worldly desires. DD 37

### Questions Answered: The Special Resurrection and the Seven Thunders

- **Dan. 12:1, 2** — Many (not all) who sleep in the dust shall awake, some to everlasting life and some to shame — a special resurrection around the time of trouble, raising both the righteous who died under the three angels' messages and those who pierced Christ.
- **Rev. 10:3, 4** — Seven thunders utter their voices, but John is told to seal them and write them not — the one sealing passage in Revelation, a window of events to be understood only after they occur.
- **Rev. 10:5-7** — The angel swears that there should be time no longer; when the seventh angel sounds, the mystery of God is finished — prophetic time ends about 1798 with the opening of Daniel's book. (CTr 344.4: the seven thunders were "a delineation of events that would transpire under the first and second angels' messages" — between 1837 and 1843.)

### Questions Answered: Beasts, Kingdoms, and the Stone

- **Dan. 8:1-7** — Daniel's vision of a ram and a flying he-goat breaks the laws of nature, signaling figurative, prophetic language.
- **Dan. 8:20, 21** — The ram is the kings of Media and Persia; the rough goat is the king of Grecia, and the great horn is its first king (Alexander) — proving a beast is a kingdom, interpreted in the same chapter.
- **Rev. 17:12** — The ten kings who reign one hour with the beast are the powers of the end-time; literal kingdom names like Medo-Persia, Grecia, and Rome are not figurative because they do no violence to nature.
- **Dan. 2** — The kingdoms run from Babylon through Medo-Persia, Greece, pagan and papal Rome, America, and the final ten kings (the same ten of Rev. 17); the stone that smites the image is God's kingdom, not yet fulfilled — we are still "in the feet."

---

## Who is The Beast of Revelation 13? — Study 2

### Why We Must Study the Beast

> "When God sends to men warnings so important that they are represented as proclaimed by holy angels flying in the midst of heaven, He requires every person endowed with reasoning powers to heed the message."

- **Ps. 91:8** — Only with thine eyes shalt thou behold and see the reward of the wicked; God's people watch the judgments fall but do not receive them. To stand on the right side, we must study and stay in the word.
- **Dan. 12:4** — Daniel was told to shut up the words and seal the book until the time of the end; Daniel is the book sealed.
- **Rev. 10:2** — The angel held a little book open; Revelation is the book opened, and it interprets Daniel. The two support and unlock each other.
- **Rev. 1:1** — This is the revelation of Jesus Christ, given to be revealed, not kept sealed.
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — He causeth all, small and great, rich and poor, to receive a mark in their right hand or forehead, and no man may buy or sell without it. To know the mark, we must first know who the beast is. _Darkness Before Dawn p. 37: "The fearful judgments denounced against the worship of the beast and his image should lead all to a diligent study of the prophecies to learn what the mark of the beast is, and how they are to avoid receiving it."_
- **2 Tim. 4:3** — The time will come when they will not endure sound doctrine; that time has fully come, and the multitudes turn away to fables.

### The Beast Described in Revelation 13

> A composite beast — leopard body, bear's feet, lion's mouth, seven heads, ten horns — cannot exist in nature, so it must be understood figuratively (Miller's Rule 11).

- **Rev. 13:1** — A beast rose up out of the sea, having seven heads and ten horns, ten crowns, and upon his heads the name of blasphemy.
- **Rev. 13:2** — The beast was like a leopard, with feet of a bear and mouth of a lion; the dragon gave him his power, seat, and great authority.
- **Rev. 13:3** — One of his heads was wounded to death, and his deadly wound was healed, and all the world wondered after the beast.
- **Rev. 13:4** — They worshipped the dragon and the beast, saying, Who is like unto the beast? who is able to make war with him? — proof it is a worshipped, religious entity.
- **Rev. 13:5** — A mouth was given him speaking great things and blasphemies, and power to continue forty and two months — a time prophecy.
- **Rev. 13:6** — He opened his mouth in blasphemy against God, His name, His tabernacle, and them that dwell in heaven.
- **Rev. 13:7** — It was given him to make war with the saints and to overcome them, with power over all kindreds, tongues, and nations.
- **Rev. 13:8** — All that dwell on the earth shall worship him, whose names are not written in the book of life of the Lamb slain from the foundation of the world.
- **Rev. 13:10** — He that leadeth into captivity shall go into captivity; here is the patience and faith of the saints.

### A Beast Is a Kingdom — Daniel Identifies It

- **Dan. 7:2-7** — Four great beasts rose from the sea: a lion, a bear, a leopard, and a dreadful fourth beast with great iron teeth and ten horns — the same animal symbols gathered into the one beast of Revelation 13.
- **Dan. 7:16, 17** — Daniel asked the truth of the vision and was given the interpretation; in Bible prophecy the interpretation is the literal truth.
- **Dan. 7:17** — These great beasts, which are four, are four kings which shall arise out of the earth.
- **Dan. 7:23** — The fourth beast shall be the fourth kingdom upon earth; therefore a beast represents a kingdom, and Revelation 13 describes a real kingdom doing real deeds.
- **Jer. 50:17** — Israel is scattered; the lion that devoured him is Nebuchadnezzar, king of Babylon — so the lion is Babylon, the first of the world kingdoms.
- **Dan. 2** — The same succession is given in the image: head of gold, breast and arms of silver, following Babylon, Medo-Persia, Grecia, then pagan Rome. Rome's iron is why the fourth beast has iron teeth.

### The Little Horn — Papal Rome

> Historically the power that took the throne after pagan Rome was papal Rome — the papacy.

- **Dan. 7:8** — Among the ten horns came up a little horn, before whom three of the first horns were plucked up by the roots; it had eyes like a man and a mouth speaking great things.
- **Dan. 7:21** — The same horn made war with the saints and prevailed against them.
- **Dan. 7:24** — The ten horns are ten kings (regions) of the Roman Empire — the ten Germanic tribes into which Rome divided.
- **Dan. 7:25** — He shall speak great words against the Most High, wear out the saints, think to change times and laws, and they shall be given into his hand until a time, times, and the dividing of time. _Hobbes, Leviathan: "the Papacy is no other than the ghost of the deceased Roman empire, sitting crowned upon the grave thereof." American Catholic Quarterly Review (April 1911): the Romans "asked him to rule them... commenced the temporal sovereignty of the popes."_

### The Three Horns Uprooted

- **Dan. 7:8, 24** — Three of the first horns were plucked up as the little horn rose; the Heruli (A.D. 493), the Vandals (A.D. 534), and the Ostrogoths (A.D. 538) were the three destroyed, clearing the way for the papacy.

### Testing the Beast — Blasphemy

- **John 10:33** — The Jews charged Jesus with blasphemy because, being a man, He made Himself God; so to claim to be God is blasphemy.
- **Rev. 13:5, 6** — The beast's mouth speaks blasphemies against God. _Lucius Ferraris: "The Pope... is not a mere man, but as it were God, and the vicar of God... king of kings." St. Alphonsus Liguori: "the priest has the power... of changing them from the slaves of Satan into children of God. And God Himself is obliged to abide by the judgment of His priests." Joseph Deharbe: "The priest does really and truly forgive the sins."_ — these claims fulfill the mark of blasphemy.

### Testing the Beast — Deadly Wound, World Wonder, Worship

- **Rev. 13:3** — The deadly wound: Pope Pius VI was taken captive by General Berthier on February 15, 1798, and died in prison — the head wounded to death exactly as prophesied.
- **Rev. 13:3** — All the world wondered: for over a thousand years the papacy was the foremost world kingdom, and the pope is still among the most followed leaders on earth.
- **Rev. 13:4, 8** — Worshipped and religious: the beast is worshipped, confirming it is a religious system.

### Testing the Beast — Reign of 42 Months (1260 Years)

- **Dan. 7:25** — Power given for a time, times, and the dividing of time.
- **Rev. 13:5** — Power given to continue forty and two months — the same period, worded differently, establishing the matter by two witnesses.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — A day for a year; so 42 months = 1260 days = 1260 years (a time = 360, times = 720, half a time = 180). The papal rule began in 538 (the year the last horn was uprooted) and ended with the deadly wound in 1798 — exactly 1260 years.

### Testing the Beast — Persecution of the Saints

- **Rev. 13:7; Dan. 7:21, 25** — The beast makes war with the saints and wears them out. _W.E.H. Lecky: "The Church of Rome has shed more innocent blood than any other institution that has ever existed among mankind." The Western Watchman defended the Inquisition: "We never thought it needed a defense."_ — an estimated hundred million perished for refusing papal doctrines.

### Testing the Beast — Changing God's Times and Law

- **Dan. 7:25** — The little horn would think to change times and laws. _Catholic Christian Instructed (chap. 23, p. 272): the warrant for keeping Sunday is "the authority of the Catholic Church." Catholic Record (Sept. 1, 1923): "Sunday is our mark of authority... The church is above the bible, and this transference of Sabbath observance is proof of that fact."_
- **Ex. 20:1-17** — The Ten Commandments: the Catholic catechism omits the second (against graven images), because of the idols in its temples, and splits the tenth in two to keep the count — changing God's law as prophesied. _The Convert's Catechism of Catholic Doctrine, p. 48._
- **Rev. 22:18, 19** — A warning not to take from the words of God lest one's name be removed from the book of life — why receiving this beast's mark is so grave.

### The Mark, the Plagues, and the Call Out

- **Rev. 13:16** — He causeth all to receive a mark in their right hand or forehead; knowing the beast makes the mark identifiable.
- **Rev. 14:9** — If any man worship the beast and his image and receive his mark, the same shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God.
- **Rev. 18:4** — Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins and receive not of her plagues — those marked share the beast's punishment.

### The Second Beast — Enforcer to Come

- **Rev. 13:11** — Another beast came up out of the earth, with two horns like a lamb — a new kingdom rising about 1798, when the first beast received its deadly wound.
- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell save he that has the mark, the name, or the number of the beast; this second power will enforce the no-buy, no-sell mark. Who it is, and what the mark is, is the subject of the next study.

### Questions Answered — The Number, the Heads, and the Final Kingdom

- **Rev. 13:18** — Here is wisdom: the number of the beast is the number of a man, six hundred threescore and six (666); the papal title _Vicarius Filii Dei_ totals 666 in Roman numerals. _See Uriah Smith, Daniel and Revelation, on Revelation 13._
- **Lev. 11:2, 3** — On the 613 laws: the sanitary and dietary laws (clean and unclean meats) still stand for health, though the ceremonial sacrificial laws were fulfilled at the cross.
- **Dan. 8:8** — On beasts versus horns: the he-goat's great horn was broken and four notable ones came up — a horn springs out of the body of a kingdom.
- **Dan. 8:20, 21** — The ram's two horns are the kings of Media and Persia (one kingdom, two kings); the rough goat is Grecia, its great horn the first king (Alexander) — so the beast is the main kingdom and the horns are kings over its regions.
- **Dan. 7:13, 14** — On Islam and Daniel 7:13: the Son of Man comes with the clouds to the Ancient of Days and receives an everlasting dominion — this is Jesus receiving the final kingdom, set in the judgment scene of Dan. 7:9-12.
- **Dan. 7:9-12** — The Ancient of Days (God the Father) sits, thrones are set, the judgment is set and the books are opened; the beast is slain and given to the burning flame.
- **Rev. 17:13, 14** — Ten kings of one mind give their power to the beast (the papacy) and make war with the Lamb, but the Lamb overcomes them, for He is Lord of lords and King of kings.
- **Rev. 17:15** — The waters where the woman sits are peoples, multitudes, nations, and tongues.
- **Rev. 17:16, 17** — The ten horns shall hate the whore, make her desolate, and burn her with fire, for God put it in their hearts to fulfill His will; when the words of God are fulfilled, the Lamb receives His kingdom and fills the whole earth.

## Revealing the Mark of the Beast — The Final Countdown, Study 3

### Introducing the Mark of the Beast

> "Only with thine eyes shalt thou behold and see the reward of the wicked." God tells us things ahead so we can be aware and act on them — prophecy is not merely head knowledge.

- **Ps. 91:8** — A promise to God's people: He shows us the end of the wicked ahead of time so we change how we live, not just what we know.
- **Dan. 12:4** — Daniel is the book _sealed_ until the time of the end; the two prophetic books are inseparable and interpret each other.
- **Rev. 10:2** — Revelation is the _book open_ — the little book in the angel's hand, opened to be understood with Daniel.
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — He causeth all to receive a mark in their right hand or forehead, so that no man may buy or sell without it; this is the central warning to understand.

### The Seal of the King — Foundation of the Mark of God

> We do not study the counterfeit to learn the truth; we study the true, and then we spot the counterfeit instantly. So we begin with the mark of God.

- **Gen. 41:40-42** — Pharaoh takes off his ring and puts it on Joseph's hand; the ring signifies that Joseph now acts in the name of the king.
- **Esther 3:10-12** — The king gives Haman his ring; whatever Haman writes is sealed with the king's ring and stands as if the king himself decreed it.
- **Esther 8:8** — What is written in the king's name and sealed with the king's ring no man can reverse; the seal denotes ownership and authority — what is done is done.

### God's Mark Is His Law and His Sabbath

- **Deut. 6:6-8** — God's words are bound for a sign upon the hand and as frontlets between the eyes — His sign is in what you do and what you think.
- **Ex. 13:9** — The sign upon the hand and the memorial between the eyes is "that the Lord's law may be in thy mouth"; the sign is the Lord's law.
- **Ezek. 20:20** — "Hallow my sabbaths; and they shall be a sign between me and you" — the Hebrew _oth_ (H226) means a distinguishing mark; God's mark is the Sabbath.
- **Ex. 20:8-11** — Remember the Sabbath day: the only commandment that says "remember" is the one the world forgot; it is rooted in creation, not given only to the Jews, and it sets God's people visibly apart.

### The Sealing — Old Testament and Last Day

- **Ezek. 9:1-4** — Amid the slaughter, the man with the writer's inkhorn sets a mark on the foreheads of those who sigh and cry for the abominations; those marked are spared.
- **Rev. 7:1-4** — At the end of time, the angel with the seal of the living God seals the servants of God in their foreheads; this is the same Sabbath sign, placed on the 144,000.

### Identifying the Beast and Its Counterfeit Mark

- **Rev. 13:1-3** — The leopard-like beast from the sea with seven heads and ten horns, healed of its deadly wound — established last study as the papal system.
- **Dan. 7:25** — This power speaks great words against the Most High, wears out the saints, and thinks "to change times and laws" — God's times and God's law, namely the Sabbath.
- **Rev. 17:3-5** — A woman (a church) arrayed in purple and scarlet, decked with gold and precious stones, with a golden cup of abominations, named Mystery Babylon, the mother of harlots — the same blasphemous power riding the beast.

> The papacy testifies of itself: "Sunday is our mark of authority... The church is above the Bible, and this transference of Sabbath observance is proof of that fact." (Catholic Record, Sept. 1, 1923). "What warrant have you for keeping Sunday preferably to the ancient Sabbath which was Saturday? — We have for it the authority of the Catholic Church." (Catholic Christian Instructed, ch. 23, p. 272). "Protestants do not realize that by observance of the Sunday, they accept the authority of the spokesperson of the Church, the Pope." (Our Sunday Visitor, Feb. 5, 1950). The Lateran inscription names her "the Mother and the Head of all churches of the city and the world."

### Who Has the Mark of the Beast?

> The mark is not yet received — the testing time has not yet come.

- **Ev 234.2** — No one has yet received the mark of the beast; there are true Christians in every church, and none are condemned until the decree enforcing the counterfeit Sabbath goes forth and the line is clearly drawn.
- **Rev. 13:16** — He causeth all — small and great, rich and poor — to receive a mark in their right hand or forehead; the law is aimed at the whole world, not only God's people.
- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — The third angel warns that whoever worships the beast and his image and receives his mark — in forehead (belief) or hand (compliance) — shall drink the wine of the wrath of God.
- **Rev. 14:12** — Here is the patience of the saints: they keep the commandments of God and the faith of Jesus — the differentiating factor stated right in Revelation.
- **Rev. 13:11, 14, 16, 17** — A second beast with two lamb-like horns speaks as a dragon and makes an image to the first beast, enforcing the mark and the no-buy-no-sell decree.
- **GC 605** — Keeping the false Sabbath in compliance with the law of the state is an avowal of allegiance to a power opposed to God; keeping the true Sabbath is loyalty to the Creator. One class receives the mark of the beast, the other the seal of God.

### A Warning to Heed and a Call to Prepare

> When God sends warnings proclaimed by holy angels flying in the midst of heaven, He requires every reasoning person to heed the message and to study the prophecies to learn what the mark of the beast is and how to avoid it. (Darkness Before Dawn p. 37; cf. GC 594.)

- **12MR 219.4** — When power is allied with wickedness it serves satanic agencies; the Protestant world has set up an idol Sabbath in the place of God's Sabbath and treads in the footsteps of the papacy — for this reason God's people are to move out of the cities into the country to cultivate the land and make haste to get ready for the crisis.
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — All these things happened to them for examples and are written for our admonition, upon whom the ends of the world are come; the Bible's stories teach us how to prepare for what is coming.

### Questions Answered

- **Ezek. 20:20** — Why "sabbaths" (plural)? Because God's law as a whole is part of the sign, and Israel kept additional Sabbath rests besides the weekly one.
- **Ex. 31:16, 17** — The seventh-day Sabbath is a perpetual covenant and sign forever, rooted in the six days of creation — the sign to which all the other Sabbath rests pointed.
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — On "no buy, no sell" and property: as Noah poured everything into the ark by faith, God's people invest in obedience and trust Him to guard their property; the "own nothing" new world order comes after Sunday law, not before, so prepare the land now.

## Be Ready For Their Next Move — Study 4

_Series: The Final Countdown_

### Their Next Move: The No-Buy-No-Sell

> Effective immediately, individuals found to be persistently undermining national unity and safety will face economic and civil restrictions. The enemy's plan is to sanction God's people out of the economy — and history shows it is no fantasy.

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — The beast power causes all, small and great, rich and poor, to receive a mark; and no man may buy or sell unless he has the mark, name, or number of the beast. This is their next move against God's people.
- **Rev. 17:15** — The sea from which the first beast rose represents peoples, multitudes, nations, and tongues — establishing the symbolic key for reading Revelation 13.
- **Rev. 13:11** — Another beast comes up out of the earth; the earth being the opposite of the sea, an unpopulated region — identifying the United States as the next world power in prophecy.
- **Rev. 13:14** — This power deceives the earth by miracles and says they should make an image to the beast — America makes an identity built to look exactly like the papacy of the dark ages.

### The Third Angel: The Mark Brings the Wrath of God

- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — The third angel cries with a loud voice: whoever worships the beast and his image and receives his mark shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God. Those with the mark receive the wine/wrath of God.

### The Fourth Angel: Come Out of Her

- **Rev. 18:1-4** — A mighty angel lightens the earth with glory and cries that Babylon is fallen; then a voice says, "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues." Come out of false doctrine, do not receive the mark, and do not receive the plagues.

### The Close of Probation Anchors the Timeline

- **Rev. 15:1** — Seven angels have the seven last plagues, for in them is filled up the wrath of God — proving the wine of the wrath of God is the seven last plagues.
- **Rev. 15:5** — After this the temple of the tabernacle of the testimony in heaven was opened; an opened, empty sanctuary means the ministry is finished — this is the close of probation, occurring just before the plagues fall.
- **Rev. 16:1** — A great voice out of the temple commands the seven angels to pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth, confirming probation closes immediately before the plagues begin.

### The Time of Trouble and the Deliverance of God's People

- **Dan. 12:1** — Michael stands up (leaving the most holy place, so probation is closed), beginning a time of trouble such as never was; yet at that time God's people shall be delivered.
- **Isa. 33:1, 2** — Woe to the spoiler; "O Lord, be gracious unto us... be thou their arm every morning, our salvation also in the time of trouble" — God's salvation is given in the time of trouble.
- **Isa. 33:16** — He shall dwell on high, his place of defense the munitions of rocks; "bread shall be given him; his waters shall be sure" — God's people are delivered in the mountains, where their bread and water is made sure only during the time of trouble, after probation closes.

### Probation Is Still Open — So We Have a Work to Do

> The no-buy-no-sell happens before the close of probation. If we can still "come out" and still decide whether the mark goes on hand or forehead, then probation is open, the plagues are not yet falling, and our bread and water is not sure without our own diligent input.

- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her, my people" requires a decision; the ability to decide and to choose against the mark proves probation is still open during the no-buy-no-sell period.

### Joseph and Noah: Faith That Prepares

- **Gen. 41:25** — Joseph tells Pharaoh that the dream is one: God has shown what He is about to do — seven years of plenty followed by seven years of famine. This was a prophecy of coming crisis.
- **Gen. 41:35** — They gather and lay up food in the good years as a store against the famine, so the land perished not — they did not starve because they prepared.
- **Rev. 1:1, 3** — The revelation of Jesus Christ; "blessed is he that readeth, and they that hear the words of this prophecy, and keep those things which are written therein: for the time is at hand." To keep the prophecy means to act on it — belief causes action.
- **Heb. 11:7** — By faith Noah, being warned of God of things not yet seen, moved with fear and prepared an ark to the saving of his house, condemning the world. Faith without works is dead; Noah without works would have died.
- **Matt. 24** — Jesus foretells floods and the destruction of cities, underscoring the obedience of moving to the safety of the country before the crisis.

### The Counsel to Move to the Country

> "Educate our people to get out of the cities into the country, where they can obtain a small piece of land and make a home for themselves and their children." AH 373.2

- **Gen. 41:35** — As Joseph educated the people to grow and store food for a specific coming crisis, other "Josephs" must now educate God's people how to prepare for the no-buy-no-sell. 12MR 219.4 — "I see the necessity of the people of God moving out of the cities into retired country places where they may cultivate the land and raise their own produce... I see the necessity of making haste to get all things ready for the crisis."

### Three Pillars of Preparation: Land, Water, Wood

- **Heb. 11:7** — As Noah prepared the ark over 120 years, God's people must secure land (to grow food, build, and store), water (for drinking, garden, cleaning, hydrotherapy, and fire prevention), and wood (for heating, cooking, and building) — the three pillars of a country property, prepared with haste and stamina until God opens the way.

## Surviving Without Buying or Selling: How Much Land Do You Really Need? — Study 5

### Defining the Sequence: When Is the Time of Trouble?

> "The Lord has shown me repeatedly that it is contrary to the Bible to make any provision for our temporal wants in the time of trouble." — Early Writings 56.2. The whole study turns on rightly defining _when_ the time of trouble begins, so this quote is read in its proper context.

- **Rev. 15:5** — The heavenly temple is opened, meaning Jesus has finished His mediation; this marks the close of probation, just before the plagues of Revelation 16. EW 56.2
- **Dan. 12:1** — Michael (Christ) stands up from ministering in the most holy place; His standing up closes probation and triggers the time of trouble such as never was. EW 56.3
- **Rev. 13:16** — The mark of the beast is enforced upon all, small and great, rich and poor, in the right hand or forehead; this event is still future and comes before the close of probation.
- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell except he that has the mark; refusing it means all earthly support is cut off — the "no buy, no sell."
- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — Whoever worships the beast and receives his mark drinks the wine of God's wrath; the choice is the seal of God or the mark of the beast.
- **Rev. 18:1-4** — The fourth angel lightens the earth with glory and calls "Come out of her, my people"; that probation is still open during this final call proves the no-buy-no-sell precedes the close of probation.

### Bread and Water Sure: A Promise for the Time of Trouble

> Houses and lands will be of no use to the saints in the time of trouble, for they will then have to flee before infuriated mobs. — Early Writings 56.3.

- **Isa. 33:2** — A chapter explicitly about "the time of trouble," fixing the setting after probation has closed.
- **Isa. 33:16** — In that time of trouble the faithful dwell in munitions of rocks; bread shall be given him and his waters shall be sure — the "bread and water sure" applies only while fleeing, after the plagues have begun, not during the no-buy-no-sell.
- **Isa. 33:1-16** — The full passage shows the divinely supplied bread and water belongs to the fleeing saints in the time of trouble; until that line is crossed, God expects us to raise our own provisions. EW 56.2

### Out of the Cities: A Message Long Neglected

> The out-of-the-cities call has been on the table since 1888, but Laodicea slept and the warning was lost. We are now near the end and unready.

- **Heb. 11:7** — By faith Noah, warned of things not yet seen, prepared an ark to the saving of his house and became heir of the righteousness which is by faith; he believed the prophecy and acted on it.
- **Adventist Home 373.2** — "Educate our people to get out of the cities into the country, where they can obtain a small piece of land and make a home for themselves and their children." AH 373.2
- **Manuscript Releases vol. 12, 219.4** — "I see the necessity of the people of God moving out of the cities into retired country places where they may cultivate the land and raise their own produce... making haste to get all things ready for the crisis." 12MR 219.4
- **Adventist Home 139.6** — "God will help His people to find such homes outside of the cities" — a promise that He will provide the land and the means, as He helped Noah, Joseph, and Abraham. AH 139.6

### The Three Pillars of a Country Property

> The question is not _whether_ to prepare but _how_; and the only opinions that matter come from those who have actually done the thing — survived year to year off the land.

- **Heb. 11:7** — As Noah's ark was the divinely-appointed refuge with all provisions aboard, so the country property — land, water, and wood — is the antitypical ark for which we put in all we have.
- **Dan. 1** (Daniel and his three friends ate "pulse") — A diet of lentils and plant proteins left them fairer and fatter in flesh, the model for the high-protein, plant-based eating needed to function and grow strong during the no-buy-no-sell.

### Trusting God While Doing All in Our Power

> God warns ahead so we need not panic; faith acts first, then God opens the way.

- **PP 290.1** — At the Red Sea the people advanced into the water by faith before God divided it; "they did all that was in their power to do, and then the mighty One of Israel divided the sea." If we do everything in our power, He will make the way to the promised land. PP 290.1

### The Stages Leading to No Buy, No Sell

> Judgment begins at the house of God: those who know better are held accountable first, and at every stage of pressure people drop off to one side or the other.

- **1 Pet. 4:17** — Judgment begins at the house of God; God's people who know the truth are tested first, choosing the seal of God or the mark of the beast at each step.
- **Testimonies vol. 9, 97** — "The time of God's destructive judgments is the time of mercy for those who have had no opportunity to learn what is truth... His hand is still stretched out to save, while the door is closed to those who would not enter." 9T 97.2
- **Rev. 7** — The 144,000 receive the seal of God and proclaim the loud cry; through their message the great multitude comes out of every nation, refuses the mark, and joins the antitypical ark.

### Time of Trouble vs. The Tribulation

> "Pray ye that your flight be not in the winter... for then shall be great tribulation, such as was not since the beginning of the world." The "great tribulation" of Matthew 24 is not the end-time time of trouble.

- **Matt. 24:20-22** — The "great tribulation, such as was not," cut short "for the elect's sake," refers to the 1260-year papal persecution (538-1798), not the future time of trouble.
- **Matt. 24:29** — "Immediately after the tribulation of those days shall the sun be darkened" — the heavenly signs follow the shortened persecution (persecution easing by 1755), confirming the tribulation is past, anchored by the 1798 end of papal rule.

> Historical markers built on Dan. 2, 7, 8, 9: 538 to 1798 = the 1260 years of papal supremacy; the persecution shortened so that "no flesh" should perish, easing about 1755; the Lisbon earthquake of 1755 fulfilled the darkening, followed by the falling of the stars in 1833 — signs of Christ's coming after the tribulation.

### How Long? No Time Prophecy Remains

> Some ask whether the no-buy-no-sell lasts three and a half years. Scripture forbids fixing any such period.

- **Rev. 10:6** — The angel swears that "there should be time no longer" until probation closes; since the Sunday law and no-buy-no-sell fall inside probationary time, there is no remaining day-for-a-year nor any literal time prophecy to measure it.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — "I have appointed thee each day for a year" — the day-for-a-year principle, which no longer applies to events after probation's close, so we cannot calculate the duration of the no-buy-no-sell.

> "All we know is that we will have to raise our own provisions for the time of trouble... the time of buying and selling will be very serious." Because growing food takes months and years, the no-buy-no-sell must last long enough to prove obedience and weed out all who would take the mark — likely years, not weeks.

### Proving Obedience: Selling All to Follow

> Christ went into the wilderness and the mountains every day; to follow Him we must be where we can do likewise.

- **Mark 10:21, 22** (the rich young ruler) — Told to sell all he had and follow, he went away sad; the test now is whether we will sell all and invest in the antitypical country home to follow God.
- **Dan. 12:1** — "At that time thy people shall be delivered" — God's people make it through; their bread and water are sure in the time of trouble, refuting the idea of mass martyrdom and proving the call is to prepare, not to die in the city.

### No Death Decree Until After Probation Closes

> The death decree does not fall during the Sunday law or no-buy-no-sell; it comes only after the close of probation, and even then the faithful do not die.

- **Rev. 13:17** — During the no-buy-no-sell the pressure is economic, not lethal; one either starves, takes the mark, or has obeyed God and prepared — there is no death decree yet.
- **Dan. 12:1** — When the death decree finally goes forth after probation closes, "thy people shall be delivered" — no faithful one dies from that point onward, as Israel was not lost in the Red Sea.

### A Total Economic Boycott — Not a Currency Problem

> The no-buy-no-sell is not about which money is accepted; it is a complete cutting-off from all commerce, like the boycotts of old.

- **Testimonies vol. 5, 152.2** — "The time is coming when we cannot sell at any price. The decree will soon go forth prohibiting men to buy or sell of any man save him that hath the mark of the beast." No bartering, no alternate currency, no Bitcoin will avail — it is a total economic boycott. 5T 152.2

### One-Year Duration of the Time of Trouble

> The no-buy-no-sell has no fixed length, but the time of trouble after probation closes is measured in Scripture.

- **Rev. 10:6** — "Time no longer" applies only until probation closes; afterward the plagues fall in a defined period.
- **Rev. 18:8** — "Therefore shall her plagues come in one day" — the plagues are dated to "one day."
- **Ezek. 4:6** — Applying the day-for-a-year measure to the plagues' "one day" yields a time of trouble that lasts one year.

## The Man Who Survived the End of the World — Study 6

_Series: The Final Countdown_

### Noah's Story Is a Warning, Not a Passive Miracle

> Noah was trapped at sea for 370 days — but was that just a miracle, or the fruit of 120 years of warning and preparation? Jesus said we must learn the lessons of Noah's day for the end of the world.

- **Ps. 91:8** — Only with thine eyes shalt thou behold and see the reward of the wicked; God's protection is real, but it does not mean we stand in the firing line doing nothing.
- **Heb. 11:7** — By faith Noah, warned of God of things not yet seen, moved with fear, prepared an ark to the saving of his house, and became heir of the righteousness which is by faith.
- **Matt. 24:37-39** — As the days of Noah were, so shall the coming of the Son of man be; the world ate, drank, and married, and knew not until the flood took them all away.

### How Long Noah Was in the Ark

- **Gen. 7:11, 12** — In the 600th year of Noah's life, the second month, the seventeenth day, the fountains of the deep broke up and rain fell forty days and forty nights.
- **Gen. 8:14-17** — In the second month, on the twenty-seventh day, the earth was dried and God told Noah to go forth from the ark; from the 17th to the 27th of a 360-day year is 370 days in the ark.

### God's Specific Commands to Build the Ark

> This was not happenstance. Noah was given a prophecy that in 120 years a flood would come, and exact directions for how to prepare.

- **Gen. 6:14** — Make thee an ark of gopher wood, with rooms, pitched within and without; God commanded the best quality, built to last, not a shoddy short-term job.
- **Gen. 6:15** — The length 300 cubits, breadth 50, height 30; the size was God's command, large enough to house roughly 1,400 kinds of land animals.
- **Gen. 6:18-22** — God established His covenant, commanded two of every sort and all manner of food gathered, and Noah did according to all that God commanded him.

### The Animals Represent Kinds of People

- **Acts 10:11, 12, 28** — Peter's vision of the sheet of four-footed beasts and fowls taught that God calls no man common or unclean; the creatures of the ark typify men of all nations.
- **Rev. 7:1-3** — Four angels hold the four winds while the sealing angel cries, "Hurt not the earth till we have sealed the servants of our God in their foreheads."
- **Rev. 7:4** — There were sealed 144,000 of all the tribes of Israel — the people who were supposed to know prophecy and carry the message.
- **Rev. 7:9** — After this, a great multitude which no man could number, of all nations and kindreds and peoples and tongues — the "all kinds" of animals that respond to the message.
- **Rev. 14** — The 144,000 carry the three angels' messages, the special end-of-the-world warning to be given with a loud cry.
- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins and receive not of her plagues"; the warning to escape the judgments, as the flood was a judgment.

### The Gentiles Come to the Light

- **Isa. 60:1-3** — Arise, shine; though darkness covers the earth, the Lord's glory rises upon thee and the Gentiles come to thy light, and kings to the brightness of thy rising.
- **Isa. 60:4, 5** — All gather and come to thee; the abundance of the sea shall be converted unto thee, the forces of the Gentiles shall come — the great multitude responds though Israel struggles.
- **Isa. 60:6, 7** — The multitude of camels and dromedaries of Midian, all from Sheba, the flocks of Kedar — the children of the east, even the Muslim peoples, gathered in as prophecy is fulfilled.
- **Rev. 17:15** — The waters and sea represent peoples and multitudes and nations and tongues; the "abundance of the sea" converted is a great multitude of people.

### A Working Faith — Preach and Prepare Together

> _"He directed Noah to build an ark and at the same time preach to the inhabitants of the world that God would bring the flood waters upon the earth."_ — Signs of the Times, December 20, 1877. Every blow of the hammer was a sermon. _"Noah's faith was a working faith... that led him to act in accordance with the word of God."_ — ST April 18, 1895.

- **1 Cor. 10:11** — All these things happened unto them for examples, written for our admonition, upon whom the ends of the world are come; Noah's story is for you and me.

> _"The whole surface of the earth was changed at the flood... yet Noah preserved seed and took it with him in the ark."_ — Spiritual Gifts vol. 3, p. 76.2. Noah invested all he possessed in the ark. _"All that he possessed he invested in the ark."_ — From Eternity Past, p. 53.

### Our Antitypical Ark: The Country Property

> The flood prefigures the no-buy, no-sell crisis. We must perceive, prepare, and preach — just as Noah did for 120 years — building a self-sufficient ark before the storm. _"Again and again the Lord has instructed that our people are to take their families away from the cities into the country where they can raise their own provisions, for in the future the problem of buying and selling will be a very serious one."_ — Country Living, p. 9. _"The time is not far distant when we shall be forced to seek refuge in desolate and solitary places."_ — Country Living (last page).

- **Rev. 13:17** — No man might buy or sell save he that had the mark; no mark means no buy, no sell — the final test God's people will face, requiring preparation, not just a quiet flight.
- **Rev. 14:9** — The third angel cries with a loud voice that whoever worships the beast and receives his mark shall drink the wine of the wrath of God; we escape by preparing and heeding the warning.

### The End-Time Timeline: From Judgment to Deliverance

> 1844 began the message of judgment; 1888 was the first call out of the cities. 137 years later we should have been building, planting, and planning, but we are not. The same flow of events applies: warning, preparation, final warning, then the door shut.

- **Rev. 18:4** — The final loud cry — "Come out of her" — calls the great multitude into the ark before the close of probation and the seven last plagues.
- **Rev. 17:12, 13** — The ten horns are ten kings (regions of the new world order) which receive power as kings one hour with the beast and give their strength unto the beast.
- **Rev. 18:8** — Her plagues come in one day — a year by the prophetic day-for-a-year principle.
- **Rev. 18:10** — In one hour is thy judgment come; the new world order's one hour follows after Babylon's judgment.
- **Rev. 17:16** — The ten horns shall hate the whore, make her desolate and naked, eat her flesh, and burn her with fire — the judgment that comes at the very end.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — Each day appointed for a year — the prophetic key by which one hour equals fifteen days and one day equals a year.

### Fleeing to the Mountains When the Death Decree Comes

> After the close of probation and through the plagues, God's people remain on their self-sufficient property — the ark — until the new world order enforces the death decree. Only then do they flee into the mountains, trusting wholly on God.

- **Dan. 12:1** — Michael stands up and a time of trouble commences, such as never was; the death decree belongs to this final stage.
- **Isa. 33:2** — O Lord, be gracious unto us; be thou their arm every morning, our salvation also in the time of trouble.
- **Isa. 33:16** — He shall dwell on high; his place of defense shall be the munitions of rocks; bread shall be given him, his waters shall be sure — God feeds the fleeing saints in the wilderness.

### Two Classes, One Choice

> _"Many at first appeared to receive the warning, yet they did not turn to God with true repentance... and were soon among the most defiant scoffers."_ — From Eternity Past, pp. 53, 54. _"In that time before the flood, as in this, there were two classes, the righteous and the wicked... God gave direction that an ark was to be built for those who desired to be saved from the coming destruction."_ — Christ Triumphant, p. 56. There is no third group — either the seal of God or the mark of the beast; we show we believe God by preparing, and in building the ark the character itself is transformed. _"Agriculture is the A B C of education."_ — Health Food Ministry, p. 39.

---

## The Secret to Skipping Death Revealed — Study 7

### Enoch Walked With God

> The secret to skipping death and surviving the end-time events is hidden in one of the Bible's shortest stories. Only two men have ever escaped death; a final living group will do the same.

- **Gen. 5:21-24** — Enoch lived 365 years, walked with God, "and he was not; for God took him" — the first man translated to heaven without seeing death. The first key: to be translated alive, you must walk with God.
- **Amos 3:3** — "Can two walk together, except they be agreed?" To walk with God, Enoch had to be in agreement with God; we likewise must agree with His will and ways.
- **2 Thess. 2:10** — Those who perish do so because they "received not the love of the truth" — walking with God requires a love of the truth itself, not of what is popular or what family, friends, or church say.

### Translated By Faith — That He Should Not See Death

- **Heb. 11:5** — "By faith Enoch was translated that he should not see death... he had this testimony, that he pleased God." The second key: we must please God to be translated. EP 49.4
- **Rev. 14:12** — "Here are they that keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus." The same faith Enoch had is the faith of the final group, the 144,000.
- **EP 49.4 (From Eternity Past)** — Enoch's godly character "represents the state of holiness which must be attained by those who shall be 'redeemed from the earth' (Revelation 14:3) at Christ's second advent. Then, as before the Flood, iniquity will prevail."

### Enoch and the 144,000 — The Same Experience

> Enoch was taken alive; the 144,000 are redeemed alive. Both are Adventists (waiting for the advent), both live in the country, both preach judgment, both warn of the second coming.

- **Rev. 7:4** — The sealed number is 144,000 of all the tribes of the children of Israel — the special end-time group compared to Enoch.
- **Rev. 14:3** — The 144,000 "were redeemed from the earth" and sing a new song no man else could learn; like Enoch, they are taken alive.
- **EP 49.4 (From Eternity Past)** — "Like Enoch, God's people will seek for purity of heart... Like Enoch they will warn the world of the Lord's second coming and by their holy example will condemn the sins of the ungodly. As Enoch was translated to heaven, so the living righteous will be translated from the earth before its destruction by fire." See 1 Cor. 15:51, 52; 1 Thess. 4:16-18.

### Enoch the Prophet — A Judgment Message

> Just your holy example becomes a living rebuke; the ungodly feel judged even when you say nothing.

- **Jude 1:14-16** — Enoch, "the seventh from Adam," prophesied of the Lord coming "with ten thousands of his saints, to execute judgment upon all" — proof he understood the final judgment of the ungodly.
- **Jude 1:16** — The ungodly are "murmurers, complainers, walking after their own lusts... having men's persons in admiration because of advantage" — gaining advantage for selfish gain, the same spirit seen at the end.
- **CET 238.1 (Christian Experience and Teachings)** — "The prophetic gift is not limited to any one age... Enoch, the seventh from Adam, was a prophet. Looking down through the centuries, he saw with prophetic vision the coming of the Lord, and the execution of the final judgments upon the ungodly."

### The Three Angels' Messages — The Judgment Begun

- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — The first angel's message: "Fear God, and give glory to him; for the hour of his judgment is come" — the announcement that the judgment has begun.
- **Rev. 14:8** — The second angel: "Babylon is fallen."
- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — The third angel warns that whoever worships the beast and receives his mark "shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God" — the judgments fall on the wicked once the judgment process ends.

### The Fourth Angel — Light Lightening the Earth

> Light is truth, but specifically the true understanding of last-day prophecy. The lamp gives light only when filled with oil — the Holy Spirit, who leads into all truth and shows things to come.

- **Rev. 18:1-3** — Another angel comes down with great power and "the earth was lightened with his glory," crying "Babylon the great is fallen"; the merchants of the earth waxed rich through the abundance of her delicacies — the fourth angel's message, the loud cry.
- **John 16:13** — The Holy Spirit (the oil in the lamp) "will guide you into all truth... and he will shew you things to come" — a true understanding of prophecy.
- **LS 411.5 (Life Sketches)** — "Now comes the word that... New York is to be swept away by a tidal wave. This I have never said... when the Lord shall arise to shake terribly the earth, then the words of Revelation 18:1-3 will be fulfilled" — fulfilled when the great buildings fall (the Twin Towers, Sept. 11, 2001).

### The Day of Great Slaughter — When the Towers Fall

- **Isa. 30:23-25** — In the day the latter rain falls, "in the day of the great slaughter, when the towers fall," there shall be rivers and streams of waters upon every high hill — the towers falling marks the day of great slaughter.
- **Isa. 27:7** — Speaks of the slaughter, "the slaughter of them that are slain"; verse 8, "when it shooteth forth thou wilt debate with it" — a great debate on truth precedes the day of slaughter; the defenced cities shall be desolate.
- **Ezek. 9:1-6** — At the cry "with a loud voice," six men with slaughter weapons come, but the man with the writer's inkhorn first sets a mark on the foreheads of those that sigh and cry; begin at the sanctuary — God's sealing of His people before the day of slaughter, beginning with His own people.

### The Time of Trouble — God's People on Earth

> It is a deception that God's people will be taken away before the time of trouble; they will be here, sustained in the mountains.

- **Dan. 12:1** — The "time of trouble, such as never was" — God's people are on the earth during this time, not removed before it.
- **Isa. 33:1, 2** — A time of woe; "be thou their arm every morning, our salvation also in the time of trouble" — Isaiah 33 names both the time of woe and the time of trouble.
- **Isa. 33:16** — "He shall dwell on high... bread shall be given him; his waters shall be sure" — in the time of trouble God's people are in the mountains and their bread and water are sure; before that time, our bread and water are not sure without our own effort.
- **Rev. 13:17** — The "no buy, no sell" — none can buy or sell food, water, power, clothing, or any necessity unless they worship as the beast dictates; this triggers the close of probation and the seven last plagues.

### Come Out of Her — The Loud Cry

- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues" — the fourth angel's message repeating the second and third: do not take the mark or receive the wine of the wrath of God.
- **Rev. 18:3** — "The merchants of the earth are waxed rich through the abundance of her delicacies" — connected to the merchants who met at the World Trade Center.

### Watching and Working — Obedience Like Enoch and Noah

> When men of God saw prophecy of future events, they took action. Be seen working — stand out like Noah building his ark, so the world asks "What is that?"

- **ChS 96.1 (Christian Service)** — "Those who are watching for the Lord, are purifying their souls by obedience to the truth. With vigilant watching they combine earnest working... They are declaring the truth that is now specially applicable. As Enoch, Noah, Abraham, and Moses each declared the truth for his time, so will Christ's servants now give the special warning for their generation."
- **COL 332.1 (Christ's Object Lessons)** — "Be ambitious, for the Master's glory, to cultivate every grace of character... This you may do; for Enoch pleased Him though living in a degenerate age. And there are Enochs in this our day."
- **CCh 312.4 (Counsels for the Church)** — Christ's followers "are to be channels of light... Enoch did not become polluted with the iniquities existing in his day; why need we in our day?"

### Leave the Cities — Country Living

> Country living is not just about saving our skin; it is a character development that cannot take place in the city.

- **Rev. 13:17** — Because of the coming "no buy, no sell," God's commandment-keeping people must prepare now to grow their own food and be self-sufficient. CL 30.4
- **CL 30.4 (Country Living)** — "As God's commandment-keeping people, we must leave the cities. As did Enoch, we must work in the cities but not dwell in them."
- **CL 13.2 (Country Living)** — Schools in the city wait to "attract and demoralize" children, and "the work of character building is tenfold harder" there — a tenfold easier advantage is found in the country.
- **CL 30.2 (Country Living)** — "The cities are to be worked from outposts... 'Shall not the cities be warned? Yes; not by God's people living in them, but by their visiting them, to warn them of what is coming upon the earth.'"
- **CL 30.5 (Country Living)** — As the voice of Lot was heard in Sodom, "yet Lot could have preserved his family from many evils, had he not made his home in this wicked, polluted city... Enoch walked with God, and yet he did not live in the midst of any city, polluted with every kind of violence and wickedness, as did Lot in Sodom."

### Follow the Lamb Whithersoever He Goeth

- **Rev. 14:1** — The Lamb stands on Mount Zion with the 144,000 "having his Father's name written in their foreheads" — His ownership, His seal, His character embodied in them.
- **Rev. 14:4** — They "were not defiled with women" (not polluted by false doctrine or a false church), "they are virgins... these are they which follow the Lamb whithersoever he goeth" — if Jesus went to the country to commune with God, His followers go where He goes.
- **Rev. 14:5** — "In their mouth was found no guile" — they do not speak like the world in any way; they are visibly, recognizably different.

### Q&A — The Seal of God vs. the Mark of the Beast

- **Rev. 14:12** — The saints "keep the commandments of God, and the faith of Jesus"; the seal of God is found in the fourth commandment — the seventh-day Sabbath.
- **Rev. 13** — The beast (the papacy) seeks to change "times and laws"; the only time and law changed is the Sabbath to Sunday, which is the mark of the beast. A progressive Sunday law leads to no buying or selling for those who refuse to worship on Sunday.

### Q&A — The Light of 1844 and the Midnight Cry

> In 1844, from April through August, Samuel S. Snow gave the "True Midnight Cry" with five points proving the advent message.

- **Rev. 14** — The hour-of-judgment message (October 22, 1844) is an intricate part of the first angel's message; the True Midnight Cry (TRMC) explains how the date is reached. EW (Early Writings) — the light shining on the path is the midnight cry, and those who denied it fell off the path.

### Q&A — Laodicea and the Year 2001

- **Rev. 3:14-17** — Laodicea is "wretched, and miserable, and poor, and blind, and naked" — yet thinks she is rich and in need of nothing; she had cast away the truths but thinks she still has them all.
- **EW (Miller's Dream)** — The casket of jewels (truths) is torn in pieces and the gems scattered; a man with a dirt brush gathers them and places them in a larger chest, brighter than before — the experience of Laodicea, whose truths began to be restored in 2001.

### Q&A — The Investigative Judgment and Standing Alive

- **Matt. 14:30** — Peter began to sink when he took his eyes off Christ; those alive when the judgment closes stand not by their own strength but by holding so tightly to Christ that nothing else absorbs the mind.

### Q&A — Destruction of the Cities

- **Joel (whole book)** — Describes the destruction coming upon the wicked cities. The seven last plagues are the effects of wars — destruction by wars, the third world war, like nothing the planet has ever seen.
- **CL 7.3, 7.4 (Country Living)** — "The ungodly cities of our world are to be swept away by the besom of destruction... the cities full of transgression... will be destroyed by earthquakes, by fire, by flood. All the world will be warned."
- **GC 589.3 (The Great Controversy)** — Satan, "while appearing... as a great physician who can heal all their maladies... will bring disease and disaster, until populous cities are reduced to ruin and desolation," working in accidents, conflagrations, tornadoes, hailstorms, floods, cyclones, tidal waves, and earthquakes.

### Q&A — The Two Groups Saved Alive (Typology of Noah's Ark)

- **2 Kings 2:11** — Elijah, like Enoch, was taken to heaven without seeing death; the two who escaped death typify the two end-time groups who will not die.
- **Gen. 7 (Noah's Ark)** — Noah and his family represent the 144,000; the animals that entered the ark represent the great multitude — two groups saved alive.

### Q&A — The Latter Rain and the Sealing

- **Deut. 32:2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain" — the latter rain is the true doctrine; God's people cannot be sealed in error, so the latter rain of true last-day prophecy began falling after 2001 to seal them into truth.

### Q&A — Satan's Appearing and the Death Decree

- **Satan appears as Christ (whiteboard)** — Near the close of probation, after Sunday law, Satan will appear as Christ, working with the powers of earth toward a universal Sunday law; his end goal is to enforce the death decree against God's people, who will flee to the mountains for 15 days but none will die.

### Q&A — Time No Longer; the Day of Atonement

- **Rev. 10:6** — "There should be time no longer" — after October 22, 1844, prophetic time ceased; we are now in the antitypical Day of Atonement, one prophetic day, so the length of the no-buy-no-sell period cannot be calculated.

### Q&A — The Omega Apostasy: Spiritualizing Prophecy

> The alpha apostasy (Kellogg's "Living Temple") spiritualized Scripture; the omega does the same to literal prophecy — Daniel 11, Revelation 9, 16, 17, and 13 are now being spiritualized away.

- **GC 607.1 (The Great Controversy)** — As the movement for Sunday enforcement grows bold, "the law will be invoked against commandment keepers; they will be threatened with fines and imprisonment, and some will be offered positions of influence, and other rewards and advantages" — the same advantages Enoch warned of (Jude 16). The five stages of the Sunday law: fines, imprisonment, inducements, no buy/no sell, and finally the death decree.

### Q&A — The 15 Days of Babylon's Fall

- **Rev. 18:10** — "In one hour is thy judgment come" — using the day-for-a-year principle (Ezek. 4:6), one hour is a twenty-fourth of a prophetic day (a year); 360 days divided by 24 is roughly 15 literal days.

### Q&A — The Seven Trumpets and the Seven Last Plagues

> The ten plagues of Egypt and the seven last plagues happen for the same reason; some Egyptian plagues struck God's people, but none of the last plagues touch God's people.

- **Ex. 10:13, 19** — The plague of locusts was brought by an east wind and removed by a west wind; since wind equals wars, this is typology of an eastern war and a western war connected to the woe and the locust beast.
- **Rev. 9:20, 21** — The seven trumpet plagues are the "first plagues" — local in scope.
- **Rev. 16** — The seven last plagues are identical in order to the trumpet plagues, but global — which is why they are called the "last" plagues, implying seven "first" plagues precede them.

### Q&A — Prophecy Revives the Dry Bones

- **Ezek. 37** — Israel's dry bones, stripped of skin, are revived only when Ezekiel prophesies to them; prophecy is what wakes God's people.
- **Micah 3** — The false teachers of prophecy "tear the skin off" the bones and remove the people's life — which is why God's people lack the passion for the truth and for getting ready for the last-day events.
