## Fear What's Coming? — Study 8

_The Final Countdown — End-Time Events Timeline live study series, with MacKenzie Drebit (Amazing Discoveries)._

### Fear Comes From the Unknown — God Gives a Sound Mind

> The fear of the final events comes because people are in the dark and don't know what's coming. God's answer is to shift us from the unknown to the known — to understand the time so we are not caught unaware like a thief in the night.

- **2 Tim. 1:7** — God has not given us the spirit of fear, but of power, love, and a sound mind; that sound mind is the frontal lobe, the seat of reason where we receive the seal of God or the mark of the beast. Fear puts us in a reactive, panicked state; faith and a sound mind keep us steady when everything collapses.

### The Events Connected With the Close of Probation Are Clearly Presented

> "The events connected with the close of probation and the work of preparation for the time of trouble are clearly presented." Most have no more understanding of these truths than if they had never been revealed.

- **Rev. 3:15** — Christ wishes we were cold or hot, not lukewarm; the cold person realizes how spiritually poor he is and is driven to change, while the lukewarm is complacent and feels no need.
- **Rev. 3:17** — Laodicea thinks she is rich and in need of nothing, not knowing she is wretched, miserable, poor, blind, and naked — which is exactly why she has no idea what events lead to the close of probation.
- _EGW: "The events connected with the close of probation… are clearly presented. But multitudes have no more understanding of these important truths than if they had never been revealed. Satan watches to catch away every impression that would make them wise unto salvation, and the time of trouble will find them unready." Mar 160._

### Nothing to Fear But Forgetting How God Led Us

- _EGW: "We have nothing to fear for the future, except as we shall forget the way the Lord has led us, and His teaching in our past history." CET 204.1 (Christian Experience and Teachings 204.1)._ We must not despise the old paths and our pioneer history.
- _EGW: "But if men refuse to search for the truth as for hid treasure, if the mind is pleased with the theories of error, the soul will remain in darkness… Error never sanctifies. It can do no good. And how full of darkness is the soul that receives error as truth." Ms 4, 1898._ Only truth sanctifies; a false understanding of the events produces a false growth of character.

### The Revelation Is a Book We Need to Study

> "As we near the close of this world's history, the prophecies relating to the last days especially demand our study." The last book of the New Testament is full of truth we need to understand.

- **Rev. 1:1** — The revelation of Jesus Christ, which God gave to show His servants things which must shortly come to pass — prophecy is from Jesus, the road map of where the pitfalls lie.
- **Rev. 1:2** — John bore record of the word of God and the testimony of Jesus Christ and all things that he saw.
- **Rev. 1:3** — Blessed is he that readeth and they that keep the things written; prophecy is not merely intellectual but practical — it demands action and changes the character.
- _EGW: "As we near the close of this world's history, the prophecies relating to the last days especially demand our study… But Christ through His servant John has here declared what shall be in the last days." COL 133.2._

### The Prudent Foresee and Prepare; the Sluggard Sleeps

> A prudent man foresees the evil and hides himself. Fear comes from not preparing; by preparing we become ready.

- **Prov. 22:3** — A prudent man foreseeth the evil and hideth himself, but the simple pass on and are punished; the prudent makes provision while the simple presume.
- **Prov. 6:6-8** — Go to the ant, thou sluggard; she provideth her meat in the summer and gathereth her food in the harvest — we must store up for the winter of final events while it is still the preparation time.
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — All these things happened unto them for examples and are written for our admonition, upon whom the ends of the world are come; the ant's harvest applies directly to us.
- **Prov. 6:9-11** — How long wilt thou sleep, O sluggard? Poverty comes as an armed robber upon the unprepared — if you don't prepare for the winter, you will starve.
- **Prov. 18:15** — The heart of the prudent getteth knowledge and the ear of the wise seeketh knowledge; intellectual fear is removed by acquiring the knowledge we need.

### Three Areas of Preparation: Spiritual, Mental, Physical

> Spiritual fear is alleviated by obedience; mental fear by acquiring knowledge; and both are sealed by physical, practical preparation.

- **Rom. 8:28** — All things work together for good to them that love God; whatever happens, ask what God wants you to learn and how to grow, for past experiences prepare us for what is coming.
- **Phil. 4:7** — The peace of God which passeth all understanding shall keep your hearts and minds; this peace comes because God tells us ahead of time and nothing surprises us.
- **John 14:27, 29** — Peace I leave with you; let not your heart be troubled, neither let it be afraid; "now I have told you before it come to pass" — God tells us beforehand precisely so we need not fear.

### A Calm Walk With Jesus Through Conflict

- _EGW: "Christ calls us to walk with him in this world in the path of humble, trustful obedience, which will secure a pure, holy, happy life. Which will we choose — liberty in Christ, or bondage and tyranny in the service of Satan?" RH Aug. 2, 1881, par. 7._
- _EGW: "We need not be alarmed if this path of liberty is laid through conflicts and sufferings… The peace which passeth knowledge will cost us battles with the powers of darkness, struggles severe against selfishness and inward sins." RH Aug. 2, 1881, par. 8._ Peace is won by fighting the good fight.

### Perfect Love Casts Out Fear

> When love for souls and for Christ propels us, fear is no longer even an option.

- **1 John 4:18** — There is no fear in love, but perfect love casteth out fear, because fear hath torment; he that feareth is not made perfect in love.
- **1 John 4:17** — Herein is our love made perfect, that we may have boldness in the day of judgment; we can be bold in the time of trouble when our love is perfected and our fear cast out.

### Noah's Obedience — The Pattern for Final Preparation

> God said, "Build a boat" — not "I will make you float." He says, "Prepare for the final events and I will protect you in your obedience," not in a magical bubble of disobedience. Noah put all that he had into the ark.

- _EGW: "The 'time of trouble such as never was' is soon to open upon us; and we shall need an experience which we do not now possess, and which many are too indolent to obtain… In that time of trial, every soul must stand for himself before God. Though Noah, Daniel, and Job were in the land… they shall but deliver their own souls by their righteousness." GC 622.4 (Ezek. 14:20)._ That righteousness was obedience.

### The Sequence: No Buy, No Sell to the Time of Trouble

> On the whiteboard timeline: independent stages of fines, imprisonment, and inducements (bribery) lead into the full no-buy-no-sell period, then the close of probation, then the seven last plagues — the time of trouble. We are here, in the preparation time.

- _EGW: The out-of-the-cities / country-living message began in 1888, and many have lived and died since without preparing; we are mercifully still in the preparation time and must get out of the cities before the destruction of the cities. (Country Living principle.)_

### The Sealing and Judgment of the Living

> Revelation 18:1-3 began the sealing / judgment of the living, identifying the 144,000 who give the loud cry. The sealing is "a settling into the truth, both intellectually and spiritually" — which is why understanding the true final events is part of being sealed.

- **Rev. 18:1-3** — The beginning point of the sealing and the judgment of the living.
- **Rev. 7:1, 3** — Four angels hold the four winds until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads; only when the sealing is complete do the four winds blow.
- **Rev. 14** — The 144,000 and their message connected to the sealing.
- **Isa. 30:22-25** — Among the passages confirming the sealing and judgment of the living.
- **Ezek. 37** — The four winds, connected to the four winds of Revelation 7.
- **Jer. 4:1-13** — Read with the four-winds passages as part of the sealing sequence.
- _EGW: "Just as soon as the people of God are sealed in their foreheads — it is not any seal or mark that can be seen, but a settling into the truth, both intellectually and spiritually, so they cannot be moved — just as soon as God's people are sealed and prepared for the shaking, it will come. Indeed, it has begun already." 4BC 1161 (LDE 219)._
- _EGW: "Everyone is to have sufficient light to make his decision intelligently." GC 605 (LDE 217.4)._

### The Image of the Beast — the Great Test Before Probation Closes

- _EGW: "The Lord has shown me clearly that the image of the beast will be formed before probation closes; for it is to be the great test for the people of God, by which their eternal destiny will be decided." 2SM 81 (LDE 227.3); the great test is shown to be Sunday-law enforcement._
- **Rev. 13:11-17** — The two-horned beast makes an image to the beast portrayed in Revelation 13:1-10; to learn what the image is, study the characteristics of the beast itself — the papacy.
- **Rev. 13:17** — The "no buy, no sell" that captivated the speaker's study for seventeen years; the mark-of-the-beast test must take place to complete the sealing process.

### Events That Lead to the Close of Probation

> These events are the keys that tell us when probation closes and the door is shut — and yes, we will know.

- **Rev. 15, 16** — The chapters describing when probation's time is closed and the plagues poured out.
- **Rev. 14:9-12** — The third angel's warning against the mark, marking that time of decision.
- **Rev. 18** — Babylon's fall and the call "come out of her, my people," sounding at the close.
- **Dan. 11:45** — Comes to his end with none to help him, connected to the close-of-probation crisis.
- **Dan. 12:1** — Michael stands up; probation closes and the great time of trouble such as never was begins — interconnected with Daniel 11:45.

### Time No Longer — Why No Time Can Be Calculated Now

> We make no time predictions from Noah or Esther; we are in the day of atonement, prophetically still "October 22, 1844," until probation closes. The Bible gives no time prophecy between 1844 and the close of probation.

- **Rev. 10:6** — Time no longer — meaning prophetic time ended at the great disappointment of October 22, 1844; once probation closes, time resumes and the plagues fall in measured periods (one day, one hour).

### The Pioneer Charts — What Was Clearly Presented

> The 1843 chart was voted by the conference (1842) to lay out the prophecies of Daniel and Revelation; the 1850 "Ellen White chart" was made by vision (with Otis Nichols) and shows the image of the beast, the ten kings of Revelation 17, and the seven last plagues as the third woe. This is the light from behind — the true midnight cry.

- **Dan. 2** — The pioneers fully grasped the prophetic line of kingdoms underlying the charts.
- **Rev. 11** — Featured on the 1850 chart in connection with the final events.
- **Rev. 17** — The ten kings who give their power to the beast, shown on the chart in connection with the image of the beast.

### Q&A — Two Times of Trouble, Jacob's Trouble, and the Death Decree

> "Little" and "big" time of trouble denote intensity, not length; the intensity rises all the way to the second coming.

- **Dan. 12:1** — The second, full time of trouble; its first phase is the plagues falling (trouble for the wicked) and its second phase is Jacob's trouble (trouble for God's people).
- **Ezek. 4:6** — A day for a year; an hour is a twenty-fourth of a day, so the "one hour" of judgment equals fifteen days of prophetic time — the span of Jacob's trouble.
- _EGW: "Jacob and Esau represent two classes… Jacob's distress when he learned that Esau was marching against him with four hundred men represents the trouble of the righteous as the decree goes forth to put them to death." 3SG 131.1._
- _EGW: "Soon we heard the voice of God like many waters, which gave us the day and hour of Jesus' coming. The living saints, 144,000 in number, knew and understood the voice…" LS 65.1._
- _EGW: "Soon I heard the voice of God, which shook the heavens and the earth… their captivity was turned." EW 272.3._ The death decree and Jacob's trouble fall at the very end, the final hour of trial.

### Practical First Steps Out of the Cities

> Don't say it's too late — twenty years of "too late" has already passed. Pray, move toward the country, learn skills, and let the truth do the convicting. The ark is your prepared country property.

- **Rev. 7** — The 144,000 sealed and prepared; like Noah we ready our "ark" — country property and self-sufficiency — then sound the loud cry, "come into the ark." (Farm apprenticeship: farm.amazingdiscoveries.org; How to Country YouTube channel.)

## How to Survive What's Coming — Study 9

_Series: The Final Countdown_

### The Wrath of God Upon the Cities

> We are living amid the perils of the last days. The wrath of God is preparing to come upon all the cities — not all at once but one after another. — _17LtMs, Ms 233, 1902, par. 4_

- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — In the third angel's message, whoever worships the beast and receives his mark drinks the wine of the wrath of God poured out without mixture.
- **Rev. 16** — The seven angels are told to pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth; this is the chapter of the seven last plagues, poured without mercy, unlike the ten plagues of Egypt which were still mixed with mercy.
- **Dan. 12:1** — The time of trouble such as never was; it does not happen overnight but builds through a succession of events.

### The Destruction of the Cities Is a Warning

- **Matt. 24** — Jesus told of the destruction coming on Jerusalem; the signs were not a call to do nothing but a command to take decided action and flee the city.
- **AH 146.3** — "So long as God gives me power... I shall continue to call upon parents to leave the cities and get homes in the country, where they can cultivate the soil." The terrible calamities recently visited on the land show the judgments of the Lord are falling, a warning to prepare for what is coming.
- **Zeph. 1:14-16** — The great day of the Lord is near, a day of wrath, trouble, distress, wasteness, desolation, darkness and gloom; a day of the trumpet and alarm against the fenced cities and high towers.
- **Rev. 18:8** — Her plagues come in one day; in prophecy a day is a year, so the plagues come within one prophetic year.
- **Ezek. 4:6; Num. 14:34** — The prophetic principle that a day represents a year.

### Sodom: An Ensample to Those Who Should Live Ungodly

- **2 Pet. 2:6** — God turned Sodom and Gomorrah into ashes, making them an example to those who afterward should live ungodly — meaning other cities at the end will likewise be turned to ashes.
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — All these things happened to them for examples and were written for our admonition, upon whom the ends of the world are come.

### Lot's Deliverance — and His Delay

- **Gen. 19:12-14** — The angels tell Lot to bring all he has out of Sodom, for the Lord has sent them to destroy it; but to his sons-in-law he seemed as one that mocked. To be inside a city marked for destruction is to be destroyed with it.
- **Gen. 19:15-17** — The angels lay hold of Lot's hand and his family's and set them outside the city: "Escape for thy life; look not behind thee, neither stay thou in all the plain; escape to the mountain." Not the city, not even the plain — the mountain.
- **Gen. 19:18-20** — Lot argues he cannot escape to the mountain "lest some evil take me," and pleads to flee to the little city of Zoar instead — the same excuses people make today about going only a little way out.
- **PP 159.3** — Lot's sons-in-law laughed at his "superstitious fears"; well enough off where they were, they could see no evidence of danger and could not believe beautiful Sodom would be destroyed.
- **PP 160.1** — Lot delayed; daily distressed yet with no true conception of the city's iniquity, he did not realize the necessity for God's judgments to check sin, and his family clung to Sodom.
- **PP 161.1** — Confused and terrified, Lot pleaded he might die; his faith had grown dim. He should have trusted himself wholly to the divine Messenger without doubt or question, but like so many others he endeavored to plan for himself, fleeing to Bela (Zoar), a city only a few miles off yet equally doomed.
- **PP 161.2** — Lot's wife looked back and became a monument of God's judgment; though her body was on the plain, her heart clung to Sodom and she perished with it because she resented that her possessions and children were left to ruin. Lot's hesitancy and delay led her to lightly regard the divine warning.

### Out of the Cities — Into the Mountains

> Who will be warned? We say again, Out of the cities. — _CL 14.4_

- **CL 14.4** — Do not consider it a great deprivation to go into the hills and mountains; seek the retirement where you can be alone with God to learn His will and way.
- **LDE 95.3** — The Lord calls for His people to locate away from the cities, for in such an hour as ye think not fire and brimstone will be rained from heaven upon these cities; proportionate to their sins will be their visitation. When one city is destroyed, do not regard it as a light affair and build again in that same destroyed city.
- **2SM 356.5** — "If we place ourselves under objectionable influences, can we expect God to work a miracle to undo the results of our wrong course?—No, indeed. Get out of the cities as soon as possible, and purchase a little piece of land" with a garden, where children can watch the flowers growing and learn lessons of simplicity and purity.
- **19MR 334.3** — Let no centers be made in the cities; make no more investments there. Fathers and mothers, gather your children into your own houses, away from those disregarding God's commandments — what shall it profit a man to gain the whole world and lose his own soul.
- **6T 195.1** — The experience of the Israelites was written for the last days; before the overflowing scourge comes, the Lord calls all Israelites indeed to prepare. "Get out of the large cities as fast as possible." Establish church schools and give children the word of God as the foundation of their education.
- **GC 622.4** — The time of trouble such as never was is soon to open; we need an experience we do not now possess and which many are too indolent to obtain. In that time every soul must stand for himself before God; though Noah, Daniel, and Job were in the land, they should deliver only their own souls by their righteousness (Ezek. 14:20).
- **CL 32.1** — "It will then be time to leave the large cities, preparatory to leaving the smaller ones for retired homes in secluded places among the mountains." City is crossed off — large or small; the place of safety is the mountains, read as literally written.

### The Seven Last Plagues Are the Effects of War

- **Rev. 16:10** — The fifth plague brings darkness on the seat of the beast; the meaning of this darkness is unlocked by comparison with Revelation 9.
- **Rev. 9:2** — Darkness rises out of the bottomless pit, a clue for understanding the darkness of Revelation 16:10. (See also Ellen White's 1850 chart on the woes.)
- **Rev. 7:1** — The four winds; in Scripture wind is a symbol of war.
- **Isa. 27:8** — A rough wind and an east wind, the same symbol of war.
- **Rev. 8; Rev. 9** — The seven trumpets; deadly instruments of war will be invented in connection with Armageddon and the plagues.
- **Rev. 9:20** — The trumpets are identified as plagues and as wars, defining the principle that the plagues are wars; the seven trumpets and the seven last plagues strike the exact same areas (darkness, the Euphrates, etc.).

### How Long, and How Far — The Death Decree and the Flight

- **Rev. 18:10** — Babylon's judgment comes "in one hour"; prophetically one hour is a twenty-fourth of a prophetic year, which equals fifteen days.
- **Rev. 17:12** — The ten kings receive power as kings "one hour" with the beast — the same fifteen days during which the ten kings reign with the papacy. (See the 1850 Ellen White chart, downloadable at JudgmentDayDocumentary.com.)
- **Isa. 33:2, 16** — In the time of trouble, when God's people flee into the mountains at the death decree, their bread shall be given and their water sure; this is when we do not store up food but are led by God's angels to the caves.

### Country-Living Q&A: Faith, Family, and the Plan

- **Gen. 7** _(Noah's ark)_ — Faith is not staying in the city expecting a magical bubble of protection; that is presumption. Righteousness was Noah's obedience in building the ark — and the ark was built large, to carry others, not himself alone.
- **Mark 8:36** — What shall it profit a man if he gain the whole world and lose his own soul; an unbelieving spouse is not a reason to disobey God's warning to move — go in faith and pray they follow.
- **Isa. 60** — The Gentiles coming to Zion are called camels and other creatures; the animals brought into the ark represent the great multitude who join the 144,000 (who built the ark) and come in before the door closes — so we prepare provisions not only for ourselves but for all who heed the message.

## I Investigated Ellen G. White's "Country Living" Book — Study 10

### The Image of the Beast and the Mark

> "If we do not want to receive the mark of the beast or worship the image of the beast... and if you don't take that, you're not going to be able to buy or sell." The whole crisis turns on the difference between the image and the mark.

- **Rev. 13:14** — A power on earth deceives men and tells them to make an image to the beast that was wounded by a sword and lived. The image is the punch line of the chapter.
- **Rev. 13:15** — Life is given to the image so that it both speaks and decrees that all who refuse to worship it should be killed.
- **Rev. 13:16** — All — small and great, rich and poor, free and bond — are caused to receive a mark in the right hand or forehead. Not some, but all.
- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell except he have the mark, the name of the beast, or the number of his name. This is why preparation to raise your own produce is urgent.
- **Dan. 7:16, 17** — The angel explains that the beasts are kings or kingdoms; therefore the image is made to a kingdom, not to a person.

### The Out-of-the-Cities Line of Events

> The little booklet _Country Living_ (compiled 1946, after Ellen White's death) gathers her counsel out of chronological order. Read each quote by its original date and the seeming contradictions resolve.

- **Luke 21:20, 21** — As the siege of Jerusalem by Roman armies was the signal for the Judean Christians to flee, so a decree enforcing the papal Sabbath will be the warning to us. CL 32.1; 5T 464.3 (1885)
- **Gen. 19:15-17** — Had Lot stayed in Sodom when destruction came, he could not have been saved; the call to flee is not optional. CL 31.4
- **Gen. 7** — Had Noah not entered the ark, he would not have been saved; obedience must be immediate. The final movements will be rapid ones.

### "Out of the Cities" — The Message Intensifies

> The 1885 statement says "the time is not far distant." By 1906 it is an urgent imperative. Something happened between those dates that changed the emphasis.

- **CL 32.1** — "The time is not far distant... we shall be forced to seek a refuge in desolate and solitary places." 5T 464.3 (1885)
- **CL 31.4** — "Out of the cities; out of the cities! — this is the message the Lord has been giving me." Life Sketches 409, 410; Review and Herald July 5, 1906, par. 7
- **CL 31.4 (context)** — At Loma Linda an awful scene of buildings shaken down passed before her; for years the great cities have been warned of divine judgments for long-continued disobedience. RH July 5, 1906
- **CL 24.2** — "The time has come, when, as God opens the way, families should move out of the cities." It has already come, even if you do not know the sign. Manuscript 50, 1903

### The First Sign Has Already Come

> The warning was tied to a Sunday law. If "the time has come," the first such siege must already be past — and it is.

- **CL 21.1** — "The Protestant world have set up an idol Sabbath... For this reason I see the necessity of the people of God moving out of the cities." Letter 90, 1897
- **John 19:6, 7** — The Sunday idol is set up as was the image; men placing themselves where God should be echo Pilate's accusers who said, "We have a law, and by our law he ought to die." 12LtMs Letter 90, 1897, par. 15
- **Dan. 3** — As Nebuchadnezzar's advisers ensnared the Hebrew captives by a decree that every knee bow to the image, so men strive today to turn God's people from their allegiance; but the persecutors are destroyed by the recoil of their own actions.

### National Reform and the Threatened Sunday Law

> "Petitions signed by thousands have been sent in, asking that the civil and religious interest be not blended." Clergymen professing to serve God uttered blasphemies and cared no more for the Bible than infidels.

- **2 Thess. 2:3, 4** — When men lay aside a plain "Thus saith the Lord" and accept human enactments, they reveal the attributes of the great apostasy.
- **Blair National Sunday-Rest Bill (1888)** — Senate Bill No. 2983, introduced by Senator H. W. Blair, May 21, 1888, to secure the first day of the week as a day of rest and religious worship — the first siege, just as the Roman armies came and withdrew. The Lonely Years vol. 3, p. 395
- **GC 605.3** — Those who present the third angel's message have been called alarmists, their predictions of church-state persecution called groundless; but as Sunday legislation is agitated the message now produces an effect it could not before.
- **CL 11.1** — Erelong there will be such strife and confusion in the cities that those who wish to leave will not be able — and not be able and be lost. We must prepare before the crisis.

### The World's Fair of 1893 and the Forming Image

> Uriah Smith records that in carrying the point to close the Fair on Sunday, Congress did the very thing the Constitution forbids.

- **Ex. 20:8-11** — A senator called for a Bible and had the fourth commandment read; grave statesmen then voted that the day enjoined as the Sabbath is Sunday. DAR 573.4 (Uriah Smith, 1897)
- **DAR 574 (Uriah Smith, 1897)** — This legislating on a religious question broke down the barrier against church-state union; the amendment clergy boast they now have Congress in their hands. "How far away, then, is that image?"
- **GC 445** — When the leading churches of the United States influence the state to enforce their decrees and sustain their institutions, then Protestant America will have formed an image of the Roman hierarchy, and civil penalties on dissenters will result.
- **GC 443** — Wherever the church has obtained secular power she has punished dissent; the Church of England's long persecution of nonconformists in the 16th and 17th centuries is the pattern.
- **2 Thess. 2:3** — "There shall come a falling away, and that man of sin be revealed." Apostasy in the church prepares the way for the image to the beast. GC 443

### The Prophecy Must Be Fulfilled

> Skeptics said an indifferent, liberal America could never enforce religious law. The answer of faith: the prophecy must be fulfilled.

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — To receive the mark in the forehead is to assent in mind to the institution that constitutes it; to receive it in the hand is to signify allegiance by outward act. DAR 579 (Uriah Smith)
- **Rev. 13:11-14** — The very liberal opposition becomes the stimulus that secures the religious amendment; an irrepressible conflict is precipitated and the government must become nominally Christian or wholly secular. The Marvel of Nations 230, 235

### Sealing Time of the 144,000

> The closing work for the church is the sealing of the 144,000 — living saints sealed during the Sunday-law crisis.

- **Rev. 7:4** — The 144,000 are of the tribes of Israel — God's commandment-keeping people who give the final loud cry and are sealed before Jesus comes.
- **Rev. 14:1** — They are a literal number who stand without fault; nothing in a literal 144,000 breaks natural law, so by Miller's rules it is read literally.
- **3T 266.2** — In the closing work for the church, in the sealing time of the 144,000 who stand without fault before the throne, God's people view sin in its real character and deal plainly with it.
- **Hab. 2:2** — God showed Ellen White the need of "getting out a chart" so the truth made plain upon tables would draw souls; the vision came at Otis Nichols' house, who then published it. 15MR 210.3; Letter 26, 1850
- **CL 30** — The cities are to be worked from outposts — not by God's people living in them, but by visiting them to warn of what is coming. Letter 182, 1902

### Truth Is the Plain Interpretation — Against Spiritualizing

> "Truth equals interpretation." When the angel gives the plain interpretation, no symbol remains to be spiritualized away.

- **Dan. 7:16, 17** — Daniel asks "the truth" and is given the interpretation: the symbolic beasts are literal kings. Symbol resolves into plain truth.
- **Dan. 11:2** — "Now will I show thee the truth" — the plain, unsymbolized interpretation; therefore "three kings" means literal kings.
- **GC 598.3** — The truths most plainly revealed have been clouded by learned men who teach the Scriptures have a mystical, secret meaning not in the language; such men Jesus called false teachers who "know not the Scriptures."
- **GC 360.1** — Wolff condemned the phantomizing system that reads "Jews" as Gentiles and "Jerusalem" as the church — the same spiritualizing now applied to Daniel 11.
- **AA 474.2** — The follower of Christ will meet spiritualistic interpretations of Scripture but is not to accept them.
- **1SM 196.3** — Such fanciful, spiritualistic interpretations undermine the foundations of our faith. (Cf. 5BC 1089.1 on the omega of apostasy: a mystical meaning on plain words makes vital truths of little significance.)

### Daniel 8 — Literal Directions, Literal Kingdoms

> The directions and numbers in Daniel are literal, not spiritual. Read by Miller's rules, the prophecy becomes simple.

- **Dan. 8:3, 4** — The ram with two horns pushes west, north, and south, so it is located in the east — Persia, which is modern Iran.
- **Dan. 8:20** — The angel states the ram's two horns are the kings of Media and Persia; no assumption needed.
- **Dan. 8:5-7** — The he-goat comes from the west — Greece — and breaks the ram's horns, as Alexander conquered Persia.
- **Dan. 8:8** — When the great horn is broken, four notable horns come up toward the four winds: Thrace, Syria, Egypt, and Macedonia (the western horn).
- **Dan. 8:9** — Out of one of them (Macedonia, the west) comes the little horn, Rome — therefore Rome arises in the west and cannot be the king of the north.

### Daniel 11 and the King of the North

> "Now will I show thee the truth" — Daniel 11 is the unsymbolized full story of Daniel 8: Persia, Greece, and its four divisions, read by literal geography.

- **Dan. 11:2** — Three kings stand up in Persia, then a fourth richer than all stirs against Greece — plainly named kings, no symbols.
- **Dan. 11:3, 4** — A mighty king (Alexander) rules, then his kingdom is broken and divided toward the four winds — identical to Daniel 8.
- **Dan. 11:40** — At the time of the end the king of the south pushes and the king of the north comes like a whirlwind with chariots, horsemen, and many ships — a literal, realistic war that breaks no natural law.
- **Dan. 12:4** — The time of the end is when Daniel's book is unsealed — after the 1260-year period.
- **Dan. 12:7** — "A time, times, and a half" = 1260 prophetic days. Ezek. 4:6
- **Ezek. 4:6** — "A day for a year" makes the period 1260 years — 538 to 1798, the papal reign.

### The 1798 Fulfillment

> The papacy received its deadly wound February 15, 1798, so it was already out of the way; the king-of-the-north war of verse 40 must be another power.

- **Dan. 11:40** — In 1798 Turkey (the Ottoman Empire, then holding Syria and Lebanon north of Israel) was the king of the north; Egypt the king of the south; the war between Egypt, France, and Turkey fulfilled the chariots, horsemen, and many ships. Bible Readings for the Home Circle (1888) p. 391-393
- **Jer. 4:5-13** — The same invader is the lion, the trumpet of alarm, the destroyer of the Gentiles, the full wind (war) coming as clouds with chariots as a whirlwind and horses swifter than eagles — laying the cities waste.

### The Locust Power of Revelation 9

> The fifth and sixth trumpets describe the same king of the north under the symbol of locusts — the Ottoman power.

- **Rev. 9:2, 3** — Smoke rises from the bottomless pit and locusts come upon the earth — the fifth trumpet, the first woe.
- **Rev. 9:7** — The locusts are like horses prepared for battle with the faces of men (horsemen) and the teeth of lions.
- **Rev. 9:17** — Fire-breathing locust-horses with heads of lions, issuing fire, smoke, and brimstone — breaking natural law, hence a symbol of a nation. GC 334.4 identifies the Ottoman/Turkish power.

### The Locusts Identified — Children of the East

> Scripture interprets its own symbol: the locust is a nation, and that nation is the Muslim descendants of Ishmael.

- **Judges 6:4, 5** — The Midianites come up as grasshoppers (locusts) without number and destroy the increase of the land unto Gaza.
- **Judges 7:12** — The Midianites, Amalekites, and "all the children of the east" lay along the valley like grasshoppers for multitude.
- **Judges 8:24** — Gideon's foes wore golden (crescent) earrings "because they were Ishmaelites" — the locusts are the descendants of Ishmael.
- **Joel 1:4, 6** — The four stages of locust devour the land, "for a nation is come up upon my land" with teeth of a lion — the locust is a kingdom.
- **Joel 1:15, 17, 19** — The day of the Lord is at hand as destruction from the Almighty; barns broken, fields wasted, fire devouring the pastures.
- **Joel 2:1-4** — Blow the trumpet, an alarm of war; a day of darkness, fire before and behind, an army appearing as horses and horsemen.
- **Joel 3:9-12** — Prepare war, beat plowshares into swords; after the harvest the heathen gather to the valley of Jehoshaphat for judgment — the battle scene.

### Daniel 11:44, 45 — Tidings Out of the East and North

> "But tidings out of the east and out of the north shall trouble him." We are watching these very tidings — Iran's war and Russia's involvement — stir the Turk.

- **Dan. 11:44** — Tidings (news) out of the east (Persia/Iran) and the north (Russia) trouble the king of the north (Turkey), who goes forth with great fury to destroy.
- **Dan. 11:45** — He plants his palace between the seas in the glorious holy mountain (Jerusalem) — a literal location — yet comes to his end, and none helps him.
- **Dan. 12:1** — The next event after verse 45 is the close of probation, the standing up of Michael, and the time of trouble such as never was.
- **19LtMs Letter 103, 1904, par. 17** — "The world is stirred with the spirit of war... The prophecy of the eleventh of Daniel has nearly reached its complete fulfillment," and much of that history will be repeated.

### The East Wind, the West Wind, and the Four Winds

> The winds are war; the holding and loosing of them marks the close of probation.

- **Isa. 27:7, 8** — Just before the day of slaughter He stays His rough wind in the day of the east wind — a war in the east blowing before the four winds.
- **Ezek. 9:2, 4** — Six men come from the north with slaughter weapons, but first a mark is set; the sealing precedes the slaughter.
- **Ex. 10:13, 19** — The east wind brings the locust upon Egypt, and a mighty west wind removes it — type of the east wind (war in Iran) bringing the Turk to Jerusalem and a west wind (war from America) removing him.
- **Rev. 7:1** — Four angels hold the four winds of the earth until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads.
- **6T 14** — The four winds are held until the sealing is finished; then the powers of earth marshal for the last great battle.
- **15LtMs Letter 79** — When the restraining power is removed, deadly instruments of warfare will be invented and the nations unite under satanic agencies for the battle of Armageddon.
- **Ezek. 37** — The dry bones come together at prophesying, but live only when the prophet prophesies to the four winds; preaching the four-winds message awakens God's sleeping people.

### The Eastern Question and Armageddon

> The Eastern Question — what the east has to do with prophecy — was foundational Adventism, taught by Uriah Smith, A. T. Jones, and E. J. Waggoner, and affirmed by Ellen White.

- **Rev. 16:16** — The nations are gathered to a place called Armageddon — a literal war, not to be spiritualized away.
- **GC 334** — In 1840 Josiah Litch's exposition of Revelation 9 predicted the fall of the Ottoman Empire to the very day, August 11, 1840 — a fulfillment that excited wide interest.
- **4T 279.1** — Elder Uriah Smith spoke upon the Eastern Question to deep interest; the people listened with earnest attention.
- **Dan. 11:45; Rev. 16:12-21** — Waggoner: the events following the removal of the Turkish capital to Jerusalem are narrated in Revelation 16, and the battle of Armageddon is one of them. Present Truth, Sept. 10, 1896
- **Dan. 12:1** — Waggoner: the king of the north comes to his end in Palestine, then Michael stands up and there is a time of trouble such as never was. Present Truth, April 1, 1897
- **Ev 577** — Daniel and the Revelation should have attention as never before; we may say less of the Roman power and more of what the prophets have written, keeping the human agent out of sight. (Cf. 1MR 60.5: the book Daniel and the Revelation should be brought before people as the very book for this time.) A. T. Jones: the last verses of Daniel 11 relate to Turkey as king of the north at Constantinople. ARSH Oct. 2, 1900
- **RH June 7, 1906** — The events connected with the close of probation are clearly presented in the prophecies, yet multitudes have no more understanding of them than if they had never been revealed, and the time of trouble will find them unready.

## The Criteria for a Country Property — The Final Countdown, Study 11

### The Warning: An Ark for the Coming Destruction

> "We are living in the very last days of this earth's history. The destruction of these cities is a warning to us to prepare for what is coming upon the earth." — Christ Triumphant 56

- **CTr 56** — God gave direction that an ark was to be built for those who desired to be saved from the coming destruction; in the same way He commands us to build our antitypical ark for the coming time of trouble. CTr 56
- **CTr 56** — When God warns us of judgments meant for the wicked, He does not want us to partake of them; if we do, we are counted with the wicked, so we must not be swept away in the modern flood that is coming. CTr 56
- **CTr 56** — He was about to clothe Himself with vengeance and execute His judgment against the transgressors of His law, so we need to be in our ark before the plagues and the time of trouble fall. CTr 56

### Out of the Cities into the Country

- **Letter 5, 1904, par. 3** — Again and again the Lord has instructed that His people take their families away from the cities into the country where they can raise their own provisions, for the problem of buying and selling will be a very serious one.
- **Letter 5, 1904, par. 3** — Get out of the cities into rural districts where the houses are not crowded closely together and where you will be free from the interference of enemies.
- **2SM 356** — He wants us to live where we can have elbow room; if we place ourselves under objectionable influences, we cannot expect God to work a miracle to undo the results of our wrong course. 2SM 356
- **2SM 356** — Get out of the cities as soon as possible and purchase a little piece of land where you can have a garden and your children can learn lessons of simplicity and purity. 2SM 356
- **CL 21** — Move out of the cities into retired country places where you can cultivate the land and raise your own produce, making haste to get all things ready for the crisis. CL 21
- **MM 310** — To parents in the cities the Lord sends the warning cry: gather your children away from those who practice evil and get out of the cities as fast as possible. MM 310
- **MM 310** — Parents can secure small homes in the country with land for cultivation, orchards, vegetables, and small fruits to take the place of flesh meat; God will help His people to find such homes outside the cities. MM 310
- **CL 29** — The Lord desires His people to move into the country, settle on the land, raise their own fruit and vegetables, and bring their children into direct contact with the works of God in nature. CL 29

### Secluded Homes Among the Mountains

- **CL 32** — It will be time to leave the large cities, preparatory to leaving the smaller ones, for retired homes in secluded places among the mountains — homes in the mountains, not caves. CL 32
- **Gen. 19:12-14** — The angels commanded Lot to bring his family out of Sodom because God had sent them to destroy the city; the command was to leave, not to wait for a miracle bubble.
- **Gen. 19:15-17** — "Escape for thy life; look not behind thee, neither stay thou in all the plain; escape to the mountain, lest thou be consumed" — the mountains are where God can protect you.
- **Gen. 19:18-20** — Lot compromised and begged to flee to the little city of Zoar instead of the mountain; his hesitation and debating with God cost him his family.

### Ellen White's Own Garden — What "A Little Piece of Land" Meant

> "Father has excellent health. He has worked hard on the place here... I have been gathering up shrubs and flowers until we have quite a garden."

- **CL 29** — Her personal garden held over an acre of strawberries, raspberries, more than an acre of potatoes, several acres of corn, fifty hard maples, peach and pear trees, and pie plant — more than six acres, which she called "quite a garden." CL 29
- **MS / Paradise Valley account** — The Whites bought adjoining parcels rather than a single small lot; borrowing money to secure land is not against counsel when you have a plan to pay it back quickly, for the property ought not pass into the hands of worldlings.
- **MS (Elmshaven account)** — Ellen White refused to sell small lots or let families crowd in on every spare piece of uncultivated land, showing the property must keep space enough for an uncultivated buffer area.

### How Far From the Cities — "Miles Away"

- **CET 217.1 (1874)** — Oakland, San Francisco, Sacramento, and Woodland were named as "large cities" — yet Sacramento was only 17,000-18,000 and San Francisco 149,000; we must be far from cities like these, not only from New York or Los Angeles. CET 217.1
- **PM 186.4** — The workers connected with the publishing house must be closely guarded, so that young men and women are not placed where they will be ensnared by Satan. PM 186.4
- **FE 492.3** — As the years pass and the city grows, it becomes necessary to move the work to a more rural place; if the cities spread, His people must move farther out, again and again. FE 492.3
- **FE 492.3** — God's people should obtain homes where they can live without requiring high wages — land and houses are far cheaper in the country than in the city. FE 492.3
- **LDE 95.2** — The call is for our people to locate miles away from the large cities; one look at San Francisco shows the necessity of getting out. LDE 95.2
- **PM 186.3** — Of New York, "any place within thirty miles of that city would be too near" — at a wagon pace of 3.5 mph that is 8.5 hours away, and still too close. PM 186.3
- **LDE 107.5** — A school site eight miles from the small town of St. Helena (about 2.3 hours by wagon) was pronounced superior and free from city temptations; the working minimum is at least two hours from a large city. LDE 107.5

### The Size and Substance of the Ark — Land, Water, Wood

- **CL 21** — Noah's ark was not tiny; it was large enough for all the animals and provisioned to last 370 days and beyond — the pattern for a property sized to survive the no-buy-no-sell crisis, getting all things ready beforehand. CL 21
- **CL 21** — Get all things ready for the crisis: a self-sufficient homestead needs garden, greenhouse, livestock, logging, food preservation, water storage, natural remedies, and education — about one acre of land produces a year of food for one person, so plan a five-acre fenced garden, ten acres of wood, ten of pasture, and a minimum of forty-plus acres total. CL 21

### The Saints Are Protected in Their Country Homes

- **EW 34** — In the time of trouble the saints fled from the cities and villages to their homes in the country; the wicked who pursued them with the sword found it broke and fell as powerless as straw. EW 34
- **Rev. 14:9-12** — The third angel's message warns people not to receive the mark of the beast and its plagues; warning the cities of what is coming is the very reason God's people visit them.
- **Rev. 18:1-4** — The fourth angel's message — "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins" — is the final cry to leave the cities; we warn the cities by visiting from outposts, not by living in them.
- **Ev 377** — In every city where the truth is proclaimed churches are to be raised up, and meeting houses may be purchased; but the people of God travel in to work the cities rather than settling in them, and these outposts will grow fewer as the end nears. Ev 377
- **CL 30** — The cities are to be worked from outposts; they are to be warned not by God's people living in them but by visiting them to warn of what is coming upon the earth. CL 30

## What Happens Next Will Shock You — The Final Countdown, Study 12

> An overview of the biblical prophetic timeline of the last days, walking from 1888 to the close of probation, showing that each waymark — out of the cities, the shaking, the sealing, the image of the beast, Sunday law, the loud cry, Satan personating Christ, the seven last plagues, and Armageddon — is grounded in scripture and the Spirit of Prophecy, not in private speculation.

### The First Siege of Sunday Law — 1888

> "We're going to show from the Bible and the Spirit of Prophecy that these aren't just my own ideas." The whole timeline opens in 1888, the year of the National Sunday Rest Bill.

- **Matt. 24** — As Christ warned, the first siege of Jerusalem by the Roman armies was the signal for the Judean Christians to flee; those who waited for the second siege were destroyed. This pattern is the model for the first siege of Sunday law in 1888. _(timeline waymark)_
- The National Sunday-Rest Bill, Senate Bill No. 2983, introduced in the first session of the Fiftieth Congress by Senator H. W. Blair, May 21, 1888 — the first siege, now withdrawn but already accomplished. _(supporting evidence)_
- Ellen White's 1885 warning that, like the early disciples, God's people will be forced to seek refuge, and the decree enforcing the papal Sabbath will be the signal to leave the large cities for retired homes among the mountains. 5T 464.3
- After 1888 she wrote that because the Protestant world had set up an idol Sabbath and was treading in the footsteps of the papacy, she saw the necessity of God's people moving out of the cities to cultivate the land and raise their own produce. CL 21.1

### Righteousness by Faith and the Out-of-the-Cities Message — 1888 Onward

- **Rev. 3** — God's people fell into the Laodicean state; righteousness by faith was rejected, deepening their lukewarmness.
- Righteousness by faith is faith that acts on what God says will happen; Noah was accounted righteous because he built the ark in faith on God's prophecy of the flood — a hearer who does nothing has no righteousness by faith.
- The out-of-the-cities message has been sounding since 1888 — longer than Noah had to build his ark — and runs all the way to the close of probation, calling two separate groups out at two separate times.

### 9/11 and the Buildings That Fall — 2001

> One of the most ominous dates in history; the place of the vision (New York City) is the place of the event.

- **Rev. 18:1-3** — Ellen White declared these verses would be fulfilled when the great buildings of New York are thrown down — a prophecy connected to 2001. LS 411.5
- **Isa. 30:25** — "When the towers fall," rivers and streams of waters break out upon every high mountain — the latter rain, coming as the towers fall.
- Vision of the night season in New York City: fireproof buildings rising story after story, consumed as if made of pitch, the fire engines unable to stay the destruction — a foreview of 9/11. 9T 11.1; 9T 12.3
- "What terrible scenes will take place when the Lord shall rise to shake terribly the earth!" — the whole of Revelation 18 is a warning of what is coming on the earth. LS 411.5

### The Fourth Angel's Message — 2001

- **Rev. 18:1-4** — Another angel comes down from heaven with great power, the earth lightened with his glory, crying "Babylon the great is fallen... Come out of her, my people." This fourth angel falls on the same line as the towers, 2001.
- The fourth angel's message is the beginning of the light whose glory fills the whole earth — it has begun, but is now being given again.

### The Shaking

- **Ezek. 37:7** — Ezekiel prophesied to the dry bones; there was a noise, and behold a shaking, and the bones came together — prophesying the final events causes the shaking.
- **Ezek. 38:19** — In the fire of God's wrath, there shall be a great shaking in the land of Israel; the shaking has commenced in the church and will only grow stronger.

### The Sealing of the 144,000 — Beginning 2001

- **Rev. 7:1-4** — Four angels hold the four winds; another angel ascends from the east with the seal of the living God, sealing the servants of God in their foreheads — and the number sealed is 144,000.
- **Rev. 7:3** — "I heard the number of them which were sealed" — it says the number, not the title, indicating a literal 144,000.
- The seal is not a visible mark but a settling into the truth, both intellectually and spiritually, so they cannot be moved; one cannot settle into the truth unless the truth has first been given. Mar 200.2
- As soon as God's people are sealed and prepared for the shaking, it will come — indeed it has begun, the judgments of God upon the land warning us of what is coming. Mar 200.2
- **1 Pet. 4:17** — Judgment must begin at the house of God; the sealing comes to God's people first.

### The Image of the Beast (Formed Before Probation Closes)

- **Rev. 13:14, 15** — The two-horned beast tells those on earth to make an image to the beast which had the wound by a sword and did live; he gives life to the image, that it should speak and decree death for non-worshipers.
- The image is to be formed by studying the characteristics of the beast itself — the papacy, a church that controlled the power of the state; for the United States to form an image, the religious power must so control the civil government that the church wields the authority of the state. GC 443
- The image of the beast is the church-state structure; it will be formed before probation closes, for it is the great test for the people of God by which their eternal destiny will be decided. 2SM 80.4; LDE 227.3

### National Sunday Law — The Mark of the Beast

> The mark of the beast is Sunday worship; the image of the beast comes first, then the buildup to Sunday law (the second siege).

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — All, small and great, are caused to receive a mark in the right hand or forehead, so that no man may buy or sell save he that has the mark or the name of the beast.
- **Rev. 13:15** — The second beast (America, American Protestantism) gives "life" to a dead institution — resurrecting a church-state structure, not literal life to a person.
- When Sunday observance is enforced by law, those who reject God's sign and honor Rome's token will accept the sign of allegiance to Rome — the mark of the beast; no one has yet received it because it is not yet enforced by the state, and people must first be brought to choose. GC 449.1
- When the nation so abjures the principles of its government as to enact a Sunday law, Protestantism will join hands with popery, giving life to the tyranny long watching to spring again into active despotism. 5T 711.4

### The Loud Cry and Growing Your Own Food

- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — The third angel follows with a loud voice, warning that whoever worships the beast and his image and receives his mark shall drink of the wine of the wrath of God, tormented with fire and brimstone.
- The honest, once bound by husbands and parents, eagerly lay hold of the truth, which becomes dearer than life; the change is the latter rain, the refreshing from the presence of the Lord, the loud cry of the third angel. CET 177.2
- **Rev. 14:14-16** — The Son of man thrusts in His sickle and the harvest of the earth is reaped — the harvesting message of the angels ministering in heaven.
- **Rev. 14:17-19** — Another angel with a sharp sickle gathers the clusters of the vine, for the grapes are fully ripe — the final gathering announced before probation closes.

### The Close of Probation

- **Dan. 11:45–12:1** — He plants the tabernacles of his palace in the glorious holy mountain, yet comes to his end; "and at that time" Michael stands up and a time of trouble begins — one unbroken sentence, the sign that probation has closed.
- When Michael stands up He leaves the most holy place; probation closes — the righteous remain righteous, the filthy filthy still.
- The events connected with the close of probation and the work of preparation for the time of trouble are clearly presented, yet multitudes understand them no better than if never revealed; Satan catches away every impression. DD 36.3

### Satan Personates Christ — At the Close of Probation

> "This quote alone should show it does not happen before Sunday law." Satan's appearing is the culmination, not the precursor.

- The wrath of Satan increases as his time grows short, and his work of deceit reaches its culmination in the time of trouble — after God's long-suffering has ended and the wicked have passed the boundary of their probation. TA 273.1
- As the crowning act in the great drama of deception, Satan himself will attempt to personate Christ — only the wicked welcome him, after the close of probation. TA 273.1

### Turkey Removed From Jerusalem — Daniel 11:45

- **Dan. 11:45** — He comes to his end with none to help him; the removal of the Turkish power from Jerusalem falls on the same line as the close of probation, triggering the time of trouble.

### The Time of Trouble

- **Dan. 12:1** — "There shall be a time of trouble, such as never was since there was a nation" — this trouble begins when Michael stands up at the close of probation, triggered by the events connected with it.

### The Seven Last Plagues and the Third Woe

- **Rev. 15:1** — Seven angels having the seven last plagues, in which is filled up the wrath of God — falling after the close of probation, after a brief gap.
- **Rev. 16:1** — A great voice from the temple commands the seven angels to pour out the vials of the wrath of God upon the earth.
- **Rev. 8:13** — The angel flying through heaven cries "Woe, woe, woe"; the third woe is the seven last plagues, connected to the first two woes.
- **Rev. 9** — The fifth, sixth, and seventh trumpets are the three woes; the first two woes were the Ottoman Empire, so the third woe is also the Ottoman power — a scourge on those who persecute God's people. GC 334.4
- In 1840 the predicted fall of the Ottoman Empire was a remarkable fulfillment of prophecy; Turkey, through her ambassadors, fulfilled it at the very time specified. GC 334.4

### The New World (American) Order

- **Rev. 17:12** — The ten horns are ten kings who have received no kingdom as yet, but receive power as kings one hour with the beast — the new world order, established late in the plagues.
- God holds back the world so His people can finish their work and protects their property until the time to flee — God, not Satan, is in control.

### Jacob's Trouble and the Death Decree — In the Sixth Plague

- **Rev. 13:15** — The image of the beast causes that as many as would not worship should be killed — the death decree.
- **Jer. 30:5-7** — "Alas! for that day is great... it is even the time of Jacob's trouble; but he shall be saved out of it" — once the death decree goes forward, none of God's people die.
- **Rev. 16:12** — The sixth angel pours his vial on the Euphrates and the water is dried up, signifying the Ottoman/Turkish power removed so the ten kings can reign. CET 100.3
- The four angels hold the four winds until Christ's work in the sanctuary is done, and then come the seven last plagues, which enrage the wicked against the righteous — placing the death decree late, in the sixth plague. CET 100.3
- **Rev. 17:12** — The ten kings reign one hour with the beast — one prophetic hour being fifteen literal days, the brief span of the death decree before God's deliverance comes.

### The Battle of Armageddon

- **Rev. 16:16** — He gathers them together into a place called in the Hebrew tongue Armageddon — the closing battle of the prophetic timeline.

### Watch and Write the Vision

> "We're trying to write the vision, make it plain, so that it's clear."

- **Hab. 2:1-4** — Stand upon the watch, write the vision and make it plain upon tables, for it is yet for an appointed time; "the just shall live by faith" — faith in the vision justifies and makes us righteous.
- **Deut. 32:2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain... the showers upon the grass" — the truth and the prophecies are the latter rain, given again now to ripen the harvest.

### Questions Answered: The Cities, Probation, and the Mark

- **Rev. 18:8** — "Her plagues come in one day" — a prophetic day for a year means the plagues fall for one year; "time" in Daniel 12:1 is not a prophetic year but points to a specific time, by Miller's rules.
- The time is near when the large cities will be terribly shaken; let God touch the buildings and in a few hours they are in ruin — God showing what will come on the whole world. CL 7.2
- Calamities most awful will follow one after another; if churches repent and return to their allegiance, other cities may be spared for a time — proof that probation is still open and the destruction is a warning. Ev 27.3
- The wrath of God is preparing to come upon all cities, not all at once but one after another, as Sodom and its surrounding cities were destroyed; if punishment in one city does not lead the others to repentance, their time will come. 17LtMs, Ms 233, 1902, par. 4
- The 144,000 is a literal number — Scripture is not to be spiritualized; "I heard the number," not the title, of those sealed.

## Connecting the Dots - Sunday Law Is Already Here! — Study 13

### The Warning to Flee Jerusalem — A Type for the Last Days

> "This entire chapter is a warning to those who shall live in the last scenes of this earth's history." — _Christ Triumphant_ 353.3.

- **Matt. 24:1, 2** — Jesus foretells that not one stone of the temple will be left upon another; a literal prophecy of Jerusalem's destruction in AD 70.
- **Matt. 24:3** — The disciples ask privately for the sign of His coming and of the end of the world, joining the fall of Jerusalem to the final events.
- **Matt. 24:15-19** — When the abomination of desolation (the Roman armies) stands in the holy place, those in Judea must flee to the mountains without turning back. CTr 353.3.
- **Luke 21:20, 21** — "When ye shall see Jerusalem compassed with armies," flee to the mountains; this was the promised sign the waiting Christians watched for. GC 30.2.
- **Matt. 24** — Christ's prophetic words reached beyond AD 70 to the final conflagration when the Lord arises to punish the world for its iniquity. CTr 353.3.

### The Promised Sign and the Christians' Escape

> "Not one Christian perished in the destruction of Jerusalem." — _Great Controversy_ 30.2.

- **Luke 21:20, 21** — After Cestius surrounded the city he unexpectedly abandoned the siege, giving God's people their opportunity to obey the Savior's warning. GC 30.2.
- **Luke 21:20, 21** — God's merciful providence overruled events so that neither Jew nor Roman should hinder the flight of the Christians; the Jews pursued the retreating army while the believers escaped. GC 30.2.
- **Matt. 24:16-20** — When the siege was resumed by Titus terrible calamities fell upon Jerusalem, but every obedient Christian had already fled. GC 30.2.
- **Luke 21:21** — Josephus records 1.1 million perished, the majority Jews trapped during Passover; the warning to obey and leave is deadly serious. _(Wikipedia, Siege of Jerusalem, 70 CE.)_

### The Day of the Lord Comes as a Thief

> "For yourselves know perfectly that the day of the Lord so cometh as a thief in the night." — 1 Thess. 5:2.

- **1 Thess. 5:1, 2** — Paul says the times and seasons are so plain he need not write; the day of the Lord arrives unseen, like a thief, upon the unwatchful.
- **Rev. 16:14** — The kings of the earth are gathered to "the battle of the great day of God Almighty" — the day of God's plagues.
- **Rev. 18:8** — Her plagues come in one day, death and mourning and famine, and she is utterly burned with fire; this is the day of the Lord.
- **Joel 2:1, 2** — Blow the trumpet in Zion and sound an alarm, for the day of the Lord is at hand — a day of darkness, gloominess, and thick darkness.
- **1 Thess. 5:2, 3** — The day of the Lord is the close of probation; when they say "peace and safety," then sudden destruction comes and they shall not escape.
- **1 Thess. 5:4-8** — Believers are not in darkness that the day should overtake them as a thief; they must watch and be sober, putting on faith, love, and the hope of salvation.
- **1 Thess. 5:9** — God hath not appointed us to wrath but to obtain salvation through Jesus Christ; He does not want us to perish in the plagues poured on the cities.

### Unconscious of the Close of Probation

- **1 Thess. 5:3** — The wicked will be planting, building, eating, and drinking, unconscious that the final irrevocable decision has been pronounced in the sanctuary above. Mar 264.2.
- **Gen. 7:16** — As Noah entered the ark and the door was shut unknown to the world, so probation will close before men realize it; once shut it is closed forever.

### Not One in Twenty Ready — The Solemn Call to Obey

> "It is a solemn statement that I make to the church, that not one in twenty whose names are registered upon the church books are prepared to close their earthly history." — _Christian Service_ 41.1.

- **Matt. 24:16** — As the obedient Christians proved their faith by physically fleeing Jerusalem, so readiness requires action, not merely profession. ChS 41.1.
- **Luke 21:21** — The faithful who fled were few against Jerusalem's millions; look only to Jesus and obey, not to the unprepared majority. ChS 41.1.

### The Decree Enforcing the Papal Sabbath — Our Signal

> "As the siege of Jerusalem by the Roman armies was the signal for flight to the Judean Christians, so the assumption of power on the part of our nation, in the decree enforcing the papal Sabbath, will be a warning to us." — _Country Living_ 32 (1885).

- **Luke 21:20** — The Roman siege was the signal to flee; the national decree enforcing Sunday is the parallel signal to leave the large cities for retired homes in secluded places among the mountains. CL 32.1.
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — A time is coming when none may buy or sell; therefore God's people must move to the country to raise their own provisions. RH July 5, 1906, par. 10.
- **Rev. 18:8** — "Out of the cities; out of the cities!" — the earthquakes and floods will come, and we are not to establish ourselves in the wicked cities. RH July 5, 1906, par. 10.
- **Lt 90, 1897** — The Protestant world has set up an idol Sabbath and treads in the footsteps of the papacy; hence the necessity of moving out of the cities to cultivate the land. CL 21.1.

### The First Siege — The Sunday Law Has Already Come

> "The time is not far distant..." (1885) became "Out of the cities!" (1906) — the warning sign fell between those dates.

- **Rev. 10:6** — There should be "time no longer"; we cannot measure our siege as Jerusalem's was measured, so we must make haste while probation lingers.
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — The National Sunday-Rest Bill (Senate Bill No. 2983, introduced by Senator H. W. Blair, May 21, 1888) was the first siege and the out-of-the-cities call. _(National Sunday-Rest Bill, 1888.)_
- **RH Dec. 18, 1888** — We are not to presume on prophecy and let calamity come; we must work earnestly to delay as long as possible the threatened calamity and lift the standard of religious liberty.

### Preparing the Ark — Grow Your Own Food

> "Though the dwelling may be small, yet there should be land in connection with it that may be cultivated." — _Adventist Home_ 139.4 (RH, 1903).

- **Gen. 6:14-22** — As Noah labored 120 years to build and provision the ark, so the country home must be a true ark, not a tiny dinghy, prepared with seeds, food, and shelter for years.
- **Rev. 13:17** — Because none may buy or sell, the family must raise produce, flax, and herbs — not merely survive on potatoes — to be healthy for the final medical-missionary work. CL 21.1.
- **Heb. 11:7** — Noah's obedience was counted righteousness; the same obedient action of leaving the cities is required of God's people now.

### Martyrs, the 144,000, and the Final Loud Cry

- **Deut. 33:25** — "As thy days, so shall thy strength be" — those who flee, are imprisoned, or are martyred _prior to the last closing conflict_ are promised strength. _(Letters and Manuscripts, vol. 6, Ms 6, 1889, par. 40.)_
- **Rev. 14:1-5** — The 144,000 are living saints who must be alive to see Jesus come and to give the loud cry, so they do not perish in the great destruction; type of Noah's family and the three Hebrews.
- **Dan. 3** — The three worthies who refused to bow to the image are a type of those who will face the image of the beast; none of them died.
- **Dan. 11:45; 12:1** — Daniel 11:45 is the sign immediately before Michael stands up; since probation has not closed, verse 45 cannot yet be fulfilled.
- **Dan. 12:1** — "At that time shall Michael stand up" marks the close of probation and the beginning of the time of trouble such as never was.

### Bread and Water Sure — The Time of Trouble vs. Sunday Law

> Mar 181.2 is misapplied to Sunday-law preparation; it belongs to the later flight into the wilderness.

- **Dan. 12:1** — The time of trouble follows the close of probation, years after Sunday law; only then does the counsel against laying up provision apply. Mar 181.2.
- **Isa. 33:2** — "O Lord, be gracious unto us... be thou our arm every morning, our salvation also in the time of trouble" — the time when the plagues are falling.
- **Isa. 33:16** — "He shall dwell on high... bread shall be given him; his waters shall be sure" — God sustains the fleeing saints in the wilderness, when planning to hide food becomes contrary to principle. Mar 181.2.

### How Far From the City — Counsel on the Country Home

- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and receive not of her plagues" — the heart of the fourth angel's message and the final call out of the cities.
- **Rev. 18:1** — Another angel comes down with great power, lighting the earth with his glory and crying mightily; the final loud cry that gathers the great multitude into the ark.

## Are You the 1 in 20? — Study 14 (The Final Countdown)

### The Shaking and the Winnowing Floor

> "We are in the shaking time, the time when everything that can be shaken will be shaken." The next event on the timeline is a mighty shaking among God's people that decides who is ready for the events to come.

- **Matt. 3:12** — Christ holds the fan, thoroughly purges His threshing floor, gathers the wheat into the garner, and burns the chaff; the winnowing of grain is itself a shaking process.
- **6T 331.2** — "If we only knew what is before us we would not be so dilatory in the work of the Lord. We are in the shaking time, the time when everything that can be shaken will be shaken." The shaking has begun, though the final shaking comes in the crisis. _Testimonies vol. 6, 331.2_
- **3SM 385.3** — The great issue so near at hand will weed out those whom God has not appointed, leaving a pure, true, sanctified ministry prepared for the latter rain. _Selected Messages vol. 3, 385.3_
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — The great issue is the mark of the beast and the no-buy, no-sell crisis, the final division of God's people from the world. An initial weeding out comes first, then rain and harvest, then a final shaking.

### The Cause: A Straight Testimony to Laodicea

- **EW 270.2** — The shaking is caused by the straight testimony called forth by the counsel of the True Witness to the Laodiceans; some will not bear it and rise up against it, and this is what causes the shaking among God's people. _Early Writings 270.2_
- **Rev. 3:14-17** — The message of the True Witness to Laodicea: lukewarm, thinking themselves rich and in need of nothing, yet wretched, miserable, poor, blind, and naked — blind to prophecy and ignorant of the final events.
- **6T 331.2** — The Lord will not excuse those who know the truth if they do not in word and deed obey His commands; if we make no effort to win souls we are held responsible for the work we might have done. The sealing is a settling into the truth, which cannot happen without first knowing the truth. _Testimonies vol. 6, 331.2_

### False Theories and a Spurious Loud Cry

- **TM 112.1** — When the shaking comes by the introduction of false theories, surface readers anchored nowhere slide like shifting sand into any position; Daniel and Revelation must be studied, along with the other prophecies of the Old and New Testaments. _Testimonies to Ministers 112.1_
- **Deut. 32:1, 2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain" — the latter rain is the true doctrine returning to the people; on the wrong foundation it looks like a storm of confusion, on the right foundation it ripens the fruit.
- **3SM 410.1** — God is raising up a class to give the loud cry; Satan's object is to get up new theories and a spurious loud cry that the real message may not have its effect when it comes — itself one of the greatest evidences the true loud cry will soon be heard. _Selected Messages vol. 3, 410.1_

### Serving Mammon: Not One in Twenty

> "It is a solemn statement that I make to the church, that not one in twenty are prepared to close their earthly history." Are you the one in twenty?

- **ChS 41.1** — Not one in twenty whose names are registered upon the church books are prepared to close their earthly history; they are professedly serving God but more earnestly serving mammon, a half-and-half work that constantly denies Christ. A worker can serve mammon by withholding truth to keep funding, friends, or membership. _Christian Service 41.1_
- **GC 608.2** — As the storm approaches, a large class who professed the third angel's message but were never sanctified by obedience abandon their position and join the opposition; gifted, pleasing evangelists employ their powers to deceive, become the most bitter enemies of their former brethren, and stir up the rulers when Sabbathkeepers are brought before the courts. _Great Controversy 608.2_

### Separation, Unity, and Departing from the Faith

- **6T 400.3** — As trials thicken, both separation and unity appear in the ranks; some now ready to take up weapons of warfare will prove they did not build on the rock, and those who had great light but did not improve it go out from us, not having received the love of the truth, departing from the faith. Departing the faith means leaving the truth — bright lights ceasing to emit light. _Testimonies vol. 6, 400.3_
- **CET 107.3** — The mighty shaking has commenced and will go on; all will be shaken out who are not willing to take a bold, unyielding stand for the truth and to sacrifice for God — a sacrifice that must be a freewill offering, for the plain truths of God's word, not thunders and lightnings of Sinai, are what move the honest. _Christian Experience and Teachings 107.3_

### The Latter Rain and the Valley of Dry Bones

- **CET 177.2** — Those clothed with the armor speak forth truth with great power; the bound are freed, the honest lay hold of truth dearer than life — and the angel explains the great change is the latter rain, the refreshing from the presence of the Lord, the loud cry of the third angel. _Christian Experience and Teachings 177.2_
- **Ezek. 37:1-10** — The valley is full of very dry bones (Israel, blind and dead); the prophet is commanded to prophesy, and as he prophesies there is a noise and a shaking and the bones come together — yet there is no breath until he prophesies to the four winds and the breath enters and they stand, an exceeding great army.
- **Rev. 7:1-3** — Four angels hold the four winds until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads; the four winds of Ezekiel reappear here, tied directly to the sealing of the 144,000 — the sealing being a settling into the truth.

### The Prophets Who Slay the Bones

- **Micah 3:1-5** — The heads of Jacob and princes of Israel, who should know judgment, instead pluck the skin off God's people and break their bones; these are the prophets who make the people err, biting with their teeth and crying "Peace" — false teachers who left the people listless and unaware of the final events.

### Built Upon the Rock — What Cannot Be Shaken

- **Mar 219.4** — There must be no neglect of the former rain; only those living up to the light they have receive greater light, and unless we daily advance in the active Christian virtues we will not recognize the latter rain even as it falls all around us. _Maranatha 219.4_
- **Matt. 7:24-27** — The wise man who hears and does builds on the rock; when the rain, the floods, and the winds beat on the house it stands, but the house of the hearer-who-does-not falls, and great is its fall.
- **Heb. 12:25-27** — "Yet once more" signifies the removing of the things that are shaken, that the things which cannot be shaken may remain; to be shaken is to be removed, so we must be so settled into the truth we cannot be moved.
- **Amos 9:9** — God will sift the house of Israel among all nations as corn is sifted in a sieve, yet not the least grain shall fall to the earth — the striving will be shaken but not removed.
- **Isa. 30:28** — His breath, like an overflowing stream, reaches to the midst of the neck to sift the nations; the unpopular truth will rise to the neck, and only the firm will not be swept away and drowned.

### Q&A: Time, the Latter Rain, and the 1260 Years

- **Rev. 10:6** — "Time no longer" is prophetic time, suspended since October 22, 1844, during the day of atonement; therefore no date can be set for Christ's coming, and dates like 2027 or 2030 break the laws of prophecy. Prophetic time resumes at the close of probation.
- **Mar 219.2** — There is to be a wonderful manifestation of God's power, but it will not move those who have not humbled themselves; in their blindness they think it dangerous, brace to resist it, and persist in saying "I am rich, and increased with goods, and have need of nothing" — bracing against the latter rain. _Maranatha 219.2_
- **Rev. 12:6** — The woman in the wilderness 1,260 days; on the day-for-a-year principle this is 1,260 years, the period of papal supremacy.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — "I have appointed thee each day for a year" — the prophetic key that converts the 1,260 days into 1,260 years.
- **Rev. 13:5** — The beast continues forty-two months — the same 1,260 days/years of the antichrist (papal) power, fulfilled from A.D. 538 to 1798 when it received the deadly wound.
