## The Judgment has Already Started and No One Noticed — Study 15

### Plagues and Judgments Are Already Falling

> "We are living in the time of the end. The fast-fulfilling signs of the times declare that the coming of Christ is near at hand." — 9T 11.

- **Rev. 16** — When we hear "plagues" we think of the seven last plagues poured out after probation closes; yet Ellen White says plagues and judgments are "already falling," so there must be plagues before the seven last plagues. 9T 11.
- **Rev. 9:20** — "The rest of the men which were not killed by these plagues" — Revelation 9 continues the seven trumpets of Revelation 8, and these trumpet judgments are themselves called plagues, proving plagues have already fallen.
- **Rev. 8** — The seven trumpets are seven alarms of war; a trumpet is an alarm of war, and these were literal wars in history, so wars and plagues can be used synonymously. GC 334.
- The calamities by land and sea, the unsettled state of society, and the alarms of war are portentous, forecasting approaching events of the greatest magnitude; the agencies of evil are consolidating for the last great crisis, and the final movements will be rapid ones. 9T 11.

### Nine-Eleven, the Day of the Lord, and Daniel 11

> "On one occasion, when in New York City, I was in the night season called upon to behold buildings rising story after story toward heaven... warranted to be fireproof." — 9T 12.

- The "scene that next passed before me was an alarm of fire" — the lofty, supposedly fireproof buildings were pronounced perfectly safe, yet were consumed as if made of pitch, and the fire engines could do nothing to stay the destruction. 9T 13.
- **Dan. 11** — "The world is stirred with the spirit of war. The prophecy of the eleventh chapter of Daniel has nearly reached its complete fulfillment." This vision of burning New York buildings was 9/11, tied directly to war, oil, and the Middle East. 9T 14.
- **Joel 1:15-18, 12** — "Alas for the day! for the day of the Lord is at hand"; the vine is dried up and joy is withered from the sons of men, the very passage Ellen White quotes to identify the day of the Lord. 9T 15.
- **Rev. 16:14** — The spirits of devils go forth to gather the kings of the earth "to the battle of that great day of God Almighty"; the day of God, the day of the Lord, is the day of the plagues.
- **Jer. 4:19, 20** — "I am pained at my very heart... because thou hast heard, O my soul, the sound of the trumpet, the alarm of war. Destruction upon destruction is cried; for the whole land is spoiled." 9T 15.
- **Jer. 4:7, 11-13** — The destroyer of the Gentiles comes to lay the cities waste; a dry wind and a full wind, clouds and chariots like a whirlwind, horses swifter than eagles, and a woe pronounced — note the whirlwind, horses, chariots, and woe.

### The Four Winds and the Sealing of the Living

- **Rev. 7:1-3** — Four angels stand on the four corners of the earth holding the four winds that they should not blow on the earth, the sea, or any tree, until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads.
- **Rev. 7:2** — Another angel ascends from the east having the seal of the living God and cries with a loud voice; the loud voice signals urgency, for the winds were about to blow and God's people were not yet sealed.
- **Dan. 7:2** — The winds strove upon the great sea; winds are a symbol of war, blowing every time a nation changes power, so holding the four winds is restraining strife and commotion among rising nations.

### Early Writings: "Hold! Hold! Hold! Hold!"

> "I saw four angels who had a work to do on the earth, and were on their way to accomplish it." — EW 38.

- Jesus is clothed with priestly garments, still in the most holy place of the heavenly sanctuary, which means probation is open and people can still repent; yet His people were not ready for the four winds, which only blow after probation closes. EW 38.
- He gazed in pity on the remnant and cried, "My blood, Father, My blood, My blood, My blood!" — four pleadings of His blood for the four winds. EW 38.
- An angel with a commission from Jesus flew swiftly to the four angels, waving something up and down and crying with a loud voice, "Hold! Hold! Hold! Hold! until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads" — a four-fold "Hold" for the four winds. EW 38.
- The four angels had power from God to hold the four winds and were about to let them go; but while their hands were loosening and the winds were about to blow, the merciful eye of Jesus gazed on the unsealed remnant and bid them hold until His servants were sealed with the seal of the living God. EW 38.

### The Judgment Passing to the Living

> "Solemn are the scenes connected with the closing work of the atonement. Momentous are the interests involved therein." — CIHS 188.1.

- The judgment is now passing in the sanctuary above, and for many years this work has been in progress; soon — none know how soon — it will pass to the cases of the living, and our lives are to come up in review. CIHS 188.1.
- **Rev. 10:6** — There is no prophetic time from 1844 until the close of probation, so we do not know the day, yet we are told it will "soon" pass to the cases of the living.
- **Rev. 3:3** — "Watch and pray, for ye know not when the time is. If therefore thou shalt not watch, I will come on thee as a thief" — only those who are not watching have Him come as a thief. CIHS 188.1.
- **6T 130** — "Now, when the great work of judging the living is about to begin, shall we allow unsanctified ambition to take possession of the heart and lead us to neglect the education required to meet the needs in this day of peril?"

### Ezekiel 9: The Mark Before the Slaughter

- **Ezek. 9:1-3** — A loud voice calls every man with his destroying weapon near, and six men come with slaughter weapons, one clothed in linen with a writer's inkhorn — the same loud voice and slaughter that the four winds bring.
- **Ezek. 9:4-6** — Go through Jerusalem and set a mark upon the foreheads before the slaughter; slay utterly old and young, "but come not near any man upon whom is the mark; and begin at my sanctuary" — the sealing of living people protects them from the slaughter weapon, exactly as in Revelation 7.

### The Seal Is a Settling Into the Truth

- **Mar 200.2** — "Just as soon as the people of God are sealed in their foreheads — it is not any seal or mark that can be seen, but a settling into the truth, both intellectually and spiritually, so they cannot be moved." You cannot be settled in something you are unsure of; the sealed must know, study, and not be deceived about the final events.
- The judgments of God are now upon the land, "to give us warning, that we may know what is coming" — the shaking has begun already, and the 9/11 plague-judgment-war stirring is a warning of what is coming. Mar 200.2.

### The Latter Rain Is Doctrine

> "Ask ye of the Lord rain in the time of the latter rain." — Zech. 10:1.

- **Zech. 10:1, 2** — Ask for rain in the time of the latter rain; "for the idols have spoken vanity, and the diviners have seen a lie, and have told false dreams; they comfort in vain" — a command, not a question, proving we can know the time of the latter rain.
- **Deut. 32:2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain, my speech shall distil as the dew... and as the showers upon the grass" — the rain and the showers are doctrine, specifically the correct doctrine of the final events; the latter rain is the sealing message.
- **Ezek. 37:7-10** — Prophesying to the wind to "come from the four winds" is what woke the dry bones; preaching the prophecy of the four winds and the judgment of the living, with its noise and shaking, is what raises the army.

### Woe to the Idol Shepherds

- **Zech. 11:17** — "Woe to the idol shepherd that leaveth the flock" — not the "idle" lazy pastor, but the idol (I-D-O-L) shepherd; his arm is dried up and his right eye darkened, so he cannot see prophecy or have vision.
- **Isa. 30:22, 23** — First you must defile and cast away your graven images of silver and molten images of gold — your pet ideas and false doctrines — and only then will He give the rain of thy seed; you cannot receive new doctrine while still clinging to the false.
- **Hosea 13:2** — They have made molten images "according to their own understanding" — the idols are their own private understanding of prophecy, leaving them dead bones fed false vision and false peace.
- **Micah 3:2, 5, 6** — The prophets who make God's people err "bite with their teeth, and cry, Peace"; therefore night and darkness shall be upon them and they shall have no vision — false prophecy leaves men with no light.

### Be Not in Darkness — You Know the Times

- **1 Thess. 5:1-2** — "Of the times and the seasons, brethren, ye have no need that I write unto you. For yourselves know perfectly that the day of the Lord so cometh as a thief in the night" — the church knew the prophecies, times, and seasons.
- **1 Thess. 5:3-4** — "When they shall say, Peace and safety, then sudden destruction cometh upon them... But ye, brethren, are not in darkness, that that day should overtake you as a thief" — only the wicked are surprised by the day of the Lord.
- **Rev. 18:8** — The day that comes as a thief is not the loud, glorious Second Coming but the day of the Lord, the close of probation, the day of the plagues.

### When the Plagues Fall, Few Will Know

- **Rev. 16:15** — "Behold, I come as a thief. Blessed is he that watcheth and keepeth his garments" — brought up only in the sixth plague, this is when the deceived finally realize they were false prophets, dry bones, naked and miserable, poor and blind.
- **GC 334.4** — The falling of the Ottoman power on August 11, 1840 was an exact fulfillment of prophecy; God takes care that prophecy and history agree, for if more than one event could perfectly fulfill it there would be no absolute truth — denying a perfect fulfillment by attributing it to Satan is a dangerous deception.
- There is to be in the churches a wonderful manifestation of the power of God, but it will not move those who have not humbled themselves; they will see only something they think dangerous, something which will arouse their fears, and they will brace themselves to resist and oppose the work, asking, "Should we not know the Spirit of God, when we have been in the work so many years?" LDE 209.3 (Review and Herald Extra, Dec. 23, 1890).

### Questions Answered

- **Dan. 11:41** — On who escapes out of the hand of the king — Edom, Moab, and the chief of the children of Ammon — consult Uriah Smith's _Daniel and Revelation_ (the 1897 edition that Ellen White referred to), at his treatment of Daniel 11:41.
- **Rev. 6** — The four horsemen of the apocalypse (white, red, black, and pale horse) are the seven seals of Revelation 6; study Uriah Smith's _Daniel and Revelation_ (1897) on Revelation 6.
- **Rev. 7:1** — The four winds are sent by God, not Satan; they are God's angels and God's plagues, and it was God who said "Hold," for the four winds bring His judgments, destruction, and death upon the earth.

## The Final Warning Has Begun — Are You Ready for the Close of Probation? — Study 16

### The Closing Scenes of the Atonement

> "Solemn are the scenes connected with the closing work of the atonement." The judgment of the living is now upon us, and there are real events — scenes — attending the close of probation.

- **Mark 13:33** — Watch, lest probation pass while you are unaware; only the watching servant will not be overtaken. CIHS 188.1
- **Rev. 3:3** — To the one who refuses to watch, Christ comes as a thief; the watchful know what to look for and are not surprised. CIHS 188.1
- The events connected with the close of probation and the work of preparation for the time of trouble have been _clearly_ presented; any version of these events newer than that original presentation is error. DD 36.3

### The Four Winds and the Angry Horse

- **Rev. 7:1-3** — Four angels hold the four winds from the four corners of the earth until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads; the winds and the sealing are linked.
- The four winds are represented as "an angry horse seeking to break loose, and rush over the face of the whole earth, bearing destruction and death in its path." 3SM 409.1
- **Jer. 4:7, 11-13** — The destroyer of the Gentiles comes; a full wind (north, south, east, and west) brings desolation, his chariots as a whirlwind and his horses swifter than eagles — this is past the time of cleansing.

### The Locust Army of Joel

- **Joel 1:4-6** — The palmerworm, locust, cankerworm, and caterpillar are four stages of one creature; this devouring locust is "a nation" with the teeth of a lion.
- **Joel 2:1-5** — The day of the Lord brings darkness, fire, and devastation; the locusts have "the appearance of horses," running like horsemen with the noise of chariots set in battle array.
- **Dan. 7:16, 17** — A beast represents a kingdom; the locust, being a beast, likewise represents a nation.
- **Rev. 16:14** — The day of the Lord is "the battle of that great day of God Almighty."
- **Rev. 18:8** — Her plagues come in one day — death, mourning, famine, and fire; this is the day of the Lord, the day of the plagues.

### The Locusts Identified as Turkey

- **Rev. 9:2-4** — Smoke from the bottomless pit darkens the sun; locusts come forth and are commanded to hurt only the men who lack the seal of God — the same sealing language as Revelation 7.
- **Rev. 9:7-9** — The locusts are shaped like horses prepared for battle, with teeth as the teeth of lions (as in Joel and Jeremiah) and wings sounding like chariots and many horses running to battle.
- **Judges 6:5, 6; 7:12; 8:24** — The locust-like multitudes are identified as the descendants of Ishmael and the people of the east.
- The 1840 fulfillment was Josiah Litch's exposition of Revelation 9, predicting the fall of the Ottoman Empire; at the time specified, Turkey accepted the protection of the allied powers — the event exactly fulfilled the prediction. GC 334, 335
- These horsemen, chariots, full winds, and whirlwinds are all "the same players" — the worldwide war from Turkey held back by the angels.

### Revelation 18 and the Fall of the Towers

> "Then the words of Revelation 18:1-3 will be fulfilled" when the great buildings of New York are thrown down. LS 411.5

- **Rev. 18:1-3** — Another angel lightens the earth with his glory, crying "Babylon is fallen, is fallen"; verse 3 names the merchants of the earth, identifying the World Trade Center where the merchants of the earth combined.
- **John 9:7** — The blind man must wash in the pool of Siloam ("Sent") to come seeing; Laodicea, thinking she sees, must wash to receive her sight.
- **Luke 13:4, 5** — Those slain when the tower in Siloam fell were no worse sinners than others; their fall was a warning to repent — a picture of the towers falling as a sign of coming judgment.
- **Rev. 18:4, 11** — "Come out of her, my people" and the merchants weeping that no man buys their merchandise belong to a later event than 9/11 — the full outpouring of the plagues, not the 2001 sign.

### Searching Jerusalem and the Sealing

- **Zeph. 1:9-12** — A great crashing and a cry connected with the merchants precede the time when God searches Jerusalem with candles — the sealing work.
- **Ezek. 9** — God's mark is set on the foreheads of those who sigh and cry for the abominations; six men with slaughter weapons follow — the day of slaughter comes right after the sealing.
- **Isa. 27:2, 3, 6-9** — God waters His vineyard night and day with the latter rain (the doctrine), causing Israel to take root, blossom, and fill the world with fruit; He stays His rough wind in the day of the east wind, after which the iniquity of Jacob is purged.

### The Latter Rain and the Whirlwind

- **Joel 2:23** — God gives the former rain moderately, then pours the latter rain on roots grown deep enough to absorb it — the truth coming to prepared soil.
- **Hosea 8:7** — They have sown the wind (war) and shall reap the whirlwind (the four winds); the stalk yields no meal and the stranger swallows it up — the meaning of 9/11.
- **Prov. 1:27** — Destruction comes as a whirlwind; the destruction of the wicked in the plagues is that whirlwind.
- **Isa. 30:25** — Rivers and streams of waters come upon every high mountain "in the day of the great slaughter, when the towers fall" — the latter rain (original doctrine) returning since 2001.

### Turkey as a National Guidepost

> "These angels now hold the winds of strife, waiting for the church of God to prepare for his coming."

- The sealing angel goes through Jerusalem (the church) to seal the faithful, and while this work goes forward Turkey stands as a national guidepost, that men may know what is going on in the sanctuary above. SDP 248
- **Dan. 11:45** — "No man knows when Turkey will take its departure from Europe, but when that move is made, earth's history will be short"; the events at the close of probation are the original, clearly-presented teaching, not the spiritualized interpretation. SDP 248
- **Dan. 12:1** — At that time (the end of Dan. 11:45) Michael stands up and there is a time of trouble such as never was — this is the close of probation, the event clearly presented. SDP 248
- The fate of Babylon, Medo-Persia, Greece, and Rome is recorded for today's nations; while the world watches Turkey, let the servant of God watch his great High Priest, whose ministry for sin is almost over. SDP 248

### The Spurious Loud Cry and the True

- **Acts 3:19** — "Repent ye therefore... that your sins may be blotted out, when the times of refreshing shall come" — the latter rain now falling; do not mistake it for something else.
- Satan stirs up minds to give false interpretations of Scripture — a spurious loud cry — so the real message may not have its effect; this is one of the greatest evidences that the true loud cry will soon be heard. 3SM 410.1
- If a message causes no shaking among the conservative Laodiceans, it is because it is not the true loud cry, not the latter rain.

### Question: Are the Locust Armies of Joel God's Remnant?

- **Joel 2:11** — God calls the locust host "his army" because they do His bidding (the destruction of the plagues), not because they are His holy people; the heathen who sting like scorpions are never God's description of His people.
- **Rev. 17:16** — The ten horns hate the harlot and burn her, for God put it in their hearts to fulfill His will; doing God's will does not make them God's people.
- **Joel 3:11** — "Assemble yourselves... all ye heathen" — confirming this devouring army is the heathen, not the remnant.

### Question: Did 9/11 Begin the Third Woe?

- **Rev. 9 (Third Woe)** — No; the third woe falls _after_ the close of probation and is the same as the seven last plagues, administered through worldwide war. The 1850 Adventist chart reads "The third woe cometh quickly — the seven last plagues."
- **Dan. 11:45 / Dan. 12:1** — Turkey going to Jerusalem is the sign that probation will close imminently; when Turkey is removed from Jerusalem and Michael stands up, Turkey wages all-out war, beginning the third woe — the seven last plagues.

## 144,000: Who Are They? — Study 17 (The Final Countdown)

### The Sealing And The Four Winds

> "If it is connected to this 144,000 in this chapter, do you not think there is some connection between the 144,000 and these four winds?"

- **Rev. 7:1-4** — Four angels hold the four winds of the earth until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads; the number sealed is 144,000 of all the tribes of the children of Israel.
- **Rev. 17:15** — The "sea" in this prophecy is people; so the winds are held back from blowing on the peoples of the earth.
- **Rev. 7:2-4** — Another angel ascends from the east with the seal of the living God, crying to the four angels not to hurt the earth till the sealing is complete; once the sealing is over, probation shuts and the winds blow.
- **GC 439** — Winds are a symbol of strife; the four winds striving upon the great sea represent the terrible scenes of conquest and revolution by which kingdoms have attained to power.
- **3SM 409.1** — The angels hold the four winds, represented as an angry horse seeking to break loose and rush over the whole earth, bearing destruction and death in its path.
- **Dan. 12:1** — Michael stands up and a time of trouble such as never was follows; at that time God's people who are written in the book are delivered — the same scene that follows the sealing.

### The Mark Of The Beast And The Final Test

- **Rev. 13** — The whole question circles back to the mark of the beast: the exaltation by human authority of Sunday in place of the Bible Sabbath. 7T 141
- **7T 141** — The substitution of the laws of men for the law of God, exalting Sunday in place of the Sabbath, is the last act in the drama; when it becomes universal, God will reveal Himself.
- **15MR 15** — The image of the beast is formed before probation closes, for it is the great test by which the eternal destiny of God's people is decided; this is the test they must pass before they are sealed.
- **15MR 15** — All who keep God's law and refuse a spurious sabbath receive the seal of the living God; those who accept the Sunday sabbath receive the mark of the beast.
- **RH Jan 30, 1900** — There can be only two classes: each is stamped either with the seal of the living God or with the mark of the beast.

### Israel Of Today And The Sinfulness Of Sin

- **9T 164** — To be purified, Seventh-day Adventists must have the Holy Spirit; "the Israel of today" who humble themselves are God's modern Israel, not literal Jews removed as a people after the stoning of Stephen.
- **3T 266** — The true people of God view sin in its real, sinful character and deal faithfully with sin, especially in the closing work of the church, in the sealing time of the 144,000 who are to stand without fault before the throne of God.

### Not All Who Profess Will Be Sealed

> "Not all who profess to keep the Sabbath will be sealed."

- **CET 189** — Many who teach the truth to others will not receive the seal; they had the light of truth and knew every point of the faith, but had not corresponding works and did not command their households after them.
- **Matt. 8:29 / Mark 5:7** — Even the devils confessed Jesus as the Son of God; knowing without obedient works saves no one — actions must align with words.
- **Isa. 58:1** — The 144,000 cry aloud and spare not, giving the loud cry to friends, family, and workplace, warning of the coming no-buy-no-sell mark of the beast.

### The Sealing Is A Settling Into The Truth

- **CET 189** — None receive the seal while a spot or stain remains; we must cleanse the soul-temple, then the latter rain falls as the early rain fell at Pentecost.
- **Rev. 3:17** — We are too easily satisfied, feeling rich and increased with goods, not knowing we are wretched, miserable, poor, blind, and naked — the Laodicean state.
- **Mar 200.2** — The sealing is not a visible mark but a settling into the truth, both intellectually and spiritually, so the people of God cannot be moved.
- **CET 177** — The number of this company had lessened; the careless and indifferent who would not plead and agonize were shaken out and left behind in darkness, their places filled by others taking hold of the truth.

### The Latter Rain And The Loud Cry

- **CET 177** — Those clothed with the armor speak the truth with great power; many bound by relatives are set free, and an angel says the change is "the latter rain, the refreshing from the presence of the Lord, the loud cry of the third angel."
- **Rev. 18:1** — Another angel comes down from heaven with great power, and the earth is lightened with his glory — the source of that great power.
- **Deut. 32:2** — Doctrine drops as the rain; the latter rain is the final outpouring of the message/doctrine that must first be received before it can be given to the world.
- **Rev. 14:9** — The third angel warns with a loud voice that whoever worships the beast and receives his mark shall drink the wine of the wrath of God — this warning is the loud cry of the third angel.
- **UL 218** — Self is the most difficult thing to manage; lay self, temptations, ideas, and feelings at the foot of the cross, then the soul is ready for divine instruction.
- **Jude 24** — He is able to keep you from falling and present you faultless before His glory with exceeding joy.

### The Number Is Literal

- **Rev. 7:4** — "I heard the number of them which were sealed" — Scripture records a literal number, not a title.
- **Judg. 7:7** — Gideon ended with 300 men, a literal number though small.
- **Dan. 7** — The lion's one head, the bear's leadership, the leopard's four heads, and the terrible beast's ten horns with one plucking up others are all literal counts, not spiritualized numbers.
- **EW 15** — The living saints, 144,000 in number, knew and understood the voice while the wicked thought it thunder; they were all sealed and perfectly united, with God, New Jerusalem, and Jesus' new name on their foreheads.
- **CET 13** — "Eight in number" children all converted — Ellen White's plain literal use of a number.
- **CET 20** — "Twelve in number" went down to be baptized — again a literal number, confirming the Bible's consistent literal usage.

### The Largest Body Of United Righteous

- **Gen. 7** — Only eight souls entered the ark out of the world's millions; the 144,000 is by far a larger remnant.
- **1 Kings 19:18 / Rom. 11:4** — In Elijah's day 7,000 had not bowed to Baal; the faithful remnant grows larger across history, and 144,000 is the greatest body of 100% united righteous ever.
- **Rev. 7:4** — The 144,000 are an army; as only men were numbered in Israel, women and children are counted on top of this, as the 5,000 fed were men "beside women and children."

### The Vision Of The Straight And Narrow Path

- **EW 14** — The Advent people travel a path cast high above the world, with a bright light behind them at its beginning which the angel says is the midnight cry; this light shines all along the path so they do not stumble.
- **EW 14** — Those who keep their eyes fixed on Jesus are safe, but those who rashly deny the light behind them lose it, are left in darkness, and fall off the path; the 144,000 are those who do not deny the midnight-cry light.
- **EW 284** — God will not suffer the wicked to slay those expecting translation who refuse the decree and mark of the beast; those who mocked the saints' going up will behold their glorious deliverance.

### Redeemed From Among Men — Translated Alive

- **Rev. 14:1, 4-5** — The 144,000 stand with the Lamb on Mount Zion, His Father's name in their foreheads; not defiled with women, following the Lamb wherever He goes, redeemed from among men, with no guile in their mouths and without fault before the throne.
- **DAR 583.4** — "Redeemed from among men" can apply only to those translated from among the living; the 144,000 are the living saints who will be translated at the second coming of Christ.
- **Phil. 3:11** — Paul labored to attain a resurrection from among the dead — the hope of those who sleep in Jesus — contrasted with the 144,000's redemption from among the living.
- **Heb. 11:5** — By faith Enoch was translated that he should not see death, having the testimony that he pleased God. EP 49.4
- **EP 49.4** — Enoch's godly character represents the holiness required of those redeemed from the earth at Christ's advent; as Enoch was translated, so the living righteous, the 144,000, will be translated before the earth's destruction by fire.
- **1 Cor. 15:51, 52; 1 Thess. 4:16-18** — The promise that the living righteous will be changed and caught up — the translation of the 144,000.
- **Isa. 60:5** — The harvest the 144,000 reap: the abundance of the sea and the forces of the Gentiles converted and gathered in as the great multitude.

### Questions Answered: 144,000 And The Great Multitude

- **Rev. 7:4** — The 144,000 are of the tribes of Israel — modern Adventism, the priests who know the truth and give the loud cry; judgment begins at the house of God.
- **Rev. 7:9** — The great multitude is of all nations, kindreds, peoples, and tongues — a separate, distinct group with different clothing, those coming in through the loud cry.
- **Rev. 14:4** — Not defiled with "women" means not defiled with the doctrines of the other churches, Babylon; the 144,000 have no guile and are not joined to Babylon.
- **Rev. 18:1-4** — The 144,000 give the fourth angel's message — the third angel given with greater power — calling "Come out of her, my people," that they partake not of Babylon's sins.

### Questions Answered: The Secret Rapture

> "The righteous go through the time of trouble; they are not taken away."

- **Luke 17:35-37** — Two are in the field, one taken and one left; when asked "Where, Lord?" Jesus answers "Wheresoever the body is, thither will the eagles be gathered" — the taken are taken away in judgment, not raptured.
- **Matt. 7:24-27** — The house on the sand was taken away by the flood; being "taken" is destruction, not deliverance.
- **Gen. 7** — In Noah's day it was the people outside the ark who were taken away in judgment, while Noah was left safe.
- **Dan. 12:1** — In the time of trouble such as never was, God's people are not removed but pass through it; the righteous are those left, while the wicked are taken in judgment.
- **Ps. 91:7** — A thousand shall fall at thy side and ten thousand at thy right hand, but it shall not come nigh thee — the righteous stand through the time of trouble, not taken away.

## Adventists Are Not Christians — The Final Countdown, Study 18

> What is the image of the beast, and when are we to see it formed? On our timeline of final events the image must be set up before the Sunday law, the mark of the beast, and the no-buy-no-sell — and it is forming right now, under our nose.

### The Second Beast and the Image It Builds

- **Rev. 13:11** — A second beast rises out of the earth with two horns like a lamb but speaks as a dragon: a young, Christian-looking world power that will yet speak with the voice of persecution.
- **Rev. 13:12** — He exercises all the power of the first beast (the papal beast that ruled 538–1798) and causes the earth to worship that beast whose deadly wound was healed.
- **Rev. 13:13** — He does great wonders, even making fire come down from heaven in the sight of men, deceiving the world by real miracles.
- **Rev. 13:14** — By those miracles he tells men to make an image to the beast which had the wound by a sword and did live — there is the image of the beast.
- **Rev. 13:15** — He gives life to the image so that it both speaks and decrees that as many as will not worship it should be killed.

### A Beast Is a Kingdom; an Image Is a Likeness

- **Dan. 7:17, 23** — The four great beasts are four kings; the fourth beast is a fourth kingdom — confirming that in prophecy a beast represents a kingdom.
- **Gen. 1:26** — God made man "in our image, after our likeness"; an image is a likeness, a thing made to look like the original.
- **Ex. 20:4** — "Thou shalt not make unto thee any graven image, or any likeness" — an image is a likeness, so an image to the beast must look like the beast: America will come to resemble the papacy of the Dark Ages.

### What Constitutes the Papacy — and Its Image

> To understand the image of the papacy, we must first understand the papacy itself.

- **Rev. 13:14** — The command to make an image to the beast sends us back to define the beast: the papacy was a church clothed with civil power, an ecclesiastical body with authority to punish all dissenters by confiscation, imprisonment, torture, and death. DAR 546.3 (Uriah Smith, Thoughts on Daniel and the Revelation)
- **Rev. 13:14** — An image of the papacy would be another ecclesiastical body clothed with similar power: let the Protestant churches be clothed with power to define and punish heresy under the penalties of civil law, and we have an exact representation of the papacy in its supremacy. DAR 546.3
- **Rev. 13:14** — The left's opposition to a religious amendment is the very stimulus that drives its friends to secure it; no neutral ground is left, and the government must become either nominally wholly Christian or in reality wholly secular. (Uriah Smith, The Marvel of Nations, 1897, p.229)

### When Is the Image Formed? Church United to State

- **Rev. 13:15** — When the leading churches of the United States, uniting on points of doctrine held in common, influence the state to enforce their decrees and sustain their institutions, then Protestant America will have formed an image of the Roman hierarchy, and civil penalties on dissenters will result. GC 445.1
- **Rev. 13:14** — The image to the beast represents that form of apostate Protestantism developed when the Protestant churches seek the aid of the civil power to enforce their dogmas. GC 445.2
- **Rev. 13:14** — Sunday-reform bodies such as the American Sabbath Union and the Woman's Christian Temperance Union swung into line, the latter adding to its temperance aim "the enthroning of Christ in American politics" to secure a theocracy and better observance of Sunday — papacy over again, called in prophecy an image of the beast. DAR 571.4
- **Rev. 13:14** — National Reformers (Christian nationalists) urge that the organized church itself officially speak "the voice of God in the halls of Congress," for no association is clothed with that authority but the church is divine. DAR 571.5-6 (F. M. Foster, Christian Statesman, October 1892)

### Speaking As a Dragon Today

> "You have no idea the dragon you have awakened." — this is biblical language; the dragon is being awakened to speak, to legislate, and to put the image into effect.

- **Rom. 13** — At the Charlie Kirk memorial the call went out for rulers to "wield the sword for the terror of evil men," appealing to Paul's godly government — but the "evil" are the heretics of differing opinion, and under a Sunday law that terror falls on those who do not worship on Sunday.
- **Rev. 13:15** — Voices now openly say the magistrate may seize the property of "apostate" churches and give it to "the faithful," calling jail or eviction the moderate, soft position — no longer freedom of religion but church-state structure, papacy all over again.

### A Christian Sunday, Never the Seventh Day

- **Rev. 13:15** — Christian-nationalist advocates affirm that Sabbath laws would close businesses "on Sundays," explicitly privileging the Christian holy day — Sunday, not Saturday — as the law of the land. (The word "Sabbath" has been held in U.S. law since the early 1900s to mean Sunday, the first day.)
- **Matt. 7:22-23** — Many will plead, "I prophesied in Thy name," yet hear, "I never knew you; depart from me, ye that work iniquity" — keeping or claiming the Sabbath, loving Jesus, or reading the Bible is not the criterion when one is opposed to the third angel's message.

> Asked which churches may vote in a Christian nation, the advocate answered: Catholics, Orthodox, Baptists, Anglicans, Presbyterians — yes; Mormons, Jehovah's Witnesses, and Seventh-day Adventists — no, "they're a cult." Adventists deceive themselves to think they can walk in agreement with an ideology directly opposed to their message warning against the mark of the beast.

### The Protestant Spirit Already Manifested

- **Rev. 13** — Rome destroyed multitudes she adjudged heretics, and the Protestant church has shown the same spirit: the burning of Michael Servetus at Geneva under Calvin, the oppression of dissenters by the Church of England, the hanging of Quakers and whipping of Baptists by the Puritan fathers — proof that men of strong religious prejudice cannot forbear to coerce, a state of things we now look for under the closing prophecy of chapter 13. DAR 605.1
- **Rev. 13** — Whenever the church has obtained secular power she has used it to punish dissent; Protestant churches that allied with worldly powers persecuted nonconformist ministers with fine, imprisonment, torture, and martyrdom in the 16th and 17th centuries. GC 443.3

### Miracles, the Counterfeit Revival, and the False Fire

- **Rev. 13:14** — Closely tied to the working of miracles is the erection of an image to the beast; the deception wrought by the miracles prepares men to comply with the demand to make the image. DAR 546.2
- **5T 451.1** — When Protestantism reaches across the gulf to grasp the hand of Rome and clasp hands with spiritualism, and the country repudiates every principle of its Constitution as a Protestant and republican government, then we may know the end is near.
- **GC 464.1** — Before God's final judgments there will be among His people such a revival of primitive godliness as has not been seen since apostolic times, and many will separate from the worldly churches to accept the truths proclaimed for the second coming.
- **GC 464.1** — Satan will forestall the genuine revival with a counterfeit, making it appear in deceived churches that God's blessing is poured out, with great seeming religious interest, while the work is of another spirit.
- **2 Thess. 2:9, 10** — The coming of the Lord is preceded by the working of Satan with all power and signs and lying wonders; before the second advent there will be like manifestations of satanic power. GC 553.2
- **Rev. 13:13, 14** — John foretells real miracle-working power — fire down from heaven — and "no mere impostures are here foretold": men are deceived by the miracles Satan's agents truly have power to do, not which they only pretend. GC 553.2
- **Matt. 24:24** — Those who look to miracles as a sign of divine guidance are in grave danger; the enemy works through his agents who have departed from the faith — ex-Adventist evangelists who will seemingly bring fire down from heaven by "lying wonders" to deceive, if possible, the very elect. 19MR 372.2

### The Mark and the Need of an Ark

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — He causes all, small and great, rich and poor, free and bond, to receive a mark in the right hand or forehead, so that no man may buy or sell save he that has the mark, the name, or the number of the beast — the end goal of the image of the beast.
- **CL 9.5** — Again and again the Lord has instructed our people to take their families from the cities into the country where they can raise their own provisions, for the problem of buying and selling will be very serious; we should now get out of the cities into rural districts, free from the interference of enemies. (Letter 5, 1904)

### Preparing the Modern Ark: Land, Water, Wood

> The three pillars of a country property are land, water, and wood — how much, and how to get it.

- **CL 9.5** — Like Noah, who could not buy or sell in the ark and so prepared it to sustain all its life, we need a country home ready to sustain us and those who join us through the loud cry; one acre of garden per person, with separate land for wood and pasture, plus livestock, food preservation, water systems, natural remedies, and education for the no-buy-no-sell years.

### Q&A — The Sunday Law and the Death Decree

- **Rev. 10** — We live in "time no longer"; there are no time prophecies left to tell how long until the Sunday law, only the certainty that it will be pushed by the churches upon the state, and that growing our own food will take years.
- **Matt. 24:20** — The great tribulation of which Jesus spoke was the Dark Ages of 538–1798, not the final beheading some fear; the death decree comes only at the sixth plague.
- **Dan. 12:1** — When the death decree comes, God's people will be delivered; the faithful who are sealed and have not taken the mark are saved and will flee into the wilderness, fed and protected, until deliverance comes.

## No Buy. No Sell. No Escape. — Study 19

### The Mark and the Seal Are the Same Act

> The end-time conflict is about worship. A mark is set in the forehead on both sides — the question is whose mark.

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — A power causes all, small and great, rich and poor, free and bond, to receive a mark in their right hand or forehead; and no man may buy or sell without the mark or name of the beast.
- **Ezek. 9:4** — The Lord sets a mark on the foreheads of those who sigh and cry for the abominations; the word for "mark" is the same word as "seal."
- **Rev. 7:2-8** — Another angel ascends with the seal of the living God, sealing the servants of God in their foreheads — the same forehead sealing as Ezekiel 9.

### The Sun-Worship Abomination Behind Sunday

- **Ezek. 8:12** — The ancients of Israel do abominations in the dark in the chambers of their imagery, saying the Lord sees them not.
- **Ezek. 8:13, 14** — A greater abomination follows: women sit weeping for Tammuz, a pagan sun-deity, at the gate of the Lord's house.
- **Ezek. 8:15, 16** — The greatest abomination: twenty-five men in the inner court, backs to the temple, faces to the east, worshipping the sun — picturing the end-time church facing the sun. Sun-worship passed from paganism (Sol Invictus) into the church under Constantine in A.D. 321 as Dies Solis, the day of the sun, the origin of Sunday.

### The Beast and Its Mark of Authority

- **Rev. 13:1** — The beast rises from the sea with seven heads and ten horns, full of the name of blasphemy — its attributes (deadly wound, worshipped, reigns 42 months, persecutes the saints, removes three kingdoms, thinks to change God's time and law) identify the papacy.
- **Dan. 7:16, 17** — A "beast" in prophecy means a kingdom; so the mark of the beast is the mark of this kingdom-power.
- Identifying the beast as the papacy of 538-1798, we ask the power directly what its mark is. Rome answers: "Sunday is our mark of authority... The church is above the Bible, and this transference of sabbath observance is proof of that fact." (Catholic Record, Sept. 1, 1923); the change from Sabbath to Sunday "was her act... a mark of her ecclesiastical power and authority." (C. F. Thomas, Nov. 11, 1895)
- Rome further confesses you may read the Bible from Genesis to Revelation and find no line authorizing the sanctification of Sunday — the Scriptures enforce Saturday, the day she never sanctifies. (Cardinal Gibbons, _Faith of Our Fathers_)

### Sunday Enforced — The Image and Mark of the Beast

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — Because America causes all to receive the mark and forbids buying and selling, this is the moment the mark of the beast becomes enforced.
- The change of the Sabbath into Sunday is the sign or mark of the authority of the Roman Church — "the mark of the beast." GC 448.1
- Enforcing Sunday-keeping by Protestant churches is an enforcement of the worship of the papacy; in enforcing a religious duty by secular power the churches form an image to the beast, so Sunday enforcement in the United States is an enforcement of the worship of the beast and his image. GC 448.3

### A Conflict Over Worship

- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — The first angel's everlasting gospel calls every nation to fear God, give Him glory, and worship Him that made heaven and earth — worship of the Creator, who set apart the seventh day.
- **Rev. 14:9-11** — The third angel warns that whoever worships the beast and his image and receives his mark drinks the wine of the wrath of God; the mark is a matter of worship, not a barcode, tattoo, or chip.
- **Rev. 14:12** — Here are the saints who keep the commandments of God — all of them, not nine — and the faith of Jesus.

### Two-Horned Beast: Lamb-Like, Speaking as a Dragon

- **Rev. 13:11** — Another beast comes up out of the earth with two lamb-like horns but speaks as a dragon — Protestant America, Christian in appearance yet persecuting in voice.
- **Rev. 13:12** — He exercises all the power of the first beast and causes the earth to worship the first beast whose deadly wound was healed.
- The lamb-like horns and dragon voice point to a contradiction between the nation's professions and practice; its "speaking" is the action of its legislative and judicial authorities, enforcing some observance that is an act of homage to the papacy. GC 442.1
- The present push of "Christian nationalism" — openly calling for Sabbath laws on Sunday and privileging Sunday-keeping Christianity while classing seventh-day keepers as not Christian — is this prophecy unfolding before our eyes.

### The Mark Becomes the Mark Only When Enforced

> "We therefore now call attention to the very important inquiry, What constitutes the mark of the beast?" — Uriah Smith

- **Dan. 7:25** — The little horn (papal power) wages war against God, wears out the saints, and thinks to change times and laws — which must be the laws of the Most High, for only His law touches time (the fourth commandment). DAR 551.1
- The mark and worship of the beast are enforced by the two-horned beast; receiving the mark is a specific act it causes to be done. The third angel's message is sent mercifully in advance to prepare the people for the coming danger. DAR 560.1
- A person receives the mark only by adopting the change knowing it to be the work of the beast and accepting it on that power's authority, in opposition to the requirements of God; thus past Reformers and present sincere Sunday-keepers do not now bear the mark. DAR 560.1

### Two Classes — No Third Option

- **Rev. 15:2, 3** — In the final contest all Christendom divides into two classes only: those who keep the commandments and faith of Jesus, and those who worship the beast and receive his mark; the victors stand on the sea of glass singing the song of Moses and the Lamb. GC 450.1
- **CET 189.1** — Not all who profess to keep the Sabbath will be sealed; many who teach the truth knew the Master's will and every point of faith but had not corresponding works — refusing the seal of God means receiving the mark, with no neutral ground.

### National Apostasy and the Coming No-Buy, No-Sell

- A time is coming when the law of God is made void in our land; the rulers of our nation will by legislative enactments enforce the Sunday law, bringing God's people into great peril, and national apostasy will be followed by national ruin. RH Dec. 18, 1888
- Those keeping God's commandments must bestir themselves and work earnestly to delay as long as possible the threatened calamity; when a Protestant government sacrifices the principles of its Constitution we may plead, "It is time for thee, Lord, to work, for they have made void thy law." RH Dec. 18, 1888

### Out of the Cities — Building the Antitypical Ark

> "In harmony with the light given me, I am urging the people to come out from the great centers of population."

- The Lord urges His people out of the great centers of population; those who remain in the cities unnecessarily do so at the peril of their soul's salvation — not merely martyrdom, but salvation. CL 9.4
- Again and again God instructs His people to take their families away from the cities into the country to raise their own provisions, "for in the future the problem of buying and selling will be a very serious one." CL 9.5
- **Gen. 19** — As Lot was commanded out of Sodom into the mountains and forbidden to flee to the plains, so we must take literally the call to secluded places among the mountains, not spiritualize it away.
- **Jer. / Isa.** — God's people are pictured in the mountains, where the rocks become their munition and defense in the coming second siege.

### Acting in Faith on the Warning

- **Gen. 6-7 (Noah)** — Noah was counted righteous because he saw the warning and built the ark; we build an antitypical ark by leaving the city and preparing, and God's angels guard the ark from those who try to tear it apart.
- **Gen. 41 (Joseph)** — Forewarned of seven years of famine, Joseph did not ask what to do but took action and prepared — our pattern for buying-and-selling prophecy.
- **Matt. 24 (A.D. 70)** — When Jesus warned to flee Jerusalem into the mountains, every believing Christian obeyed though it made no sense; that is our call now.
- The time has come, as God opens the way, for families to move out of the cities; parents should secure a place as their means allow, with land to be cultivated, and separated families may be united in such places — a promise, not a threat. CL 24.2
- Self-sufficiency requires the three pillars — land, water, and wood (about one acre to feed one person for a year; about six cords of wood from six acres of forest to heat a home) — and a return to primitive godliness, knowing the physical Bible without dependence on technology.

## Alert! — Now's Your Chance to Prepare! — Study 20

### The Siege of Jerusalem as Our Pattern

> "When Christ referred to the destruction of Jerusalem, His prophetic words reached beyond that event to the final conflagration." — Christ Triumphant 353.

- **Luke 21:20, 21** — When the disciples saw Jerusalem compassed with armies, they were to flee to the mountains and the dwellers in Judea were to depart out; Christ's sign demanded immediate action. CTr 353.2
- **Luke 21** (whole chapter) — Christ warned His disciples of the destruction of Jerusalem and the temple, an event foretold by Daniel; the entire chapter is a warning to those who live in the last scenes of earth's history. CTr 353.3
- **Luke 21:20, 21** — When the idolatrous Roman standards were set up in the holy ground outside the walls, the followers of Christ were to find safety in flight; the signal must be immediately obeyed without a moment's hesitation, lest they share the general destruction. GC 25.4

### God Makes a Way of Escape

- **Luke 21:20, 21** — After the Romans under Cestius surrounded the city they unexpectedly abandoned the siege when attack seemed certain; the Roman general withdrew without apparent reason because God's merciful providence was directing events for the good of His people. GC 30.2
- **Luke 21:20, 21** — The promised sign had been given; events were so overruled that neither Jews nor Romans hindered the flight, and the Christians escaped unmolested to Pella beyond Jordan. GC 30.2
- **Luke 21:20, 21** — Not one Christian perished in the destruction of Jerusalem, though Josephus records that over 1.1 million people died in the siege. GC 30.2
- **Gen. 7** — As God gave Noah just enough time to finish the ark, so He parts the Red Sea and the Jordan for those willing to go when He says go; the believers who heeded the sign did not hesitate, and God made a way.

### The Warning to Leave the Cities

> "The time is not far distant, when, like the early disciples, we shall be forced to seek a refuge in desolate and solitary places." — Country Living, 1885.

- **Luke 21:20, 21** — As the siege of Jerusalem was the signal for flight to the Judean Christians, so the assumption of power by our nation in a decree enforcing the papal Sabbath will be our warning to leave the large cities for retired homes in secluded places among the mountains. CL 32.1 (1885)
- **Rev. 13:14, 15** — When the leading churches of the United States unite to influence the state to enforce their decrees, Protestant America will have formed an image of the Roman hierarchy, and civil penalties upon dissenters will result; this was the sign to get out of the city. GC 445.1
- **Rev. 13:11-17** — The image to the beast is the form of apostate Protestantism developed when the churches seek the aid of the civil power to enforce their dogmas. GC 445.2
- **Rev. 13** — The Protestant world has set up an idol sabbath where God's Sabbath should be and is treading in the footsteps of the papacy; for this reason God's people must move out of the cities into retired country places where they may cultivate the land and raise their own produce. CL 21.1 (1897)

### The First Siege Has Already Come

- **Dan. 9** (siege of Jerusalem foretold) — AD 66 the first siege of Jerusalem warned the believers to flee to the country and mountains; the AD 70 second siege came with no mercy for those who did not heed.
- **Rev. 13** — The National Sunday-Rest Bill (Blair Bill, Senate Bill 2904, 1888) was the first antitypical siege; the message to flee to the country and secure homes and land has already been given.
- **Ex. 20:8-11** — When the United States government incorporated the fourth commandment in its legislation and deliberately changed its Sabbath to Sunday, it asserted its independence of God in the very likeness of the papacy. _(A.T. Jones, Review and Herald, 6/26/1900)_
- **Ex. 20:8-11** — From 1888 until 1892 the whole National Reform combination tried to get Congress to decide the Sabbath question by law and fix it to Sunday as the American sabbath; the American Sabbath Union, organized in 1888, labored through 1890 for a national Sunday law. _(A.T. Jones, American Sentinel, 3/5/1891)_

### Now Is the Time to Prepare

- **Rev. 13** — Since the first siege is past, we are not waiting for any further sign before entering preparation mode; the next event is the final siege at Sunday law.
- **Ex. 20:8-11** — Get out of the cities as soon as possible and purchase a little piece of land where you can have a garden. CL 17.3 (1903)
- **Ex. 20:8-11** — The time has come when, as God opens the way, families should move out of the cities into the country; the parents should get as suitable a place as their means will allow, with land that may be cultivated. CL 24.2 (1903)

### Judgments Are Coming on the Cities

- **Hag. 2:6, 7** — When the Lord arises to shake terribly the earth He will not cease until His work in punishment is done; the destruction will begin in certain places, will be sudden, and but few will escape.
- **Isa. 26:21** — The wrath of God is preparing to come upon all the cities, not all at once but one after another; if punishment in one city does not bring others to repentance, their time will come too. (probation is still open while repentance is possible)
- **Gen. 19:15-17** — As Lot was commanded to flee Sodom, God's people are told to leave the cities so they are not consumed in the judgments meant for the wicked; to be destroyed in a judgment for the wicked is to be counted with the wicked.
- **Gen. 7:1, 23** — As Noah outside the ark would have perished, so safety is found only inside the antitypical ark of a prepared country home.

### Economic Distress and Civil War

- **James 5:1-4** — In India, China, Russia, and the cities of America thousands are dying of starvation while the moneyed men control the market, buying low and selling high; this means starvation to the poorer classes and will result in a civil war.
- **Dan. 12:1** — There will be a time of trouble such as never was and economic distress like never before; as in the Dust Bowl of the Dirty Thirties, the city dwellers starved while those with farms, root cellars, hand pumps, and draft animals were fed.
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — Before the full no-buy, no-sell decree, years of starvation may drive society to brand dissenters "useless eaters" and cut them wholly off from food, water, heat, and power.

### Preparing the Antitypical Ark

> "Everything has to be in the ark. ... The only access to things we have is in our antitypical ark, in our property, in our mountain home."

- **Gen. 6:14-22** — As Noah had access only to what was within the ark during the flood, our self-sufficient country home must supply everything; this preparation is more serious now than the farming generation of the 1890s, when nearly half the labor force were farmers, ever faced.
- **3 John 1:2** — Because the health message is the right arm of the gospel, the prepared garden must grow not only food but herbs for natural remedies — peppermint, oregano, cayenne, mullein, raspberry leaf.
- **Gen. 1:29** — Survival requires growing the complete package: carbohydrates (wheat, potatoes, corn, squash), fats (sunflower, pumpkin seed, flaxseed, soybean, brassica oils), and proteins (peas, beans, lentils, soybeans). About one acre per person is needed for a year's food.

### Faith, Patience, and Total Surrender

- **1 Cor. 10:13** — God will not suffer us to be tempted above what we are able, but with the temptation will make a way to escape; He does not remove us from the valley of the shadow of death but blesses those who have heeded the warning and reached the ark.
- **Ps. 46:10** — "Be still, and know that I am God"; as Moses spent 40 years shepherding before God said "You're ready," His people must work fervently yet wait patiently, without panic, anxiety, or worry, which spring from a lack of faith.
- **Rev. 18:1-4** — The majority of God's people are still in Babylon, but the fourth angel's loud-cry message calls them to come out of the cities and join those already prepared in the ark.
- **Gen. 22:9-12** — God stopped Abraham only when the knife was descending and his will was fully surrendered; this is how willing we must be to sacrifice everything for the truth and for obedience.

## Country Living Q&A — The Final Countdown, Study 21

### Warned Beforehand, Called to Prepare

> God does not tell us things merely as a tidbit of information; He tells us beforehand so that we will do something about it. Preparation must come before the crisis, not in it.

- **Rev. 13:17** — Because no man will be able to buy or sell, God's people must learn now how to grow food and provide for themselves without fuel, power, or commerce.
- **Gen. 41** — Pharaoh's dream of the seven fat and seven lean cows warned of famine ahead; the seven years of plenty were given for Joseph to prepare. Simple foreknowledge moved him to act.
- **Rev. 13** — As in Joseph's day, the Lord has told us ahead of time what is coming; acting on that warning is both a privilege and a requirement.
- **Gen. 6** — Noah was told a flood was coming, and building the ark filled his whole vision; it took 120 years and he had to travel to gather wood and seed. Preparation is no excuse for compromise.

### Reaping and Threshing Without Machinery

- **Rev. 13:17** — The no-buy, no-sell time demands skill in harvesting grain by hand: a scythe with a cradle lays the heads in one direction, or a hand sickle cuts and binds sheaves to dry in the field.
- **Gen. 41** — Grain is the easiest crop to grow and store; work the ground, broadcast or seed the rows, and leave it untended until harvest, just as the patriarchs laid up grain against famine.

### When to Stop Growing — Fleeing the Country Home

> The time to flee is not ours to decide. God sends His angels; they show us the way, and He feeds us. There is nothing for us to plan or carry.

- **Rev. 18:8** — Her plagues come "in one day" — prophetically one year — so once the plagues fall it is contrary to the Bible to lay up food or plant a garden for the next season.
- **Matt. 24:17, 18** — When the sign comes to flee, we are not even to return to the house for a coat; we simply go, depending wholly on God. _(EW 56.2 — it is contrary to the Bible to make provision for our temporal wants in the time of trouble; food laid up would be taken by violent hands.)_
- **Isa. 33:16** — In flight to the mountains, "his place of defense shall be the munitions of rocks... bread shall be given him; his waters shall be sure" — to prepare otherwise is to disbelieve this promise.
- **Matt. 5** — Jesus drew the crowds out of the cities into the mountains, as in the Sermon on the Mount, to a place where they could think clearly and hear God; God's people are always called to the mountains.

### The Science of the Soil — Learning to Garden

> The city grows lazy people; the discipline learned in labor builds the very stamina needed to search the word and develop Christian character.

- **AH 142.3** — "No one can succeed in agriculture or gardening without attention to the laws involved. The special needs of every variety of plant must be studied... compliance with the laws governing each is the condition of success."

### Faith for the Foreign and Landless

- **Gen. 12** — Abraham was called to move countries; where immigration or finances seem to bar the way, claim the promises, pray as never before, and move forward prayerfully, letting God open and close the doors.

### Holding Property Until the End

> Country property is protected by angels while we remain to grow our food and preach the loud cry; that protection is removed only when we flee.

- **EW 56.2** — The misused saying "houses and lands will be of no use in the time of trouble" speaks of the fleeing time alone, not of selling our country homes now; the weight of evidence forbids abandoning the very preparation God commands.

---

## Country Living Q&A Part 2: Your Questions Answered — Study 22

### Selling Houses and Lands: "Time of Trouble" vs. Now

> A popular but dangerous idea teaches that we should sell our country properties right before Sunday law. The Bible and the Spirit of Prophecy never taught a mass sale of lands; the "time of trouble" texts speak of a later moment.

- **Dan. 12:1** — When Michael stands up, probation has closed; the "time of trouble such as never was" is the period of the seven last plagues, not the Sunday-law crisis. This is when houses and lands are of no use, for the saints must flee before infuriated mobs. (CCh 281; EW 56)
- **Matt. 19:16-22** — The rich young ruler went away sorrowful, loving his possessions more than the souls he could have saved; soon the overflowing scourge sweeps such possessions away. (EW 49.1)
- **Isa.** — In that time of flight the rocks shall be the saints' munitions; we cannot plan to flee but must be ready to leave property at the angels' direction.
- **EW 56** — It is God's will that the saints cut loose from every encumbrance before the time of trouble and lay their property on the altar; He will teach them when to dispose of it so they are free, with no clogs to weigh them down. A country property is not an "encumbrance." (EW 56)
- **EW 57** — God has not required all His people to dispose of property at the same time; He teaches each, in a time of need, when to sell and how much to sell. (EW 57.1)

### Raising Your Own Provisions: Why We Move to the Country

> The whole premise is a coming time when no one can buy or sell. We move now to build the "ark," not to abandon it.

- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell except he that has the mark of the beast; this warning is why we are to raise our own veggies and provisions and live in the country.
- **CL 9.5** — Again and again the Lord has instructed our people to take their families out of the cities into the country where they can raise their own provisions, for the problem of buying and selling will be very serious; get out where you are free from the interference of enemies. (CL 9.5)
- **Heb. 11:7** — By faith Noah, warned of God of things not seen as yet, moved with fear and prepared an ark; so country living is righteousness by faith — we believe and obey before we see the danger.
- **Gen. 41** — Pharaoh's dream of seven fat and seven lean cows carried no command, yet Joseph moved with fear and prepared; the same principle governs our preparation for the coming time of trouble.
- **1 Chron. 12:32** — The children of Issachar had understanding of the times, to know what Israel ought to do; God's people are to understand prophecy so they know what to do now.

### Will Only the Men Be Sealed? (Women, Children, and the 144,000)

> A viewer feared Ezekiel 9 means only marked men survive. The counting of "men" always represents whole households.

- **Ezek. 9:1-6** — The man with the writer's inkhorn sets a mark on the foreheads of those who sigh and cry for the abominations; the destroyers slay all who lack the mark. The mark is set on the faithful, household and all.
- **Ex. 12:37** — About 600,000 men went up out of Egypt "beside children"; the men were counted, but women and children went with them as part of the household.
- **Matt. 14:21** — About 5,000 men ate "beside women and children" — the women and children still ate; the men are simply counted as heads.
- **Rev. 14:1-5** — The 144,000 are an army numbered by its men, yet women and children are reckoned with them; a woman without a faithful husband is still counted, for Christ becomes the head of her household.

### Faith for the Elderly and the Able-Bodied

> No one need fear they cannot make it to the end because of age, infirmity, or circumstance. The duty differs by ability.

- **(Lot)** — As God pulled Lot out of Sodom, He will deliver the elderly and helpless when the cities are to be destroyed if no provision has been made for them; until then, rest in His care.
- **Prov. 29:18 (vision)** — Where there is no vision the people perish; conviction from the Word drove the move and pushed us through every obstacle.
- **Heb. 11:7 (Noah)** — Noah's ark held not only himself but his family and the animals; the able-bodied must build large enough to make a way for those who cannot move themselves.
- **Country Living** — Excuses about children's education are answered: no city education is good enough; if forced to choose between book knowledge and practical country knowledge, "lose the books." (CL)
- **Gen. 41 (Joseph)** — Joseph, country-educated in the Jewish economy, was raised to the highest place in Egypt; God can elevate the faithful without passing them through the world's Babylonian systems.

### Practical Provisions: Water, Food, Fuel, and Seed

> "Provisions" means everything you depend on daily — and most of it comes from off the property. The land must supply heat, water, food, light, seed, fuel, fertilizer, and remedies.

- **Gen. 1:29** — God designed food to grow in the soil; we reject hydroponics as unsustainable and unnatural and grow in healthy soil for greater nutrition. ("of this you may freely eat")
- **(Water)** — Seek a clean spring, well, or rainwater; gravity, sand, and charcoal filters, boiling, or a wood-stove distiller purify water, but arsenic cannot be boiled out, so test your source.
- **(Land needed)** — One acre per person feeds a family (about 1.5 acres rounded down), and one acre regrows one cord of wood per year against a four-to-six-cord annual need, so five acres is too small; see "You Must Watch This Before Sunday Law."
- **(Animal power)** — Without fuel, no tractor can plow enough ground by hand, so oxen or trained animals ("Samson") must do the work, requiring hay, water, and grazing land.
- **(Plant-based fats)** — Grow flax for omega-3, sunflower, pumpkin seeds, brassica oilseeds, and where climate allows olives, walnuts, or hazelnuts; protein comes from dried beans, soy, and broad-bean tofu.
- **(Diet and the Day of Atonement)** — We are in the antitypical day of atonement; animal products excite the animal passions, so a plant-based diet best keeps the mind open to God's voice.

### Inviting Others and Self-Supporting Work in the Country

> Who is safe to bring, and how God's people can stay faithful and self-supporting as ordinary jobs close.

- **Matt. 7:16-20** — By their fruits you shall know them; we cannot read hearts, so invite prayerfully, set clear agreements, and trust God to shut or open the door.
- **(Shake off the dust)** — As Jesus taught, when truth is no longer received, shake the dust from your feet; share the warning with unbelieving family but never compromise truth to hold them.
- **(Forsake family for Christ)** — He that forsakes father, mother, brother, or sister for Christ's name is blessed; unbelieving family must not hold us back from obeying God, yet each must pray when and how to separate.
- **(Home businesses)** — Handyman and carpentry work, snow plowing, market-garden produce, a medical-missionary clinic offering treatments, and excavation are self-supporting trades for the country.

### Time, Tribulation, and the Time of Trouble

> Two distinct events are often confused: the past tribulation of the Dark Ages and the future time of trouble.

- **Matt. 24:21-22, 29** — The "great tribulation" is the 1260 years of papal persecution (538–1798); except those days were shortened no flesh would be saved, for the righteous would have been extinguished. Immediately after, the sun was darkened and the stars fell.
- **Dan. 12:1** — The "time of trouble such as never was" is a future time of worldwide war and the loosing of the four winds, bound up with the day of the Lord and the seven last plagues — a separate event from the tribulation, which ended in 1798.
- **(No date-setting)** — Since 1844 there is no prophetic time to the close of probation; we cannot say the period of no buying and selling will be three-and-a-half or seven years, so we plan to raise provisions year after year.
- **(Move now)** — The out-of-the-cities message has sounded since 1888; the next events are judgments on the cities, so God's people must be out now. The best time to start was twenty years ago; the second best is today.

### Walking by Faith Through Every Step

> Country living is a continual faith walk; each obstacle is a test that changes the character.

- **John 10:27** — "My sheep know My voice"; the great lesson is to learn to hear and follow the Lord's voice now, against the day the 144,000 must hear it under trial.
- **(Build like Noah)** — Noah built for 120 years; we keep building and praying, taking each step the Lord shows, knowing a five-acre starting place is a stepping stone, not the final destination.
- **(Claim the promises)** — Gather the roses and lilies among the thorns; renew the commitment daily — "Lord, today I choose to have faith when I see no possibility" — and move forward listening for His voice.
