## Are You Ready? The Loud Cry of the Third Angel — Study 23

### The Loud Cry Sounds Throughout Scripture

> The loud cry is not a single doctrine or a single experience — the term "a loud voice," "crying aloud," runs all through the Bible, and it must be fully understood in all its facets.

- **Rev. 14:7** — The first angel cries with a loud voice, "Fear God, and give glory to him; for the hour of his judgment is come" — the message is sounded loudly.
- **Rev. 14:9** — The third angel follows, "saying with a loud voice," warning against worshiping the beast, his image, and receiving his mark.
- **Rev. 14:15** — Another angel cries with a loud voice, "Thrust in thy sickle, and reap... for the harvest of the earth is ripe" — proving the loud cry is a harvest message, not a sowing message, and marking when it is finally given.
- **Rev. 18:1-4** — The fourth angel comes down with great power, lightening the earth with his glory (light is truth), and cries mightily with a strong voice, "Babylon the great is fallen"; verse 4 is the final plea, "Come out of her, my people," so as not to receive her plagues.
- **Ezek. 9:1, 4** — The same loud voice cries while a marking and sealing takes place in Jerusalem; God's mark is set only on the men "that sigh and that cry for all the abominations" done in the midst — they see the coming time of trouble and plead that God's people be made ready.

### Before the Loud Cry: A Counterfeit Revival

> "I saw that God has honest children among the nominal Adventists and the fallen churches..." The light will shine, and all who are honest will leave the fallen churches and stand with the remnant.

- **Rev. 13:11** — The second beast with two lamb-like horns that speaks as a dragon is America, which the prophecy gives a play-by-play of in the last events. EW 261.1
- **Rev. 13:15** — America gives life to the image of the beast, causing that as many as would not worship the image should be killed.
- **Rev. 13:16** — All, small and great, are caused to receive a mark in the right hand or forehead — the mark of the beast, which America will enforce.
- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell except he that has the mark or the name of the beast; refusing it means you cannot buy or sell. EW 261.1

> Before the loud cry is given, Satan raises an excitement in the religious bodies so that those who rejected truth may think God is with them — a revival that sounds Christian and uses the Bible, but is not built on truth. We see such a false revival in America now, being pushed toward legislation. EW 261.1

### The Foundations Must Not Be Concealed

- **The third angel's message is to be sounded by God's people and is to swell to the loud cry.** The Lord has a time appointed when He will bind off the work — when the truth proclaimed for these last days goes forth as a witness to all nations, then shall the end come. PM 389.5
- **There must be no concealing of the principles of our faith.** The natural heart brings its own corrupting principles into the work of God; the original founding principles must be brought forth, and those who love the truth will love them. PM 389.5

### The Latter Rain Is Doctrine and Truth

> As the third message swells to a loud cry, the latter rain revives and strengthens God's people to pass through the time of trouble, and their faces shine with the glory of the light which attends the third angel.

- **The fourth angel (Rev. 18:1) is a repeat of the second and third angels** — the closing work attended by great power and glory, the latter rain that strengthens the faithful. 7BC 984.4
- **Deut. 32:2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain, my speech shall distill as the dew" — the latter rain is not merely a euphoric Pentecostal experience but the outpouring of doctrine and truth; we must not spiritualize the word of God.
- **Job 29:22, 23** — "My speech dropped upon them... they opened their mouth wide as for the latter rain" — confirming the rain is the word of God, the doctrine, the truths that are coming.
- **The latter rain revives and strengthens by settling God's people into the truth** so they cannot be moved, intellectually or spiritually — this sealing is being settled into the truth to pass through the time of trouble. 7BC 984.4

### The Third Angel's Message Will Be Called a False Light

- **The third angel's message will not be comprehended;** the light that lightens the earth with its glory will be called a false light by those who refuse to walk in its advancing glory — the light advances, it does not change or become darkness. RH May 27, 1890
- **The work that might have been done will be left undone by the rejecters of truth because of their unbelief** — danger of refusing to look back at our history and original establishment of the angels' messages. RH May 27, 1890

> Read _Waymarks and High Heaps_ by Joseph Bates and _The True Midnight Cry_ by Samuel Snow — they give the history of when the three angels' messages were first proclaimed, which is critical to understanding them. (free ebooks: ebooks.amazingdiscoveries.org)

- **The Laodicean church (Rev. 3) is what composes us now** — thinking they are rich and increased with goods, yet they are miserable, poor, blind, and naked; the wonderful manifestation of God's power will not move those who have not humbled themselves by confession and repentance. RH Dec 23, 1890
- **In the manifestation of that power which lightens the earth with the glory of God** (Rev. 18), the blind will see only something they think dangerous, will brace themselves to resist it, and will oppose the work because the Lord does not work according to their ideas — saying, "should not we know the Spirit of God, when we have been in the work so many years?" RH Dec 23, 1890

### The Sabbath, the Mark, and the Great Final Test

> There are true Christians in every church, not excepting the Roman Catholic communion. None are condemned until they have had the light and seen the obligation of the fourth commandment.

- **The loud cry warns against the worship of the beast and his image;** when the decree enforces the counterfeit Sabbath, the line is clearly drawn between the false and the true, and those who continue in transgression receive the mark of the beast. Ev 234.2
- **Dan. 7:25** — The beast "thinks to change times and laws" of God; the only commandment dealing with time is the fourth, which he attempts to change from the seventh day to the first.
- **Ezek. 8** — Ezekiel sees through the hole in the wall men bowing toward the sun, worshiping in Sunday's manner — the counterfeit Sabbath behind the false revival.

> The seal of God and the mark of the beast may seem a very small distinction, but one promotes the God of heaven who created the Ten Commandments, and the other ignites the false revival. When Christ moved from the holy to the most holy place, Satan stayed back in the holy and breathed his spirit on those who did not follow Christ into the most holy.

### The Loud Cry Proclaimed by Humble Instruments

- **Thus the third angel's message is proclaimed:** the Lord works through humble instruments, qualified rather by the unction of His Spirit than by the training of literary institutions — God teaches the people through simple means like Miller's Bible rules, not requiring a theological degree. 4SP 424.1
- **Men of faith and prayer go forth with holy zeal;** the sins of Babylon, the union of church and state, the inroads of spiritualism, and the rapid progress of the papal power are all unmasked, and the people are stirred. 4SP 424.1

> Miller's 14th rule states we do not need Greek and Hebrew to understand Scripture; the demand for them takes the Bible out of the people's hands and into the leaders' — just as the Catholic Church chained the Bible and kept it in Latin. The Bible was written so a plow boy or a young child could understand it.

- **As the controversy extends and the people are called to God's downtrodden law, Satan is astir;** the power attending the message only maddens those who oppose it, and the church appeals to the strong arm of civil power, with papists and Protestants uniting. GC 607.1
- **As the movement for Sunday enforcement becomes bold, the law is invoked against commandment keepers;** they are threatened with fines and imprisonment and offered positions of influence, yet those arraigned before the courts make a strong vindication of the truth, bringing light to thousands. GC 607.1

### Each One Must Know the Truth for Himself

- **The time will come when we shall be brought before councils and before thousands for His name's sake,** and each one will have to give the reason of his faith — not as a collective, but personally; we cannot rely on a video, a book, or another's experience. RH Dec 18, 1888
- **We need to study the word of God that we may know why we believe the doctrines we advocate** (doctrines, plural) and must critically search the living oracles of Jehovah — to refuse the latter rain (doctrine) when it comes is to refuse the latter rain. RH Dec 18, 1888

### Medical Missionary Work: The Right Arm of the Loud Cry

- **The health reform is as closely related to the third angel's message as the arm to the body,** but the arm cannot take the place of the body; the presentation of health principles must be united with the message proclaimed with a loud cry to the whole world, never independent of it. CM 138.1
- **There must be a well-balanced, symmetrical development of the work in all its parts;** the health books occupy their proper place, but are only one of many lines, for the Lord has sent His message to the world in books containing truth for the last days. CM 138.1

> To do health work without giving the third angel's message is only to make healthy sinners; to evangelize without health is to be a false evangelist. Jesus did both. We eat as we do not only for health but because we live in the day of atonement and the work goes hand in hand with the gospel. All believers are called to give the three angels' messages with the right arm of the gospel.

- **Soon there will be no work done in ministerial lines but medical missionary work;** the work of a minister is to minister on the gospel plan — the last, one-on-one work, the wedge for giving the loud cry, directly connected to country living and getting out of the cities. CH 533.1

### Receiving the Latter Rain (Q&A)

- **AA 54.2** — In the time of the end the efforts of consecrated believers under the Holy Spirit are accompanied by special tokens of divine favor; the outpouring of the Spirit in the days of the apostles was the early or former rain, and the Spirit abides with the true church to the end of time.
- **AA 55.1** — Near the close of earth's harvest a special bestowal of grace is promised; this outpouring is likened to the falling of the latter rain (Zech. 10:1; Joel 2:23), the added power for which Christians are to pray to the Lord of the harvest.
- **TM 507.1** — Unless we are daily advancing in the exemplification of the active Christian virtues, we shall not recognize the manifestations of the Holy Spirit in the latter rain; it may be falling on hearts all around us, but we shall not discern or receive it — there is no contradiction, for the Holy Spirit is what enables us to receive the doctrine and truths of the latter rain.
- **TM 506.2-506.3** — Unless the former rain has done its work, the latter rain can bring no seed to perfection; "first the blade, then the ear" — there must be constant advancement in Christian experience and growth in the doctrine of Christ.

### Do Humans Give the Loud Cry? (Q&A)

- **Rev. 14 (the angels' messages)** — Emphatically yes: every message must have a literal historical fulfillment given by individuals, or it would be an ethereal concept that cannot be pointed to; the first and second angels' messages and the midnight cry had real historical events.
- **The loud cry (fourth angel) is given by people** whom God leads into all truth — God works through people because we need to give and share the message, not because He needs us.

### Will Mainline SDA Speakers Accept the Latter Rain? (Q&A)

> The answer is yes and no. Laodicea is not the liberals — it is those preaching Sunday law and present truth while missing Miller's rules, the foundations, the platform, the principles. RH Dec 23, 1890

- **1SG 75.1** — Those who had no experience in the first and second angels' messages must receive them from those who had the experience and followed down through the messages; as Jesus was crucified, so these messages have been crucified — and those who embrace but a part of the truths connected with the third message must gladly embrace the first, second, and third messages as God gave them, or have no part nor lot in the matter.
- **Lt 28, 1850 (Fairhaven vision)** — At Brother Nichols', the Lord showed Ellen White "that the truth must be made plain upon tables," causing many to decide for the truth by the three angels' messages — the first and second messages made plain on the 1843 chart, the third message placed on a companion chart, together containing the full three angels' messages built on Dan. 2, 7, 8, and 9.
- **2SM 53.4** — Those who look for miracles as a sign of divine guidance are in grave danger of deception; the enemy works through his agents "who have departed from the faith" — former ministers and evangelists — who will seemingly work miracles, even bringing fire out of heaven, to deceive and lead men to take the mark of the beast.

### Has the Call Out of the Fallen Churches Now Come? (Q&A)

- **Rev. 18:1-4** — "Nominal Adventists" means all who look for Christ's coming, not the Seventh-day Adventist church specifically; the call out of Babylon was first given by the second angel in 1843, and the fourth angel now repeats it, beginning to swell into a loud cry as persecution comes. EW 261.1
- **The shaking and sealing prepare a united people to give the loud cry harvest message;** judgment begins at the house of God and then goes to the world, calling all out of Babylon — the fallen churches and the cities — into the antitypical ark of country property, raising our own provisions, so that we need not take the mark of the beast.

## The 3 Stages of the Tribulation — The Final Countdown, Study 24

### Two Different Tribulations Distinguished

> A lot of people talk about the tribulation, but few really understand it. We need to dissect what the Bible actually says — because what's in the Bible is the most important thing.

- **Dan. 12:1** — When Michael (Jesus) stands up, He rises from His intercession; the final time of trouble follows, "such as never was since there was a nation." This is the trouble for the wicked, not for God's people.
- **Matt. 24:21** — The "great tribulation, such as was not since the beginning of the world" is a separate event — the long persecution of God's people, not the final time of trouble.
- **Matt. 24:22** — "Except those days should be shortened, there should no flesh be saved: but for the elect's sake those days shall be shortened" — God cut short the era of persecution.
- **Dan. 7** — The persecuting power reigns for a prophetic 1260 years (3½ prophetic years), the era when pagan and papal Rome killed Christians.
- **Rev. 12; Rev. 13** — The same 1260-year persecution; Revelation 13 frames it as the 42 months of the beast's authority.
- **Matt. 24:29** — "Immediately after the tribulation of those days" the sun is darkened, the moon withholds her light, and the stars fall — proof this tribulation is past, marked by literal heavenly signs.

### The Heavenly Signs That Closed the Persecution

- **Rev. 6:12, 13** — At the sixth seal: a great earthquake, the sun black as sackcloth, the moon as blood, and the stars of heaven falling like untimely figs — the same signs Jesus foretold, marking the end of the persecution era.
- **Rev. 6:12** — The great earthquake fulfilled in the Lisbon earthquake of November 1, 1755 (All Saints' Day), felt across Europe and North Africa, killing about 50,000 — persecution waned thereafter, before 1798.
- **Rev. 6:12; Matt. 24:29** — The sun darkened on May 19, 1780 (the New England Dark Day): unexplained midday darkness lasting into the night, with the moon appearing as blood that night.
- **Rev. 6:13; Matt. 24:29** — The stars fell November 12, 1833, with over 100,000 shooting stars per hour, witnessed across North America. _GC 266.4: "The persecution of the church did not continue throughout the entire period of the 1260 years... Through the influence of the Reformation the persecution was brought to an end prior to 1798."_

### Stage One — No Buy, No Sell and Judgments Begin to Fall

> The first stage brings the "no buy, no sell" against the righteous and destruction of cities upon the wicked. Probation is still open; these judgments are sent to wake people up and call them to repentance, as the plagues did in Egypt.

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — All, small and great, are caused to receive a mark, so that no man may buy or sell except he have the mark, name, or number of the beast.
- **Rev. 13:1, 2** — A beast rises out of the sea, representing the Roman papacy.
- **Dan. 7:16, 17** — A beast in prophecy is a kingdom — the Bible interprets its own symbols.
- **Rev. 13:11** — The second beast, rising out of the earth, is the United States of America.
- **EW 85.2** — "At the commencement of the time of trouble, we were filled with the Holy Ghost as we went forth and proclaimed the Sabbath more fully" — the mark concerns God's time and law, i.e. enforced Sunday worship against the seventh-day Sabbath.
- **EW 85.3** — "The commencement of that time of trouble... does not refer to the time when the plagues shall begin to be poured out, but to a short period just before they are poured out, while Christ is in the sanctuary" — probation is still open, the loud cry is given.

### National Ruin Follows National Apostasy

- **Isa. 1:4** — A sinful nation, laden with iniquity, has forsaken the Lord and provoked the Holy One of Israel — describing America joining church and state.
- **Isa. 1:7** — "Your country is desolate, your cities are burned with fire" — the consequence of the national apostasy.
- **Jer. 6:19** — God brings evil upon this people, "because they have not hearkened unto my words, nor to my law, but rejected it" — judgment for breaking the Sabbath nationally.
- **Luke 21:10, 11** — "Nation shall rise against nation, and kingdom against kingdom," with great earthquakes, famines, pestilences, and fearful signs — a sign of the first stage of the time of trouble.
- **Hos. 8:7** — "They have sown the wind, and they shall reap the whirlwind" — wind is war in Scripture; having sown war, the nation reaps a world war from every direction.
- **Rev. 10** — In the closing scenes "time shall be no longer"; no time prophecy fixes the length of this no-buy-no-sell period. _4SP 410.2: "When Protestant churches shall seek the support of the secular power... then will there be a national apostasy which will end only in national ruin."_

### Destruction of the Cities — Come Out of Them

> Sodom and Gomorrah stand as God's example to the wicked cities of today that make a law against His law.

- **Luke 17:29** — "The same day that Lot went out of Sodom it rained fire and brimstone from heaven, and destroyed them all" — a pattern of the coming destruction; God's people must be out of the cities.
- **2 Pet. 2:6** — Sodom and Gomorrah turned to ashes were made "an ensample unto those that after should live ungodly."
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — "All these things happened unto them for ensamples... written for our admonition, upon whom the ends of the world are come" — these histories warn us of the end.
- **CL 8.3** — "The time is near when large cities will be swept away, and all should be warned of these coming judgments."
- **CL 7.5** — "Calamities will come — calamities most awful, most unexpected; and these destructions will follow one after another" — repentant cities may be spared a time, but the deceived are left to calamity that their senses may be awakened.

### Stage Two — Close of Probation and the Seven Last Plagues

> The second stage: probation closes, the time of trouble of Daniel 12:1 begins, and the plagues fall. This trouble is for the wicked — God's wrath is not on His people.

- **Dan. 12:1** — Michael (Jesus) stands up from His intercession; the time of trouble such as never was begins, "and at that time thy people shall be delivered, every one that shall be found written in the book."
- **Rom. 8:34** — Christ "maketh intercession for us" — sitting equals interceding; when He stands, that intercession ends.
- **Rev. 15:1** — Seven angels have the seven last plagues, "for in them is filled up the wrath of God" — God's wrath is not upon His people.
- **Rev. 16:1, 2** — The vials of God's wrath are poured upon the earth; the first plague falls on the men who have the mark of the beast and worship his image.
- **Joel 2:1** — "Blow ye the trumpet in Zion, and sound an alarm... for the day of the Lord cometh" — the watchman's trumpet signals war.
- **Joel 2:2** — "A day of darkness and of gloominess, a day of clouds and of thick darkness."
- **Joel 2:30, 31** — Wonders in heaven and earth — blood, fire, pillars of smoke from the destroyed cities, the sun darkened and the moon turned to blood before the great and terrible day of the Lord — a third witness to these signs.

### God's People Protected Through the Plagues

- **Dan. 12:1; CET 187.4** — "When this time of trouble comes, every case is decided; there is no longer probation, no longer mercy for the impenitent. The seal of the living God is upon His people."
- **Ps. 91:5, 6** — Be not afraid of the terror by night, the arrow by day, the pestilence in darkness, nor the destruction at noonday.
- **Ps. 91:7** — "A thousand shall fall at thy side, and ten thousand at thy right hand; but it shall not come nigh thee."
- **Ps. 91:8** — "Only with thine eyes shalt thou behold and see the reward of the wicked" — the righteous merely witness the plagues.
- **Ps. 91:9, 10** — Because the Lord is our habitation, "there shall no evil befall thee, neither shall any plague come nigh thy dwelling."
- **Ps. 37:39** — "The salvation of the righteous is of the LORD: he is their strength in the time of trouble."
- **Job 38:23** — The hail is "reserved against the time of trouble, against the day of battle and war."
- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her, my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins, and that ye receive not of her plagues."
- **Rev. 18:6** — God rewards her double according to her works — the persecution she dealt is returned with interest.
- **Rev. 18:11** — "The merchants of the earth shall weep and mourn over her; for no man buyeth their merchandise any more" — her own no-buy-no-sell, doubled back upon her.

### Stage Three — The Time of Jacob's Trouble

> The final stage is the time of Jacob's trouble. A death decree goes forth against God's people, but the Bible assures us they shall be saved out of it.

- **Jer. 30:5-7** — A voice of trembling and fear; men's faces turn pale, "for that day is great, so that none is like it: it is even the time of Jacob's trouble, but he shall be saved out of it."
- **CET 100.3** — The four angels hold the four winds until Jesus' work in the sanctuary is done; then the seven last plagues come. A decree goes forth to slay the saints, who cry day and night for deliverance — and the 144,000 are delivered by the voice of God. _(See Gen. 32.)_
- **GC 648.3** — The righteous "have endured the anguish of the time of Jacob's trouble; they have stood without an intercessor... But they have been delivered, for they have washed their robes... In their mouth was found no guile; for they are without fault before God."
- **Isa. 33:2** — "Lord, be gracious unto us; we have waited for thee: be thou their arm every morning, our salvation also in the time of trouble."
- **Isa. 33:16** — "He shall dwell on high: his place of defence shall be the munitions of rocks: bread shall be given him; his waters shall be sure" — God's people flee to the mountain caves, provided for as Elijah was.
- **1 Kings 18; 1 Kings 19** — Elijah, with a death decree from Jezebel for prophesying against Baal's prophets, was hidden and fed by ravens and angels — an example of God's care in Jacob's trouble.
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — These histories were written as examples "for those who live at the end of the world."
- **Ps. 27:5** — "For in the time of trouble he shall hide me in his pavilion... he shall set me up upon a rock."

### The Final Trouble and the Second Coming

- **GC 614.1** — "When He leaves the sanctuary, darkness covers the inhabitants of the earth. In that fearful time the righteous must live in the sight of a holy God without an intercessor... Satan will then plunge the inhabitants of the earth into one great, final trouble... The whole world will be involved in ruin more terrible than that which came upon Jerusalem of old."
- **Rev. 22:11** — "He that is unjust, let him be unjust still... and he that is righteous, let him be righteous still" — every destiny is fixed at the close of probation.
- **Rev. 22:12** — "Behold, I come quickly; and my reward is with me, to give every man according as his work shall be" — Jesus' second coming ends the whole tribulation.

---

## The Last Warning — Study 25

### The Seven Last Plagues and the Heavenly Sanctuary

> The last message of warning must answer one question: warning for what? If we do not know what we are warning about, we cannot warn anyone.

- **Rev. 15:1, 5, 6, 8** — Seven angels have the seven last plagues that fill up the wrath of God; the heavenly temple is opened, then filled with smoke from God's glory, and no man can enter until the plagues are finished.
- **Ex. 40:33-35** — Moses finished the work, a cloud covered the tabernacle, the glory of the Lord filled it, and Moses could not enter — a type of the close of probation when the heavenly atonement is done.
- **2 Chron. 5:14** — The priests could not stand to minister because the cloud and glory of the Lord filled the house of God; the smoke displays who truly controls the world.

### The Third Angel's Message and the Wine of God's Wrath

- **Rev. 14:9-11** — The third angel cries with a loud voice: whoever worships the beast and receives his mark drinks the wine of the wrath of God — the seven last plagues — undiluted in the cup of His indignation.
- **Rev. 13:11-17** — The warning points back to the beast and its image, whose mark bars men from buying or selling; this is what the third angel warns against.
- **Jer. 25:15-16** — Take the wine cup of His fury and cause all nations to drink it; they drink, are moved, and are mad because of the sword God sends among them — the plagues enrage the wicked rather than bring repentance.
- **Rev. 18:6, 8, 11** — Babylon is rewarded double for her works in the same cup she filled; her plagues come in one day — death, mourning, famine, fire — and the merchants weep, for no man buys their merchandise anymore.
- **Rev. 13:17** — The beast decreed that men could not buy or sell; God answers that it will be doubly worse for those who enforced it — they reap what they sowed unless they repent first.

### The Watchman's Solemn Duty

- **Ezek. 33:1-3** — When God brings the sword upon a land, the people set a watchman; when he sees the sword come he must blow the trumpet and warn the people.
- **Ezek. 33:4-5** — Whoever hears the trumpet and takes no warning bears his own blood; he that takes warning delivers his soul.
- **Ezek. 33:6** — If the watchman sees the sword and blows not the trumpet, the people's blood will be required at the watchman's hand — the responsibility is deadly serious on every side.
- **Ezek. 33:7** — The son of man is set a watchman to the house of Israel, to hear the word at God's mouth and warn the people; everyone who knows the truth shares this obligation.

> "In a special sense Seventh-day Adventists have been set in the world as watchmen and light bearers. To them has been entrusted the last warning for a perishing world... the proclamation of the first, second, and third angels' messages. There is no other work of so great importance. They are to allow nothing else to absorb their attention." 9T 19.1

### Gideon's Three Hundred — The Few Who Will Not Be Afraid

- **Judges 7:3** — "Whosoever is fearful and afraid, let him return"; 22,000 of 32,000 departed, leaving 10,000 — a warning that fear will thin God's army.
- **Judges 7:6-7** — Only 300 lapped water with their hand to their mouth, ready and watching; by these few the Lord said He would save and deliver the Midianites.
- **Judges 7:12** — The Midianites and Amalekites lay in the valley like grasshoppers for multitude, their camels as the sand of the sea — a picture tied to Revelation 9 and Joel. (See GC 334.4)
- **Judges 7:20** — The three companies blew the trumpets, broke the pitchers, held the lamps, and cried, "The sword of the Lord" — a type of the watchmen giving the last warning.
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — These things are written for examples unto the ends of the world; Gideon's 300 typify the select 144,000 group that gives the last warning and is not afraid.

### Blow the Trumpet — The Day of the Lord

- **Joel 2:1-5** — Blow the trumpet in Zion and sound an alarm, for the day of the Lord comes — a day of darkness, gloominess, clouds and thick darkness; this is not the second coming but the day of the plagues (Rev. 16).
- **Joel 2:13-15** — Rend your heart and not your garments and turn to the Lord, for He is gracious and merciful; sanctify a fast and call a solemn assembly — the work of the day of atonement, repenting before probation closes.
- **Amos 3:3, 6-7** — Can two walk together except they be agreed? When a trumpet is blown in the city the people are afraid; surely the Lord reveals His secret to His servants the prophets before the evil comes.
- **Isa. 58:1** — Cry aloud, spare not, lift up thy voice like a trumpet, and show the people their transgression — the watchman who sees clearly must proclaim loudly.
- **1 Cor. 14:8** — If the trumpet gives an uncertain sound, who shall prepare for the battle? A message that says "we cannot know prophecy" leaves the people unready for the war that is coming.

### The Ten Virgins and the Midnight Cry Repeated

- **Matt. 25:4, 6, 7** — The wise took oil in their vessels with their lamps; at midnight the cry was made, "Behold, the bridegroom cometh," and the virgins arose and trimmed their lamps.
- **Dan. 12:1** — The message burning in the virgins' lamps is the time of trouble such as never was — the very warning seen throughout Scripture.

> "When the third angel's message is preached as it should be, power attends its proclamation... I am often referred to the parable of the ten virgins... This parable has been and will be fulfilled to the very letter, for it has a special application to this time." Review and Herald, Aug. 19, 1890, par. 3

- **Dan. 2** — The Advent preachers of 1840-44 illustrated the rise and fall of the four universal kingdoms with a carved image of Nebuchadnezzar's statue, giving the message with power because people could see it; the midnight cry will be repeated in the loud cry "to the very letter." (The Advent Herald, May 29, 1844, p. 135 — Bro. Fitch on Daniel 2, 7, and 8)
- **Dan. 7** — The same prophetic beasts are to be set before the cities in tangible displays, so people can see in person the kingdoms and the warning of the judgments to come.

---

# Judgment Day — Ten-Week Bible Study

## Ten-Week Judgment Day Bible Study — Week 1

> An introduction to the Three Angels' Messages of Revelation 14 — the "Judgment Day" message that began as a movement in the 1840s and is the final warning to go to all the world. This study lays the foundation: how important these messages are, why they must be given in their order, and why we must go back to the experience of those who first gave them.

### Contending for the Faith

- **Jude 1:3** — We are called to contend earnestly for the faith once delivered to the saints; these precious truths were given when the message first began, and we must return to them.

### The First Angel — The Hour of His Judgment Is Come

- **Rev. 14:6** — Another angel flies in the midst of heaven with the everlasting gospel, to be preached to every nation, kindred, tongue, and people; the message is unchanging good news for all the world.
- **Rev. 14:7** — Said with a loud voice: fear God and give glory to Him, for the hour of His judgment is come, and worship the Creator of heaven, earth, sea, and fountains of waters. This is Judgment Day — we must know when this hour opens and closes.

### The Second Angel — Babylon Is Fallen

- **Rev. 14:8** — Another angel announces that Babylon is fallen because she made all nations drink the wine of the wrath of her fornication; we must learn when this fall took place and what this wine is.

### The Third Angel — The Mark of the Beast

- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — A loud voice warns that whoever worships the beast and his image and receives his mark will drink the wine of the wrath of God, poured out without mixture into the cup of His indignation.
- **Rev. 14:11** — The smoke of their torment ascends forever; the worshipers of the beast and those who receive the mark of his name have no rest day nor night. We do not want to be in this group.
- **Rev. 13:17** — The third message reaches back one chapter: those without the mark of the beast will not be able to buy or sell — a warning given ahead so we can prepare.

### The Patient Saints and the 144,000

- **Rev. 14:12** — Here is the patience of the saints, who keep the commandments of God and the faith of Jesus; this is the character these messages produce.
- **Rev. 14:1** — The chapter opens with the 144,000 — a spotless people with no guile whose message to the world is these very Three Angels' Messages; to be part of this group we must understand the message.

### These Messages Are Essential to Salvation

> "We have a most important work to do — the work of proclaiming the third angel's message... All should understand the truths contained in the three messages; for they are essential to salvation." CW 175.3

- The Three Angels' Messages are not optional prophecy study; they are essential to salvation, so all — me, you, and the whole world — must understand them fully and be able to present them. CW 175.3
- The solemn messages given in their order in the Revelation are to occupy the first place in the minds of God's people; nothing else is to be allowed to engross our attention. 8T 302.1

### Given in Their Order — A Perfect Chain of Truth

> "Many who embraced the third message had not had an experience in the two former messages." EW 256.2

- Satan sought to overthrow those who skipped the first two messages, but the third angel was pointing them to the most holy place, and those with experience in the past messages pointed the way into the heavenly sanctuary; many saw the perfect chain of truth and gladly received them in their order. EW 256.2
- These messages are represented as an anchor to the people of God; those who understand and receive them will be kept from being swept away by the many delusions of Satan. EW 256.2
- A true witness must obtain an experience in the first, second, and third angels' messages; the angels symbolize the people of God, enlightened by the Spirit and sanctified through the truth, who proclaim the three messages in their order. LS 429.1
- Sanctification comes through the truth — not through self-effort or flogging ourselves, but through studying and obeying the word. LS 429.1

### The Last Message of Warning and Mercy

- **Rev. 18:1** — A fourth angel comes down from heaven with great power, and the earth is lightened with his glory; this last message of warning and mercy involves you and me. COL 79.3
- **Rev. 18:2-4** — Babylon the great is fallen and become the habitation of devils; the call "Come out of her, my people" warns us not to partake of her sins or receive her plagues — adding detail to the first three messages.
- The parable of the mustard seed reaches its triumphant fulfillment in this last generation: the last message of warning and mercy goes to every nation, kindred, and tongue (Rev. 14:6-14) to take out of them a people for His name (Acts 15:14; Rev. 18:1), and the earth is lightened with His glory. COL 79.3
- The last message of warning and mercy is to lighten the whole earth and reach all classes — rich and poor, high and low — that Christ's house may be filled. COL 228.3

### Receive the Experience From Those Who First Gave It

> "As Jesus was crucified, so I saw that these messages have been crucified." 1SG 75

- Those who had no experience in the first and second angels' messages must receive them from those who had the experience, following down through the messages; those who embrace only part of the truths connected with the third message must gladly embrace the first, second, and third as God gave them, or have no part nor lot in the matter. 1SG 75
- **Jer. 6:16** — Stand in the ways, ask for the old paths, and walk therein; but Israel said, "We will not walk therein." We must not refuse the old paths in favor of an "updated" or "clearer" gospel of our own devising.

### The Revelation of Jesus Christ — Prophecy Is a Verb

- **Rev. 1:1** — The Revelation of Jesus Christ, which God gave to show His servants things which must shortly come to pass — that is, prophecy.
- **Rev. 1:2** — John bore record of the word of God and the testimony of Jesus Christ; if we want a relationship with God we must hear His testimony, found in the book of Revelation.
- **Rev. 1:3** — Blessed are those who read and hear the words of this prophecy and keep them, for the time is at hand; true prophecy is an action that must change our character and our conduct.

### Probation, the Time of Trouble, and Preparation

- **Dan. 12:1** — When Michael (Jesus) stands up from the most holy place, probation closes; a time of trouble such as never was begins, yet His people — everyone found written in the book — shall be delivered.
- The events connected with the close of probation and the work of preparation for the time of trouble are clearly presented, yet multitudes understand them no more than if they had never been revealed; Satan watches to catch away every saving impression, and the time of trouble will find them unready. _(Darkness Before Dawn, p. 36-38)_
- The time of trouble such as never was is soon to open upon us, and we shall need an experience which we do not now possess and which many are too indolent to obtain; the most vivid presentation cannot reach the magnitude of the ordeal, and every soul must stand for himself before God. GC 622
- **Ezek. 14:20** — Though Noah, Daniel, and Job were in the land, they would deliver only their own souls by their righteousness — a righteousness accounted to them through obedience, as Noah obediently prepared the ark for a coming event he was warned of. GC 622

### Led Into All Truth by the Spirit

- **John 16:13** — When the Spirit of truth is come He will guide us into all truth and show us things to come — but only as long as we remain humble and teachable. TM 112.2
- There is need of a much closer study of the word of God; especially should Daniel and the Revelation have attention as never before, calling attention to what the prophets and apostles wrote under inspiration. TM 112.2
- When Daniel and Revelation are better understood, believers will have an entirely different religious experience and be given glimpses of the open gates of heaven that impress the character we must develop to receive the reward of the pure in heart. TM 114.3
- **Rev. 12:6** — A sample of true Bible study: the woman who fled into the wilderness for 1,260 days — what the woman, the wilderness, and the time periods mean — must be something we can search out and explain for ourselves from the Bible.

### The Sealed Book to Be Opened

- **Dan. 12:4** — Daniel was told to shut up the words and seal the book until the time of the end, when many shall run to and fro and knowledge shall be increased; the question for the next study is what event unsealed this book at the time of the end so we could understand these prophecies and the first angel's "hour of his judgment is come."

---

## When Is the Time of the End? — Week 2

### The Three Angels' Messages Are Essential to Salvation

> When the books of Daniel and Revelation are better understood, believers will have an entirely different religious experience. This is the experience the 144,000 need in order to pass through the time of trouble.

- **Rev. 14** — The Three Angels' messages are the final warning given to the world; the whole study walks through this chapter and its messages.
- **Rev. 13:17** — In the coming crisis no man may buy or sell who lacks the mark or name of the beast; the 144,000 of the next chapter refuse that mark, so we must prepare for the no-buy-no-sell time.

  _EGW supports:_ "When the books of Daniel and Revelation are better understood, believers will have an entirely different religious experience." (TM 114.3) — "We have a most important work to do, the work of proclaiming the third angel's message... All should understand the truths contained in the three messages; for they are essential to salvation." (CW 175.3) — "Those who had no experience in the first and second angels' messages must receive them from those who had an experience... As Jesus was crucified, so I saw that these messages have been crucified... those who embrace but a part of the truths connected with the third message must gladly embrace the first, second and third messages as God has given them... or have no part nor lot in the matter." (1SG 75.1)

### The Book Sealed Until the Time of the End

- **Dan. 12:4** — Daniel is told to shut up the words and seal the book "even to the time of the end," when many shall run to and fro and knowledge shall be increased; the prophecies stay sealed until that time.
- **Dan. 12:5, 6** — Two stand on either bank of the river and ask the man clothed in linen, "How long shall it be to the end of these wonders?"
- **Dan. 12:7** — He swears by Him that liveth forever that it shall be "for a time, times, and an half," after which the scattering of the holy people will be finished; this is the prophetic time-period to be unlocked.
- **Dan. 12:8, 9** — Daniel hears but understands not; he is told the words are "closed up and sealed till the time of the end."
- **Dan. 12:10** — Many shall be purified and made white, but none of the wicked shall understand; "the wise shall understand" is a promise to all who ask in humility.

### A Time, Times, and Half a Time — the 1260 Years

- **Rev. 10:1, 2** — A mighty angel (Jesus) comes down from heaven, His feet as pillars of fire, holding in His hand "a little book open"; where Daniel's book was sealed, here at this point in history it is open.
- **Rev. 12:14** — The woman is given two eagle's wings to fly into the wilderness, nourished "for a time, and times, and half a time" — the same prophetic period.
- **Rev. 12:6** — The woman (the true church) flees into the wilderness and is fed there "a thousand two hundred and threescore days"; threescore is sixty, giving 1260.
- **Num. 14:34** — "Each day for a year" establishes the day-for-a-year principle, making the 1260 days into 1260 years.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — "I have appointed thee each day for a year" confirms the day-for-a-year rule that opens the prophetic periods.
- **Dan. 7:25** — The little-horn power speaks great words against the Most High, wears out the saints, and thinks to change times and laws, given into his hand "until a time and times and the dividing of time" — the papal reign of 538 to 1798, exactly 1260 years.

### 1798 — the Wound and the Unsealing

> After the 1260 years, the deadly wound falls on the papacy and the sealed book begins to be opened.

- **Dan. 7:25** — The 1260 years that began in 538 end in 1798, when Berthier took the pope captive (he died in prison), ending papal supremacy.
- **Rev. 13** — The same wounding of the beast appears here as the deadly wound; the "42 months," 1260 days, and three and a half years are all this one period, 538 to 1798.

  _Historical note:_ February 15, 1798 was also William Miller's 16th birthday and the year he obtained the Cruden's Concordance that would unlock the prophecies. "We were interested in seeing his old family Bible, which cost $18.50, and his quarto copy of Cruden's Concordance, which was originally purchased, in 1798, for $8. These two books were almost the only ones he looked at while preparing his lectures." (Memoirs of William Miller, p. 246.2)

### Let the Bible Be Its Own Interpreter — William Miller's Method

> God used the unsealing of the book through a man willing to let the Bible interpret itself.

- **Gen. (study begun in Genesis)** — Miller began with Genesis and read verse by verse, comparing scripture with scripture by aid of the marginal references and the concordance, until each obscure text "ceased to be a difficulty." (GC 320; Memoirs of William Miller, p. 69)
- **Rev. 22:18, 19** — Because one jot or tittle changed could cost one's name in the book of life, the study uses the King James (Textus Receptus) so that every word counts, especially in prophecy.

  _EGW / method notes:_ God led Miller to compare scripture with history and found all fulfilled prophecy "had been fulfilled literally"; the Bible is "a system of revealed truths, so clearly and simply given that the wayfaring man, though a fool, need not err therein." (Memoirs of William Miller, p. 69, 72, 75) — "God sent His angel to move upon the heart of a farmer who had not believed the Bible, to lead him to search the prophecies... The commencement of the chain of truth was given to him, and he was led on to search for link after link, until he looked with wonder and admiration upon the Word of God." (EW 229.1)

### How God Prepared the Man — Plattsburgh and the Year Without a Summer

- **(Deism abandoned)** — At the Battle of Plattsburgh (Sept. 11, 1814) Miller, a captain with 1500 regulars and about 4000 volunteers, saw 15,000 British defeated and concluded the victory was "the work of a mightier power than man," weakening his deistical principles. (Memoirs of William Miller, p. 48-53)
- **(Israel comparison)** — Miller compared the United States to the children of Israel "before whom God drove out the inhabitants of their land," seeing that God is involved in the affairs of nations, as Babylon, Persia, and Greece all happened just as the Bible foretold. (Memoirs of William Miller, p. 48-53)
- **(1816, the Year Without a Summer)** — The eruption of Mount Tambora dropped world temperatures, bringing hard frost every month and crop failures; unable to farm, Miller turned to his Bible and Concordance, the event God used to set him studying the prophecies.

### The Day-for-a-Year Principle and Methodical Study

- **Num. 14:34** — "Forty days, each day for a year" — Miller saw that prophetic days are extended over as many literal years (Memoirs of William Miller, p. 75.2).
- **Ezek. 4:6** (with Ezek. 4:4-6) — "I have appointed thee each day for a year"; Miller cited Num. 14:34 and Ezek. 4:4-6 together as the basis for the day-for-a-year rule.

  _EGW supports:_ "Observe system in the study of the Scriptures in your families... be sure that the soul is fed with the bread of life... It is impossible to estimate the good results of one hour or even half an hour each day devoted in a cheerful, social manner to the word of God. Make the Bible its own expositor, bringing together all that is said concerning a given subject at different times and under varied circumstances." (Review and Herald) — "Those who are engaged in proclaiming the third angel's message are searching the Scriptures upon the same plan that Father Miller adopted," from "Views of the Prophecies and Prophetic Chronology," giving the simple but important rules for Bible study, of which Rule 1 is "Every word must have its proper bearing on the subject presented in the Bible." (Review and Herald)

### The Three (and Fourth) Angels' Messages as Prophecy

> Read as prophecy: each angel is a symbol for a messenger who gives a message, and each message has a literal historical date of fulfillment.

- **Rev. 14:6-12** — The Three Angels' messages span these verses; read as prophecy that foretells when each message will be given to the world.
- **Rev. 14:6** — "I saw another angel fly in the midst of heaven, having the everlasting gospel to preach unto them that dwell on the earth"; the angel is a symbol for a messenger who proclaims "the hour of his judgment is come" to every nation, kindred, tongue, and people.
- **Rev. 13** — The first beast (rising from the sea, seven heads and ten horns) is papal Rome, ruling 42 months; the second beast represents America and brings the mark of the beast, a literal event God's people must prepare for.
- **Rev. 14 (the 144,000)** — Revelation 14 prophesies this group and the succession of messages; the messages come in order — first, second, third — the first given several years before the second, the second a year before the third, with a larger gap before the fourth angel.

  _Note:_ The study taught that the spiritual reign of Christ, a temporal millennium before the end of the world, and the Jews' return are "not sustained by the word of God," being contrary to Miller's literal rules of interpretation. (Memoirs of William Miller, p. 72)

---

## The First Angel's Message — Week 3 (Judgment Day Ten-Week Study)

### William Miller's Method of Bible Study

> "Those who are engaged in proclaiming the third angel's message are searching the Scriptures upon the same plan that Father Miller adopted... we shall all do well to heed the principles set forth." (Review and Herald, Nov. 25, 1884)

- **Rev. 10:9, 10** — Miller took the little book, ate it up: sweet as honey in the mouth, but bitter in the belly; the opened prophecies of Daniel are digested, then come toil and disappointment. The 1844 experience is to be repeated.
- **EW 232** — Angels of God accompanied Miller; a world in wickedness and a cold, worldly church led him to endure toil, privation, and suffering. So it is when one stands for truth: God's word is a sword that separates, so we must stand for truth because it is truth.
- **Gen. 1:1ff** — Miller commenced with Genesis and read verse by verse, proceeding no faster than the meaning unfolded, comparing scripture with scripture. Memoirs of William Miller 69.3.
- **GC 320** — He laid aside all prepossessions and let the Bible be its own interpreter, finding it a system of revealed truths so plain "the wayfaring man, though a fool, need not err therein."

### Rule I — Every Word Must Have Its Proper Bearing

- **Matt. 5:18** — Till heaven and earth pass, not one jot or tittle shall pass from the law; therefore every word, even the smallest, is important and must have its proper bearing on the subject. Miller's Works, Vol. 1, p. 20.

### Rule II — All Scripture Is Necessary and May Be Understood

- **2 Tim. 3:15-17** — All scripture is given by inspiration of God and is profitable for doctrine, reproof, correction, and instruction; it makes us wise unto salvation and thoroughly furnishes the man of God. Understanding comes by diligent study, not by trying harder.

### Rule III — Nothing Revealed Will Be Hid From Those Who Ask in Faith

- **Deut. 29:29** — The secret things belong to God, but the things revealed belong to us and our children; what is in the Bible has been revealed for us to understand.
- **Matt. 21:22** — Whatsoever we ask in prayer, believing, we shall receive; so we may ask God to teach us to understand the prophecies and He will.

### Rule IV — Bring All Scriptures on a Subject Together

- **Luke 24:27** — Beginning at Moses and all the prophets, Christ expounded the things concerning Himself in all the scriptures; doctrine is found by gathering every passage on the subject.
- **Luke 24:44, 45** — All things written in the law of Moses, the prophets, and the Psalms concerning Him must be fulfilled; then He opened their understanding. If a theory has no contradiction, it cannot be in error.

### Rule V — Scripture Must Be Its Own Expositor

- **1 Cor. 2:12-16** — We have received the Spirit of God, speaking not in man's wisdom but as the Holy Ghost teaches, comparing spiritual things with spiritual; the Bible, not a teacher's guess or creed, must be the rule of interpretation.

### Rule VI — The Same Things Are Revealed Again by Different Visions

- **Hosea 12:10** — God has multiplied visions and used similitudes by the prophets; because the same truth is shown more than once, we combine the visions to establish it by two or three witnesses.
- **Hab. 2:2** — Write the vision and make it plain upon tables; prophecy is not meant to be hidden or confusing but revealed and understood.

### Rule VII — Visions Are Always Mentioned as Such

- **Dan. 8:1, 2** — "A vision appeared unto me, even unto me Daniel"; when something is a vision it is named as such, so explanations that are not labeled visions are plain, not figurative.

### Rule VIII — Figures Always Have a Figurative Meaning

- **Rev. 17:1** — The great whore sits upon many waters; "waters" is the figure to be defined. Figures represent future things — mountains meaning governments (Dan. 2:35, 44), beasts meaning kingdoms (Dan. 7:8, 17), waters meaning people (Rev. 17:1, 15).
- **Rev. 17:15** — The angel plainly declares the waters are peoples, multitudes, nations, and tongues; we do not guess the meaning, the Bible interprets itself.

### Rule IX — Parables Are Explained the Same Way as Figures

- **Mark 4:13** — "Know ye not this parable? and how then will ye know all parables?" Understanding one parable, by the subject and the Bible, opens the rest.

### Rule X — Figures May Have More Than One Signification

- **Eccl. 7:14** — "Day" used in an indefinite sense, the first of three figurative meanings of a day.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — "I have appointed thee each day for a year"; the day-for-a-year principle, a definite signification of "day."
- **2 Pet. 3:8** — One day is with the Lord as a thousand years; the third signification — a day for a thousand years. Each must be set in its proper context to harmonize with the Bible.

### Rule XI — If a Word Makes Good Sense as It Stands, Take It Literally

- **Rev. 12:1, 2** — A woman clothed with the sun, the moon under her feet, a crown of twelve stars; this breaks natural law, so symbolism must be present and the figures interpreted to their literal meaning.
- **Rev. 17:3-7** — A woman sitting on a scarlet beast full of names of blasphemy, with seven heads and ten horns ("in the spirit," therefore a vision); breaking natural law, it requires figures — but where a word does no violence to the laws of nature, it must be understood literally, not spiritualized.

### Rule XII — Trace the Figure, Substitute the Explanation

- **Rev. 13:1, 2** — Trace a figure through the Bible and replace it with its explanation: beast for kingdom, lion with eagle's wings for Babylon, bear for Medo-Persia; if it makes good sense with no contradiction, you need look no further.

### Rule XIII — The True Historical Event Must Fulfill Every Word

- **Acts 3:18** — What God showed by the mouth of His prophets He has fulfilled; if every word of a prophecy is literally fulfilled, that history is the true event, for God takes care that history and prophecy agree.
- **Rev. 17:17** — God puts it in their hearts to fulfill His will; prophecy must match history exactly, and if one word lacks fulfillment, look for another event or await its future development.

### Rule XIV — The Most Important Rule: You Must Have Faith

- **Miller's Works, Vol. 1, p. 22** — A faith that requires a sacrifice and would give up the dearest object on earth; trusting that the prophecies, once rightly understood, will surely come to pass.
- **Matt. 10:29, 30** — He who notices the sparrow's fall and numbers the hairs of our head will guard the translation of His word, so that those who trust Him will not err, though they know no Hebrew or Greek.

### Miller's Chronology and the Conclusion of 1843

> "The commencement of the chain of truth was given to him, and he was led on to search for link after link, until he looked with wonder and admiration upon the Word of God." EW 229.1

- **Gen. 6:3** — The 120 years to the flood, a predicted event fulfilled within a given time.
- **Gen. 7:4** — The seven days before the flood, with forty days of predicted rain.
- **Gen. 15:13** — The four hundred years of the sojourn of Abraham's seed.
- **Gen. 40:12-20** — The three days of the butler's and baker's dreams.
- **Gen. 41:28-54** — The seven years of Pharaoh's plenty and famine.
- **Num. 14:34** — The forty years in the wilderness, reckoned a day for a year.
- **1 Kings 17:1** — The three and a half years of famine in Elijah's day.
- **Isa. 7:8** — The sixty-five years to the breaking of Ephraim.
- **Jer. 25:11** — The seventy years' captivity.
- **Dan. 4:13-16** — Nebuchadnezzar's seven times.
- **Dan. 9:24-27** — The seven weeks, threescore and two weeks, and one week — seventy weeks determined upon the Jews, dated from B.C. 457. Memoirs of William Miller 74.2.
- **Dan. 8:14** — The 2300 prophetic days marking the vision from the Persian to the end, all terminating together about A.D. 1843. Memoirs of William Miller 75.1.
- **Dan. 12:11** — The 1335 days reckoned from the taking away of the daily and setting up of the abomination, dated from the rise of the papal supremacy about A.D. 508. Memoirs of William Miller 76.1.

### The First Angel's Message Given in America

- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — "Fear God, and give glory to Him; for the hour of His judgment is come"; the everlasting gospel, not to be changed or said to be in error, had its most direct fulfillment in America and was carried to distant lands. GC 368.1.

### Questions Answered — A Literal Historical Event in Prophecy

- **Rev. 13:1** — A beast rising from the sea with seven heads and ten horns breaks natural law, so it is a figure: the beast is the papal kingdom.
- **Rev. 13:3** — One head wounded to death, the deadly wound healed; the literal historical event is 1798, when the pope was taken captive and died in prison.
- **Rev. 13:5** — Power given to continue forty and two months — 1260 years by the day-for-a-year rule — from A.D. 538 to 1798, a literal historical date proving the prophecy. An interpretation with no exact historical fulfillment is incorrect.

### Questions Answered — The 1290 and 1335 Days of Daniel 12

- **Dan. 12:10** — Many shall be purified and made white; none of the wicked shall understand, but the wise shall understand — a promise to those who seek.
- **Dan. 12:11** — From the taking away of the daily (the word "sacrifice" omitted as a man-added italic) and the setting up of the abomination, 1290 days; dated 508 to 1798.
- **Dan. 12:12** — Blessed is he that waiteth and cometh to the 1335 days; the patience of the saints, reaching from 508 to A.D. 1843.
- **Dan. 12:13** — Daniel shall rest and stand in his lot at the end of the days, proved correct.
- **Dan. 8:12** — The daily is first mentioned here, so understanding the 1290 and 1335 requires going back to Daniel 8; those who gave the judgment-hour cry held the correct view of the daily before 1844. EW 74.2.

### Questions Answered — The Papacy and the Three Players of the Last Days

> "We may have less to say in some lines in regard to the Roman power and the papacy... the human agent is to be kept out of sight, hid in Christ." Ev 577.

- **Rev. 13:11** — After the first beast's deadly wound, the two-horned beast — America — comes prominently on the scene as the present player; its mother, the papacy, "has had her day, and it is past," yet the wound is healed and she will rise again. Spalding-Magan Collection 1.4.
- **Rev. 16:13** — The three players uniting at the end: the dragon (Satan), the beast (the papacy of Revelation 13), and the false prophet (America) form a threefold union.
- **Rev. 17:10** — Seven kings, five fallen, one is, the other not yet come; following the rules and the context of the original scarlet beast, this cannot be stretched to the U.S. presidents from Reagan to Trump.
- **Dan. 9:25** — The 2300 days are anchored to the decree to restore and build Jerusalem; no date is named outright in the Bible, but God ties each prophecy to a literal historical event we can locate and count from.

---

## The Turk in the Bible — Week 4

### The Final Warning and the Personal Duty to Give It

> "Go and tell the world of their danger." This study traces how the first angel's message rose, and our duty to give it as the experience of the pioneers is repeated to the very letter.

- **Rev. 14:6-12** — The Three Angels' Messages are the final warning and last call to the world, given just before and surrounding the close of probation.
- **Matt. 24:48-51** — The evil servant who says his Lord delays His coming and joins the drunken is cut asunder; a warning against presuming the Advent is far off.
- **Ezek. 33:8,9** — Miller felt his burden: if the watchman does not warn the wicked, the wicked dies in his iniquity but his blood is required at the watchman's hand. MWM 92.3
- **Matt. 24** (great image, Dan. 2) — Short reformative literature should move readers from the health message to the second coming. CW 126.1

### William Miller's Rules and His Conclusion

> Those proclaiming the third angel's message search the Scriptures upon the same plan Father Miller adopted in "Views of the Prophecies and Prophetic Chronology."

- **Dan. 8; Dan. 9** — Applying Miller's literal, day-for-year rules to the prophetic periods, all the prophecies terminate together about A.D. 1843. MWM 76.1
- **Rev. 14:7** — This 1843 terminus marks the start of the first angel's message, "the hour of his judgment is come"; the study holds 1843, not 1844, for this point. MWM 76.1

### Miller's Struggle to Go and Tell It to the World

- **Ezek. 33:8,9** — Convinced of a personal duty respecting the nearness of the Advent, Miller heard ringing in his ears, "Go and tell the world of their danger." MWM 92.2, MWM 92.3
- **MWM 92.4** — He struggled nine years longer, presenting Christ's coming only to those circumstance threw in his way, resisting the call to go out as a public teacher at age fifty.
- **MWM 97.2** — One Saturday after breakfast the impression came with more force than ever, "Go and tell it to the world."
- **MWM 97.3** — Overwhelmed, Miller settled into his chair saying, "I can't go, Lord," and "Why not?" seemed to be the response.
- **MWM 97.3** — He covenanted that if he should ever receive an invitation to speak publicly he would go and tell what the Bible says of the Lord's coming, expecting never to be called upon.

### God Holds Miller to His Covenant

- **MWM 98.1** — In about half an hour, before he had left the room, a son of Mr. Guilford of Dresden came asking him to come and speak to the people on the subject of the Lord's coming.
- **MWM 98.1** — Angry at the covenant he had made, Miller rebelled and retired to a grove; impressed with "Will you make a covenant with God, and break it so soon?" he finally submitted and went to Dresden.

### Miller's First Public Labors and the Spreading Message

- **MWM 98.2** — About the first Sabbath in August 1833 Miller delivered his first public lecture on the second Advent; a revival followed in which, of thirteen families, all but two persons were hopefully converted.
- **MWM 99.1** — Elder Fuller of Poultney, Vermont, sent for him to lecture, with similar effect; the straight truth as God gives it has its effect.
- **MWM 144.3** — On Miller's visit to Boston in the winter of 1840, Brother Himes issued the first number of the Signs of the Times on March 20, 1840, an anchor date for understanding present prophecy.

### 1840 — The Advent Conference and the Ottoman Supremacy

- **Dan. (prophetic periods)** — Those looking with intense anxiety for the passing of the Ottoman Supremacy saw the time had come for a body of people to proclaim the message down to the ending of Daniel's vision. Joseph Bates Autobiography 259.5 (AJB 259.5)
- **Joseph Bates Autobiography 259.5** — A call for a Second Advent Conference at Boston came about the time the Ottoman empire lost its supremacy, weeks before news of its fall reached America; from there the work continued until the message ended in autumn 1844.
- **Second Advent Way Marks and High Heaps 50.1** — October 14, 1840, the first Second Advent Conference convened in Boston and sent out its first circular; the flying angel began to move from the same quarter of the globe where, sixty years before, the sun was darkened.
- **Matt. 24; Rev. (signs)** — These heavenly signs—the darkened sun, the moon refusing her light, and the falling of the stars (1833, the year of Miller's first lecture)—mark the nearness of the second coming. Gen. 1:14

### Confused Beliefs and the Need to Build on the Rock

> "There are many in the church who take it for granted that they understand what they believe; but, until controversy arises, they do not know their own weakness."

- **5T 707** — When separated from those of like faith and compelled to stand alone, many are surprised how confused their ideas are; there has been a departure from the living God, putting human in place of divine wisdom.
- **21MR 436.6** — The testing time is right upon us; we must build upon the Rock, present the eternal principles of truth, and show what the Word declares is to take place; the God who gave Daniel instruction will confirm His servants' testimony.
- **21MR 437.1** — All the messages given from 1840 to 1844 are to be made forcible now, for many have lost their bearings; the messages are to go to all the churches.

### Revelation 9 — The Fall of the Ottoman Empire as Fulfilled Prophecy

> "In the year 1840 another remarkable fulfillment of prophecy excited widespread interest." GC 334-335

- **Rev. 9** — Two years before, Josiah Litch published an exposition of Revelation 9 predicting the fall of the Ottoman Empire, calculating it would be overthrown in A.D. 1840, in August. GC 334
- **Rev. 9** — Litch's calculation: allowing the first period of 150 years fulfilled before Deacozes ascended the throne by permission of the Turks, the 391 years and 15 days would end August 11, 1840, when the Ottoman power in Constantinople would be broken. GC 334-335 (Josiah Litch, Signs of the Times, Aug. 1, 1840)
- **Rev. 9** — When it became known, multitudes were convinced of the correctness of the prophetic principles adopted by Miller and his associates, and a wonderful impetus was given to the Advent movement; from 1840 to 1844 the work rapidly extended. GC 335

### The Seven Trumpets and the Three Woes

- **Rev. 8:2** — The seven angels stood before God and were given seven trumpets; in Scripture a trumpet is a loud warning of God's approach and an alarm to war.
- **Rev. 8:13** — An angel flying through the midst of heaven cried with a loud voice, "Woe, woe, woe," each woe belonging to one of the three remaining trumpets—the fifth, sixth, and seventh.
- **Rev. 14:6** — The angel flying in the midst of heaven with the everlasting gospel is the same angel of Revelation 8:13; the three woes are connected to and bolster the Three Angels' Messages.
- **Rev. 14:7** — Saying with a loud voice, "the hour of his judgment is come"; the loud-voiced angel in the midst of heaven links the trumpets to the first angel's message.
- **Rev. 10:7** — When the seventh angel begins to sound, the mystery of God shall be finished; understanding the earlier trumpets fixes our place in this prophetic line.
- **Rev. 10:6** — The seventh angel declares "that there should be time no longer," the close of the prophetic time periods.

### Revelation 9 — The Fifth Trumpet and the First Woe

- **Rev. 9:1-4** — The fifth angel sounded, a star fell, the bottomless pit was opened, and locusts came out of the smoke with scorpion power; they were to hurt only those without the seal of God in their foreheads.
- **Judges 6:5,6** — The Midianites came as grasshoppers for multitude and impoverished Israel; the locust symbol identifies a people of the East.
- **Judges 7:12** — The Midianites, the Amalekites, and the children of the East lay in the valley like grasshoppers for multitude—adding the eastern peoples to the locust symbol.
- **Judges 8:24** — The children of the East had golden earrings "because they were Ishmaelites"; the locusts of Revelation 9 represent the Ishmaelites and people of the East—the Turkish Ottoman power.
- **Rev. 9:10-12** — Their power was to hurt men five months; their king is Abaddon (Hebrew) / Apollyon (Greek); one woe is past, and two woes follow.

### The Prophetic Time Calculation

- **Rev. 13:5** — The beast continued forty and two months; 42 × 30 days = 1260 days, and by the day-for-year principle, 1260 years—the pattern for converting prophetic months.
- **Ezek. 4:6** — The day-for-year principle: each prophetic day equals a literal year.
- **Gen. 7:11; 8:3,4** — Noah's flood account dates the months so that thirty days are reckoned to a biblical month.
- **Rev. 9:15** — The four angels were prepared for an hour, a day, a month, and a year to slay the third part of men; the army of horsemen numbered two hundred thousand thousand, with breastplates of fire and heads as lions.
- **Rev. 9 (five months)** — Five months × 30 days = 150 days, by day-for-year = 150 years; the first period beginning July 27, 1299, when Othman founded the Turkish Ottoman Empire.
- **Rev. 9:15 (hour, day, month, year)** — An hour = 15 days, a day = 1 year, a month = 30 years, a year = 360 years; together 391 years and 15 days, ending August 11, 1840, when the Turk ruled only by permission of the Christian powers. GC 334-335

### Joel and the Locust — A Final Look

> The Book of Joel has everything to do with the time we are living in now.

- **Joel 1:4** — The palmerworm, locust, cankerworm, and caterpillar are stages of the same insect—the locust—again the children of the East, the Ishmaelites, the Ottoman Turkish powers.
- **Joel 1:20; Joel 2:1** — Blow the trumpet in Zion and sound an alarm, for the day of the Lord cometh; the same trumpet-alarm symbolism of Revelation 9.
- **Joel 2:3,4** — A day of darkness, fire devouring before them, their appearance as horses and horsemen—the same symbols used in Revelation 9 to describe this power.

### The Repeated Experience and the Coming Fulfillment

- **Rev. 14** — This is a message for the entire world, not only for Seventh-day Adventists; the everlasting gospel is to be preached to all.
- **Rev. 18** — A fourth angel comes down with a message; just as the Eastern question gave impetus to the first angel, something will again give power to the fourth angel's message.
- **Rev. 9 (unfulfilled portion)** — A next part of the Ottoman prophecy not fulfilled in 1840 is still to be fulfilled; events now happening with Turkey point to its accomplishment.
