## Decoding the 1843 Chart — Week 5

> "I saw that those who had no experience in the first and second angel's message must receive them from those who had an experience and followed down through the messages." We follow the three angels' messages in their order, gaining the experience God's people gained. _SG vol. 1, 75_

### Following the Messages in Their Order

- **Rev. 8:13** — An angel cries "Woe, woe, woe" to the inhabitants of the earth by reason of the three remaining trumpets; the last three trumpets are coupled with the three woes.
- **Rev. 9:15** — The four angels were loosed, prepared for "an hour, and a day, and a month, and a year"; this time-prophecy added up (391 years, 15 days) to the fall of the Ottoman Empire, August 11, 1840.
- **Rev. 9:20, 21** — These trumpet-plagues fell on a system that repented not of its idolatry, murders, sorceries, and fornications — the same power identified later in the book.
- **Rev. 17** — The woman riding the beast, full of blasphemy, is the papal power against which the trumpets served as a defense for God's people.
- **Rev. 14** — Three angels are messengers of the everlasting gospel; when the Ottoman prophecy was fulfilled, the flying messengers began "to make their message tell." When the principles of prophetic interpretation were vindicated, "from 1840 to 1844 the work rapidly extended." _GC 334–335_

### Write the Vision and Make It Plain

> Brethren Fitch and Hale presented the pictorial prophecies of Daniel and John painted on cloth; the conference voted unanimously to lithograph 300 of them — "the '43 charts." _Bates' Autobiography 262.1, 263.1_

- **Hab. 2:2** — "Write the vision, and make it plain upon tables, that he may run that readeth it" — fulfilled when the prophetic chart was prepared and published. _GC 391–392_
- **Hab. 2:1** — The watchman stands upon his tower and watches to see what God will say, and what he shall answer when reproved; the message carries a reproof that must humble us.
- **Hab. 2:3** — "The vision is yet for an appointed time... though it tarry, wait for it; because it will surely come, it will not tarry" — pointing to the appointed time of 1844.
- **Hab. 2:4** — "The just shall live by his faith" — righteousness by faith is bound to believing the vision, even when it seems to tarry.

### The Chart Directed by the Lord

> "As early as 1842 the Spirit of God had moved upon Charles Fitch to devise the prophetic chart, which was generally regarded by Adventists as a fulfillment of the command given by the prophet Habakkuk." _SP vol. 4, 241.2_

- **Hab. 2:2** — The Bible was designed as a guide; matters concerning salvation "were not left involved in mystery... Said the Lord by the prophet Habakkuk, Write the vision, and make it plain." _Darkness Before Dawn 7.5_
- **Hab. 2:2** — "I saw that the truth should be made plain on tables... I saw that the old chart was directed by the Lord, and that not a peg of it should be altered without inspiration." The figures were as God wanted them, and His hand hid a mistake in some of the figures. _1EGWLM 243.2_
- **Hab. 2:2** — "I have seen that the 1843 chart was directed by the hand of the Lord, and that it should not be altered; that the figures were as He wanted them; that His hand was over and hid a mistake in some of the figures." One mistake — not license to cut away the chart's prophecies. _EW 74_

### The Foundation Laid in 1842, 1843, and 1844

> "Our faith in reference to the messages of the first, second, and third angels was correct. The great waymarks we have passed are immovable... These pillars of truth stand firm as the eternal hills." _Evangelism 223_

- **Rev. 14** — The first, second, and third angels' messages are the pillars of truth; God's people are now to fix their eyes on the heavenly sanctuary where our High Priest ministers in the judgment. _Evangelism 223_
- **Jer. 6:16** — "Stand ye in the ways... and ask for the old paths... But they said, We will not walk therein" — men today refuse the foundation laid at the beginning of the work. "That which was truth then, is truth today." _MS 31_

### The Tarrying Time and the Hidden Mistake

> "After the disappointment, this scripture appeared very significant... No one, however, then noticed that an apparent delay in the accomplishment of the vision — a tarrying time — is presented in the same prophecy." _GC 391–392_

- **Matt. 25:1-5** — The ten virgins took their lamps to meet the bridegroom; "while the bridegroom tarried, they all slumbered and slept" — the tarrying time foretold in the parable.
- **Rev. 10:9, 10** — The little book (Daniel) was eaten — sweet as honey in the mouth (the lovely prophecies proven), but bitter in the belly (the spring 1844 disappointment).
- **Rev. 10:11** — "Thou must prophesy again before many peoples, and nations, and tongues, and kings" — prophesy again, not prophesy differently; the message itself was not changed.
- **Hab. 2:3** — "God designed to prove them. His hand covered a mistake in the reckoning of the prophetic periods"; the disappointment was a searching test of the true waiting ones. _EW 235.3_
- **Hab. 2:3** — When the Lord's hand was removed, the mistake was explained: the prophetic periods reached to 1844, and the same evidence that closed them in 1843 proved they would terminate in 1844 — "they discovered a tarrying time." _EW 236_
- **Dan. 8:14** — The mistake was in the starting point: had the decree not gone forth at the beginning of 457 B.C., the 2300 years would not close at the end of 1843, but reach to the fall of 1844. _Life Sketches 58_
- **Dan. 8:14** — "They were correct in their reckoning of the prophetic periods; prophetic time closed in 1844, and Jesus entered the most holy place to cleanse the sanctuary"; their error was thinking the sanctuary was the earth. _EW 243.2_

### Meet the Iceberg — Hold the Foundation

> "The warning has come: Nothing is to be allowed to come in that will disturb the foundation of the faith upon which we have been building ever since the message came in 1842, 1843, and 1844... It is to be as the Rock of Ages." _GC Bulletin_

- **Rev. 17** — The seductive theories that come to undermine the foundation flow from the harlot of Babylon riding the beast.
- **Hab. 2:2** — The instruction to "meet it" — the iceberg of seductive theories — was given in the ship vision; we drive straight into the error with truth, though it shakes the people. _1SM 205.3–206.3_
- **Rev. 14** — "I was shown three steps — the first, second, and third angels' messages... Woe to him who shall move a block or stir a pin of these messages"; the destiny of souls hangs on how they are received. _EW 258.3_
- **Rev. 14** — Individuals approach the immovable platform: some step on rejoicing, others find fault and call it laid wrong; but the faithful glorify God, for He was the Master Builder. _EW 258.3_
- **Hab. 2:2, 3** — The publication of the chart was the united testimony of the original Advent faith and a fulfillment of Habakkuk; "it was necessary that 1843 should be the first published time" so the vision should tarry, and the virgins slumber before the Midnight Cry. _RH Dec. 1850_
- **Hab. 2:3; Dan. 8:14** — The "seeming contradiction" — though it tarry, yet it will not tarry — is explained only by Second Advent history; the appointed time refers to the vision of the 2300 days that seemed to tarry by reaching into 1844. _RH Dec. 1850_
- **Heb. 10:36-39** — "For yet a little while, and he that shall come will come, and will not tarry. Now the just shall live by faith: but if any man draw back, my soul shall have no pleasure in him." _RH Dec. 1850_
- **Matt. 24:48** — In the tarrying, the evil servant says in his heart, "My lord delayeth his coming," and smites his fellow-servants — denying that the fulfillments are fulfillments at all. _RH Dec. 1850_
- **James 5:7-9** — "Be patient therefore, brethren, unto the coming of the Lord... establish your hearts: for the coming of the Lord draweth nigh... behold, the judge standeth before the door." _RH Dec. 1850_
- **Luke 12:35-37** — "Let your loins be girded about, and your lights burning... blessed are those servants, whom the lord when he cometh shall find watching" — to wait implies a passing by of the time. _RH Dec. 1850_
- **Dan. 8:14** — "If the vision did not speak in the autumn of 1844, then it never spoke, and never can speak"; all the evidence that pointed to 1843 proved the days would end in the fall of 1844. _RH Dec. 1850_

### Other Foundation Can No Man Lay

> "When the power of God testifies as to what is truth, that truth is to stand forever as the truth. No after suppositions contrary to the light God has given are to be entertained." _Christ in His Sanctuary 19.1_

- **1 Cor. 3:11** — "Let none seek to tear away the foundations of our faith... Men may suppose... that they can lay a stronger foundation than that which has been laid. But this is a great deception. Other foundation can no man lay than that which has been laid." _8T 297_

## The Fallen Daughters of Babylon — Week 6

### The Second Angel's Message

> "And there followed another angel, saying, Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city, because she made all nations drink of the wine of the wrath of her fornication."

- **Rev. 14:8** — The second angel proclaims the fall of Babylon, the message this study traces to its first historical giving.
- **Rev. 18:2** — The same cry, "Babylon the Great is fallen," repeated in the fourth angel's message that must yet be given.
- **Rev. 18:4** — "Come out of her my people, that ye be not partakers of her sins and that ye receive not of her plagues" — the call addressed to God's people still inside fallen Babylon.
- **Matt. 24:48-51** — The evil servant who says "My lord delayeth his coming" and eats and drinks with the drunken; Christ's warning to those who reject the nearness of His coming.

### Who and What Is Babylon

- **Gen. 10:10; 11:9** — From the marginal readings, Nimrod's kingdom began at Babel, so called because God confounded the language of the builders; the word means confusion. DAR 601.1
- **Rev. 17:5** — Her name on her forehead, "Mystery, Babylon the Great, the Mother of Harlots," reveals her family connections — a mother implies daughters. DAR 601.3
- **Rev. 17** — The woman of chapter 17 is a church; a vile woman represents an impure or apostate church, and Babylon is herself a harlot and the mother of daughters like herself. DAR 603.4
- **Rev. 12** — The woman is a symbol of a church; the pure woman of chapter 12 establishes the symbol that a chaste woman stands for a pure church. DAR 603.4
- **Rev. 18:24** — Babylon must be composed of many bodies; in her is found the blood of prophets and saints, showing she takes in the entire apostate church of the earth. DAR 603.4

> Babylon is the apostate religious world in general, the papal church in particular, or the city of Rome; but it is not confined to the Romish Church, which is only a prominent part of great Babylon. DAR 601.2; DAR 601.3

### Babylon's Three Divisions and the Daughters

- **Rev. 16:13** — Three unclean spirits like frogs come out of the mouth of the dragon, the beast, and the false prophet — the three parts of Babylon packaged together.
- **Rev. 13:1, 2** — The beast with seven heads and ten horns is the papacy (the beast division), already fallen centuries before. DAR 604.1
- **Dan. 2** — The great image of the prophecy used to trace the succession of kingdoms; the chart was built on this and the visions of Daniel and John.
- **Rev. 13:11** — The two-horned beast (lamb-like, speaking as a dragon) is the false prophet, identified with the daughters — Protestant America. DAR 604.1
- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — The mark of the beast: none may buy or sell save he that has the mark, the name, or the number of the beast.

> Paganism is the dragon, the great Romish apostasy is the beast, and the daughters — descendants from that church, including the two-horned beast and the great body of the Protestant churches — are the third division of great Babylon. DAR 604.1

### The Fall Is a Moral Fall

- **Rev. 18:4** — Because the voice still calls "Come out of her, my people" after the fall, the fall cannot be a literal overthrow but a moral one; she still exists to sin and her plagues are still future. DAR 607.2; DAR 612.1
- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — The first angel preaches the everlasting gospel with a loud voice, "Fear God and give glory to him, for the hour of his judgment is come."
- **Rev. 14:8** — Because the judgment-hour message was rejected, the second angel announces the moral fall of the churches; the result of rejecting present truth could not have been different. DAR 612.2

> About fifty thousand severed their connection with the denominations, raising the cry "Babylon is fallen" and "Come out of her, my people." DAR 612.1

### The Churches Testify to Their Own Fall

- **Rev. 14:8** — The churches' own writers verbalized the very words of the second angel's message about their own condition.

> The Christian Palladium, May 15, 1844: lukewarmness, division, anarchy, and desolation are distressing the borders of Zion. DAR 613.1

> The Religious Telescope, 1844: "Has God forgotten to be gracious? or is the door of mercy closed?" — unknowingly describing the shut door of the holy place. DAR 613.2

> Even the Philadelphia Sun, Nov. 11, 1844, published a call to a week of special prayer for the outpouring of the Holy Spirit — yet the Spirit leads into the truth they had rejected. DAR 613.3

> Professor Finney of the Oberlin Evangelist, February 1844: the Protestant churches were apathetic or hostile to nearly all moral reforms, with spiritual apathy almost all-pervading and fearfully deep. DAR 613.4

> The Watchman and Reflector: unparalleled church dissension among the Baptists, dating back to the very midst of the awakening. DAR 614.3

### The Laodicean Crossover and the Original Faith

- **Rev. 2; Rev. 3** — The seven churches in order: Ephesus, Smyrna, Pergamos, Thyatira, Sardis, Philadelphia, Laodicea — the framework for dating the church eras.
- **Rev. 3:4** — "Thou hast a few names even in Sardis which have not defiled their garments" — those few were called out of the Sardis (nominal) church in 1844. By rejecting God's message of time, the nominal churches completed their fall. (S.S. Snow and B. Matthias, 1845)
- **Rev. 2; Rev. 3** — The Philadelphia church (Brotherly Love) was made up of those who came out of Sardis in 1844 under the cry "Babylon is fallen"; the Laodicean church is the next and last stage. (Hiram Edson)
- **Rev. 3:14-20** — Laodicea is lukewarm, saying "I am rich, and increased with goods, and have need of nothing," not knowing she is wretched, miserable, poor, blind, and naked; counseled to buy gold, white raiment, and eyesalve.
- **Hab. 2:1-4** — "Write the vision, and make it plain upon tables, that he may run that readeth it"; the vision will not lie though it tarry. The 1843 prophetic chart fulfilled this command. 4SP 241.2

> "God is not giving us a new message. We are to proclaim the message that in 1843 and 1844 brought us out of the other churches." RH Jan. 19, 1905, par. 22

### Rejecting the First Angel Brought the Fall

- **Ev 197.2** — The vision Christ presented to John — the commandments of God and the faith of Jesus — is to be proclaimed to all nations; the churches represented by Babylon fell to become a persecuting power with horns like a lamb but speaking like a dragon.
- **4SP 230.1** — The churches that refused the first angel's message rejected light sent in mercy to arouse them from worldliness and backsliding and to keep them separate from the world.
- **4SP 231.1** — The first angel's message was given to separate the church of Christ from the corrupting influence of the world, but the multitude chose worldly wisdom over the heart-searching message of truth.
- **4SP 231.3** — As the antediluvians who rejected Noah's words found the Spirit ceasing to strive, so when men despise God's warnings His Spirit ceases to arouse conviction; when the Spirit ceases to impress truth, all hearing and all preaching is vain.
- **4SP 232.1** — When the churches spurned God's counsel by rejecting the Advent message, the Lord rejected them; the second angel proclaimed Babylon fallen, and in the summer of 1844 about fifty thousand withdrew.
- **4SP 232.2** — The message of Babylon's fall must apply to a body once pure now corrupt — fitting not the Romish Church but the Protestant churches, divided into innumerable conflicting creeds where God is not, the work of man and Satan.
- **GC 380.2** — In refusing the first angel's warning, they rejected the means of restoration and turned to the friendship of the world — the cause of the worldliness, backsliding, and spiritual death in the churches in 1844.

### Charles Fitch and the Cry in 1843

- **Rev. 14:8** — The message "Babylon is fallen" began to be introduced in 1843, requiring a literal historical fulfillment; the first message ("the hour of his judgment is come") came in 1837, so the second had to follow. (Second Advent Way Marks and High Heaps 64.3)
- **Rev. 18:1-4** — "Come out of her, my people" — the sermon by Charles Fitch in 1843 that gave this specific cry; he was the messenger God sent forth with the second angel's message. (Way Marks and High Heaps 64.3)

> The August 11, 1840 fall of the Ottoman Empire (calculated from Rev. 9) gave power to the judgment-hour message and proved Miller's rules of interpretation; "the Bible and history doth agree."

### The Midnight Cry and the 144,000

- **Christian Experience and Teachings 50, 51** — Hope centered on the coming of the Lord in 1844; the second angel's message was first proclaimed by the servants of God that summer, and in connection with it the midnight cry, "Behold, the Bridegroom cometh," aroused thousands from city to city.
- **Rev. 16:15** — "Behold, I come as a thief; blessed is he that watcheth, and keepeth his garments" — the watchman who keeps the garments of Christ's righteousness lest he walk naked into Armageddon.
- **Rev. 7; Rev. 14** — The 144,000 are found in both chapters; they are the sealed people, and Revelation 14 opens with them before flowing into their message, the three angels' messages of 1844.

> The Seventh-day Adventist Church is not Babylon — "if you are teaching that the Seventh-day Adventist Church is Babylon, you are wrong. God has not given you any such message to bear." Satan inspires false theories to divert souls from the true issue.

## The True Midnight Cry — Week 7

_Series: Judgment Day Ten-Week Study. The Midnight Cry was the message "Behold, the Bridegroom cometh," given at the prophetic midnight of the Second Advent movement. To deny this light from behind is to lose sight of Jesus and fall from the path._

### The Second Angel and the Midnight Cry

> "In connection with the message, the midnight cry was given." The fall of Babylon and the Midnight Cry cannot be separated — the second angel's message carries the cry that gives it added power.

- **Rev. 3:4** — Babylon's churches had "a few names even in Sardis" who had not defiled their garments; it was time to call them out of the corrupt mass. JUBST June 19, 1845, p.117.2 (S.S. Snow and B. Matthias).
- **Rev. 18:2** — In 1843 God sent forth the cry, "Babylon is fallen, and is become the habitation of devils, and the hold of every foul spirit, and a cage of every unclean and hateful bird. Come out of her, my people." First given in 1843, fulfilled in the summer of 1844.
- **Rev. 14:8** — The second angel "cried, Babylon is fallen, is fallen, that great city"; first proclaimed in the summer of 1844, and many left the fallen churches. CET 50, 51.

### The First Disappointment — Spring of 1844

> Miller and his associates first looked to the spring of 1844, but the prophecy pointed to the autumn. Understanding Bible reckoning — that the Jewish year begins in the spring with the month Abib — resolves the apparent discrepancy.

- **Dan. 8:14** — The 2300 days terminated in the year 1844; the misapprehension that put the end in the spring brought disappointment, but did not weaken the argument. CIHS 86.2.
- **Ex. 12:1-6** — Scripture testimony was clear that every year commenced with the new moon in the spring, just fourteen days before the yearly Passover. The Autobiography of Elder Joseph Bates, p.293.4.
- **Ex. 13:3-4** — The same testimony fixed the 17th day of April, 1844, Roman time, as the close of the year 1843, Bible time; so the first disappointment of "1843" actually fell in the spring of 1844. The Autobiography of Elder Joseph Bates, p.293.4.

### The Tarrying Time

> Down to April 1844 the churches were open to the Advent message; after the spring disappointment, opposition arose, and the believers entered the tarrying time — a season of doubt prophesied to precede the cry.

- **Matt. 25:5** — "While the bridegroom tarried, they all slumbered and slept." The disappointment threw the waiting ones into doubt and uncertainty; the bridegroom "tarried" past the first point of expectation. DAR 593.2.
- **Hab. 2:1-4** — "Write the vision, and make it plain upon tables... though it tarry, wait for it; because it will surely come, it will not tarry." The 1843 chart was the writing of this vision, and the tarrying time was foretold in it.
- **Christ in His Sanctuary p.121, 122** — Interwoven with the prophecies was instruction "specially adapted to their state of uncertainty," encouraging them to wait patiently; what was dark to their understanding would in due time be made plain.

### The Cry Made at Midnight

> "At midnight there was a cry made." Just such a cry as the parable foretold was suddenly and involuntarily raised, midway between the spring of 1844 and the autumn to which the 2300 days really extended.

- **Matt. 25:6** — "And at midnight there was a cry made, Behold, the bridegroom cometh; go ye out to meet him." The very phraseology was adopted involuntarily, in fulfillment of prophecy. DAR 593.2.
- **Matt. 25:7** — "Then all those virgins arose, and trimmed their lamps" — exactly what the believers did during the first disappointment and the tarrying time, making their light shine brighter.
- **Matt. 25:9-13** — The wise sent the foolish to "buy for yourselves"; the message must be bought anew. "Watch therefore, for ye know neither the day nor the hour."
- **Ezek. 4:6** — The day-for-a-year principle: the great prophetic period of 2300 days meant 2300 years. The cause of the sudden arousing was the discovery that this period extended to the autumn, not the spring, of 1844. DAR 593.2.

### The Light From Behind — Ellen White's First Vision

> "Near the close of the second angel's message I saw a great light from heaven." The Midnight Cry was a bright light set up behind the Advent people, lighting their feet so they would not stumble.

- **Early Writings p.237, 238** — As the churches refused the first angel's message they fell from God's favor; but the oppressed who accepted "Babylon is fallen" heard the voices of angels crying, "Behold, the Bridegroom cometh; go ye out to meet Him."
- **Christian Experience and Teachings p.57** — "It was not long after the passing of the time, in 1844, that my first vision was given me," while five women knelt in prayer at Portland.
- **Early Writings p.14.1** — On a straight and narrow path "cast up high above the world" the Advent people traveled to the city, with a bright light set behind them at the beginning of the path, which an angel said was the Midnight Cry; those who denied that light went out into perfect darkness and fell from the path.

### Snow's Midnight Cry at the Exeter Camp Meeting

> At the Exeter, New Hampshire camp meeting in the summer of 1844, S. S. Snow gave the path of his Midnight Cry message; the power of God swept the camp.

- **The Great Second Advent Movement p.522, 523** — On Sunday forenoon, while Joseph Bates preached, a man rode in at full speed, sat by the family of Elder John Couch, and whispered the cause of the disappointment and "the midnight cry that was now due."
- **The Great Second Advent Movement p.522, 523** — Sister Couch arose and said, "here is a man who has light on the midnight cry"; Bates replied, "then let him come up here on the platform, and give it to the people."
- **The Great Second Advent Movement p.522, 523** — The minister who walked into the stand was S. S. Snow; "as he uttered those words the mighty power of God swept over that camp." The Midnight Cry paper, edited by Brother Southard, "swept over the land with the velocity of a tornado... God was in it."
- **The Great Second Advent Movement p.522, 523** — Snow questioned the people: a day in prophecy is a year, a night is six months, midnight is three months, and they had been in the tarrying time just three months — "Then it is just the midnight now, and I am here with the midnight cry."
- **Dan. 8:14** — Snow showed that the decree went forth in the fall of 457 B.C., so the 2300 days would terminate Oct. 22, 1844, "on the tenth day of the seventh month," not in the spring as previously supposed. The Great Second Advent Movement p.522, 523.

### The 1843 Chart and the Original Faith

> The publication of the 1843 chart was itself a fulfillment of Habakkuk's "write the vision," showing the tarrying time and dating the 2300 days from 457 B.C.

- **Hab. 2:2, 3** — The chart published by J. V. Himes was "a fulfillment of Hab. 2"; to deny that the chart was a subject of prophecy is to leave the original faith, from which it follows that B.C. 457 was the year to date the 2300 days. Advent Review and Sabbath Herald, December 1850, p.13.6.

### Q&A — Fleeing to the Mountains

> Asked whether the move to the mountains is literal: by Miller's rules, if a thing does not break natural law, take it literally — and God's people have always found refuge in the mountains.

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — "No man might buy or sell, save he that had the mark... of the beast." Refusing the mark and being unable to buy or sell breaks no natural law, so it is literal.
- **Isa. 33:16** — "He shall dwell on high: his place of defense shall be the munitions of rocks... bread shall be given him; his waters shall be sure." Literal mountains, literal bread and water, secure during the time of trouble.
- **Dan. 12:1** — "At that time shall Michael stand up... and there shall be a time of trouble such as never was"; the people written in the book are delivered and provided for after the close of probation.
- **Country Living (Testimonies for the Church)** — "The time is not far distant when, like the early disciples, we shall be forced to seek a refuge in desolate and solitary places... it will then be time to leave the large cities... for retired homes in secluded places among the mountains."

### Q&A — The King of the South and End-Time Typology

> By Miller's rules Daniel 11 is a literal prophecy; if the king of the north is literal, the king of the south must be literal too. The Old Testament histories are written for our admonition.

- **Dan. 11:40-45** — Read literally, the king of the south is literal Egypt, abundantly outlined in Uriah Smith's "Daniel and the Revelation." Spiritualizing the prophecy makes it dark; the literal method makes it plain (DAR Preface).
- **Ezek. 38, 39** — "Set thy face against Gog and the land of Magog"; in the latter years the people are gathered against the mountains of Israel, and "in that day there shall be a great shaking in the land of Israel."
- **1 Cor. 10:11** — "Now all these things happened unto them for ensamples... written for our admonition, upon whom the ends of the world are come"; the scattering of Israel typifies our time.
- **Luke 21:12** — "They shall lay their hands on you... being brought before kings and rulers for my name's sake" — why we must learn to defend our faith as watchmen.
- **Rev. 1:1** — "The revelation of Jesus Christ, which God gave unto him, to show unto his servants things which must shortly come to pass" — God reveals prophecy so we will be ready and not taken as a thief.

### Q&A — The Foundations of 1840 to 1844

> The messages given from 1840 to 1844 are the truth, laid as a foundation that is not to be disturbed.

- **Manuscript Releases vol. 21, p.437.1** — "All the messages given from 1840 to 1844 are to be made forcible now; for there are many who have lost their bearings. The messages are to go to all the churches."
- **General Conference Bulletin April 6, 1903, par. 35** — "Nothing is to be allowed to come in that will disturb the foundation of the faith upon which we have been building ever since the message came in 1842, 1843, and 1844... It is to be as the Rock of Ages." (Compiled in the 1842 chart vote that produced the 1843 chart.)

## October 22 — Real or Mythical? — Week 8

_Judgment Day Ten-Week Study_

### The Seventh Trumpet and the Mystery of God

> A message based on time: in the days of the seventh angel's voice, the mystery of God — the time prophecies given to the prophets — is finished, only days before the judgment of the dead begins.

- **Rev. 10:5, 6** — The angel standing on sea and earth swears that "there should be time no longer" — meaning prophetic time, not the end of the world. This sets up our first question: what is "time no longer"?
- **Rev. 10:7-9** — In the days of the seventh angel's voice the mystery of God is finished; the prophet eats the little book, sweet in the mouth but bitter in the belly — the disappointment that follows the message.
- **Dan. 12:4** — The little book is the sealed book of Daniel, shut up "till the time of the end."
- **Rev. 10:2** — The same little book is now seen open in the angel's hand, proving we have reached the time of the end.
- **Rev. 11:15, 18** — When the seventh angel sounded, the kingdoms became the Lord's and the "time of the dead, that they should be judged" began — so the seventh trumpet sounds shortly before the judgment of the dead.

### Judgment Takes Place in the Sanctuary

- **Isa. 16:5** — The throne is established in mercy "in the tabernacle of David"; the word tabernacle means sanctuary, so judgment and righteousness take place inside the sanctuary.
- **Heb. 8:5** — The earthly sanctuary served as the "example and shadow of heavenly things," a pattern of the real Sanctuary in heaven where Christ now ministers as High Priest.
- **Lev. 16:15-17** — The high priest brings the blood within the veil and makes an atonement for the holy place because of the uncleanness of Israel — the cleansing the judgment requires.
- **Ex. 30:10** — Aaron makes atonement upon the horns of the altar "once in a year" — the Day of Atonement comes around once a year.
- **Lev. 23:27** — That day is fixed: "the tenth day of this seventh month there shall be a day of atonement." Mark the 10th day of the seventh month — it is critical to October 22.

### Daniel 8:14 and Why We Must Prove the Date

> We have become so Laodicean we cannot even prove our most fundamental prophecy. This is a humbling message: many talk of the 2300 days yet cannot show how to reach October 22.

- **Dan. 8:14** — After 2300 days the sanctuary shall be cleansed — the foundation prophecy of the cleansing.
- **Eze. 4:6** — "Each day for a year": the 2300 days are 2300 years, reaching to the autumn of 1844 and the antitypical Day of Atonement.

### The Latter Rain Is the Doctrine — Don't Reject the Light Behind

> The latter rain may be falling on hearts all around us, but the blind will not discern or receive it.

- **Deut. 32:2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain... as the small rain upon the tender herb" — the latter rain is doctrine, the foundational truths of the fourth angel's message, not merely an emotional outpouring.
- _Supporting: "We shall not recognize the manifestations of the Holy Spirit in the latter rain. It may be falling on hearts all around us, but we shall not discern or receive it." TM 507.1_
- _Supporting: Those who have not humbled themselves "will see only something which in their blindness they think dangerous... and they will brace themselves to resist it... 'should we not know the Spirit of God, when we have been in the work so many years?'" Mar 219.2_

### Reckoning Biblical Time — New Moons and the Thirty-Day Month

- **Isa. 66:23** — "From one new moon to another, and from one sabbath to another" — a month is reckoned from new moon to new moon.
- **Gen. 7:11; 8:2-4** — The flood from the 17th day of the second month to the 17th day of the seventh month is 150 days over five months — 150 divided by 5 gives 30 days to a biblical month.
- **Gen. 1:14** — God set lights in the firmament "for signs, and for seasons, and for days, and years" — the moon marks biblical time.

### Finding the First Month by the Barley Harvest

> To find the seventh month you must first find the first month, then simply count to seven.

- **Matt. 24:32** — The parable of the fig tree teaches us to tell the time of year by the growth of vegetation — "when his branch is yet tender, and putteth forth leaves."
- **Ex. 12:1, 2** — "This month shall be unto you the beginning of months... the first month of the year" — fixed at the time Israel left Egypt.
- **Ex. 9:28-32** — The hail plague struck "the flax and the barley... for the barley was in the ear," while wheat and rye were not grown up — the first month is when barley is ripe.
- **Ex. 23:15** — The Feast of Unleavened Bread is kept "in the month Abib," the first month, "for in it thou camest out from Egypt."
- **Lev. 23:5, 6, 9-11** — The Passover on the 14th, Unleavened Bread on the 15th of the first month, and the wave sheaf of firstfruits — all anchored to the ripe barley harvest.
- _Supporting: The Israel Agricultural Ministry's barley letter places the barley harvest in "the first and second week of April," so the first biblical month (Abib) is April._
- **Joel 2:23** — God gives "the former rain, and the latter rain in the first month" — the latter rain ripens this first-month harvest of barley.

### Counting to October 22 — The Trumpet of the First Day

- **Lev. 23:27** — Again: the Day of Atonement is the 10th day of the seventh month. With April as the first month, count April, May, June, July, August, September — October is the seventh month.
- **Lev. 23:24** — The first day of the seventh month is "a memorial of blowing of trumpets" — so ten days before the Day of Atonement a trumpet must blow.
- **Rev. 10:5-7** — Tying it together: the seventh angel's voice (the trumpet of the first day) sounds, and the mystery of God — the time prophecy — is finished only days before the judgment.
- **Rev. 8:2** — The seven angels stand before God and are given seven trumpets — the seventh angel of Revelation 10 and 11 is the seventh trumpet.
- _The Caraite Jews begin the year with the new moon in April (as the Bible teaches), while the Rabbinical mode wrongly begins it in March. If March were correct, the seventh month would not be October and October 22 could not stand. ("The True Midnight Cry," Aug. 22, 1844, p. 2.3)_

### The Historic Proclamation of October 22, 1844

> They could not proclaim the date until they saw the new moon with the eye — only then was the mystery solved.

- **Gen. 1:5** — "The evening and the morning were the first day" — the day begins at evening, so the new moon is reckoned from the start of the next day.
- **Rev. 10:5-7** — In October 1844 the astronomical new moon fell on the 11th, but the visible sliver appeared after sunset on the 12th — the biblical 13th, the first day of the seventh month. Counting ten days inclusively (13th through the 22nd) reaches October 22, the Day of Atonement, when the seventh trumpet began to sound and the mystery was finished.
- _Supporting (on-screen slide): "These fulfillments are all perfectly plain. The first message, based on time, has been given." (Review and Herald, July 22, 1852)_
- _Supporting: "This time, which the angel declares with a solemn oath... is of prophetic time, which would precede the advent of our Lord... After this period of time, reaching from 1842 to 1844, there can be no definite tracing of the prophetic time. The longest reckoning reaches to the autumn of 1844." CTr 344.5_

### The Dry Bones — Waking God's Sleeping People

> The poor, blind, and naked Laodiceans are the dry bones; the doctrine of the prophecies is the rain that wets them and brings them to life.

- **Eze. 37:1-10** — The valley of very dry bones is prophesied to twice — first bone comes to bone with a shaking, then breath comes from the four winds — and they live as "an exceeding great army," the 144,000 who accept the light from behind, the prophecies, the latter rain.
- **Deut. 32:2** — The rain that wets the dry bones is doctrine — the foundation that gives them life.

### Q&A — The 6,000 Years and the Three Woes

- **Rev. 10:6** — On the 6,000 years: the speaker affirms the earth's 6,000-year span but holds it has already ended, since "time no longer" forbids any prophetic time running past 1844; to extend it contradicts Revelation 10:6.
- **Rev. 8:13** — The three woes belong to the fifth, sixth, and seventh trumpets; the second woe (the Eastern question) is already past, and the third woe — still future — is connected to the four winds and the seven last plagues.
- **Rev. 14:6** — The "another angel" of Revelation 8:13 is the same angel flying in the midst of heaven who gives the three angels' messages, binding the trumpets to the three angels' messages and to the loud cry.

## Ellen White's Prophetic Charts — Week 9

### The Light From Behind

> The light is truth, which an angel said was the Midnight Cry. Those who denied the light behind them lost sight of the mark and of Jesus and stumbled off the path.

- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — The first angel's message centered the believers' attention on the Savior and directed the inhabitants of earth to the movement of the Son of God into the heavenly sanctuary. The Cross and Its Shadow 39-41.
- **Hab. 2:1-4** — "Write the vision, and make it plain upon tables." This prophecy is the basis of the prophetic charts; the vision will tarry but will surely speak and not lie. GC 391, 392; EW 14.1.

### The True Midnight Cry — Five Points of Proof

> These are the five points that brought the believers to October 22, 1844; they are the original light, the original faith, the original path.

- **Gen.** — The 6,000 years; the chronology from the beginning of Genesis, calculated as Miller did, is the first point of proof in The True Midnight Cry.
- **The 2520** — The "seven times of the Gentiles," the second point of proof, runs from the pagan rule of Babylon down nearly to our day.
- **Dan. 8:14** — The 2300 days, the most fundamental sanctuary prophecy of Adventism, the third point of proof.
- **Dan. 9:24-27** — The 70 weeks, the fourth point, reaching all the way to Jesus.
- **Heb.** — The types: the feasts, symbols, and examples of the sanctuary that teach us to understand the heavenly sanctuary; the fifth point of proof.

### The Great Disappointment Was Not a Failure of the Prophetic Periods

> God would not permit the great Advent movement to end in darkness and disappointment, nor leave His word involved in doubt and uncertainty.

- **Rev. 10** — The little book is sweet in the mouth but bitter in the belly; the bitter disappointment of October 22, 1844. CIHS 6, 7.
- **Rev. 11:15-18** — The Judgment of the Dead is connected with the sounding of the seventh trumpet, which began October 12.
- **Dan. 8:14** — The mistake was not in the reckoning of the prophetic periods but in the events expected; the sanctuary to be cleansed was the heavenly sanctuary, not the earth. GC 410.3; 10MR 269.1.

### The Heavenly Sanctuary Revealed

> Hiram Edson, stopped midway through a field, saw distinctly that the High Priest had entered the most holy place for the first time, not coming out to the earth.

- **Rev. 10** — Edson's mind was directed to the tenth chapter of Revelation, where he saw the vision had spoken and did not lie; the prophecy was fulfilled and the Judgment of the Dead began. CIHS 9.3.
- **Heb.** — The epistle to the Hebrews taught the antitypical fulfillment of the types and shadows; the believers, drawing near their Lord, were directed to the heavenly sanctuary. The Cross and Its Shadow 39-41.

### The Sanctuary, the Sabbath, and the Spirit of Prophecy United

> When light from the antitypical sanctuary service came to God's people, He gave them the Spirit of Prophecy again to reveal Christ's ministry in heaven.

- **Ex. 20:8-11** — Elder Bates urged the seventh-day Sabbath as the true Sabbath; the fourth commandment is the longest, defining the Living God who created all things. CET 85, 86.
- **Rev. 11:19** — In vision the temple of God was opened in heaven and the ark shown, with the fourth commandment in the very center of the ten precepts encircled by a soft halo of light. CET 85, 86.
- **Gen. 2:2, 3** — When the foundations of the earth were laid, the foundation of the Sabbath was also laid; had the true Sabbath been kept there would never have been an infidel or atheist. CET 85, 86.

### The Coming Image and Mark of the Beast

> The beast of Revelation 13 changed God's law regarding time, transferring the Sabbath from the seventh day to the first; an idolatrous sabbath will be set up.

- **Rev. 13:15** — Power is given to the image of the beast to speak and to cause that as many as would not worship the image should be killed.
- **Rev. 13:17** — No man may buy or sell save he that has the mark, or the name of the beast, or the number of his name; refusing the false sabbath bars buying and selling, so we should begin now to grow our own food.
- **Dan. 3** — Daniel's three friends faced an image set up to be worshiped, an idol like that of Revelation 13; God saved them as He will save the obedient.

### The Need for Another Prophetic Chart

> On returning to Brother Nichols', the Lord gave Ellen White a vision showing the truth must be made plain upon tables; it would cause many to decide for the truth by the three angels' messages.

- **Hab. 2:1-4** — As early as 1842 this prophecy had moved Charles Fitch to prepare a prophetic chart illustrating the visions of Daniel and Revelation — the 1843 chart, carrying the first two angels' messages. GC 391, 392; 4SP 241.2.
- **Hab. 2:2** — "Write the vision, and make it plain upon tables, that he may run that readeth it"; the publication of the chart was itself a fulfillment of this prophecy, and those who deny it leave the original faith. ARSH Dec. 1850, 13.6.

### The 1843 Chart and the Tarrying Time

> The hand of the Lord was removed from the figures and the mistake explained: the prophetic periods reached to 1844, not 1843.

- **Hab. 2:3** — "Though it tarry, wait for it"; the believers had overlooked the tarrying of the vision, and 1843 had to be the first published time so that the vision should tarry. EW 236.
- **Dan. 8:14** — B.C. 457 is the year from which to date the 2300 days; the 1843 date was necessary to create the tarrying time before the Midnight Cry aroused the virgin band. ARSH Dec. 1850, 13.5.

### The Truth Stands Forever as Truth

> When the power of God testifies as to what is truth, that truth is to stand forever; no after suppositions contrary to the light God has given are to be entertained.

- **Hab. 2:3** — "At the end it shall speak, and not lie"; if the chart is a fulfillment of this prophecy then what is on it is truth, and we must heed it. CIHS 19.1.

### The 1850 Chart — The Third Angel's Message Made Plain

> "God shewed me the necessity of getting out a chart. I saw it was needed." The vision was given October 23, 1850, and executed within a month in Boston. 15MR 210.3; James White letter to Bro. & Sis. Loveland.

- **Rev. 14:6-12** — The three angels' messages: the first two made plain on the 1843 chart and the third made plain on the 1850 chart, given by vision from God. 1EGWLM 242.4, 242.5.
- **Rev. 13:11-18** — In vision the two-horned beast had a dragon's mouth and its power in its head; this image of the papacy appears on the 1850 chart. 1EGWLM 244.
- **Rev. 17** — The mother of harlots was shown separate and distinct from her daughters; she has had her day, and her daughters, the Protestant sects, will exercise the power once manifested by the mother. 1EGWLM 244.

### The Charts Were Ordered by God — Touch Them Not

> "Means had been wasted in getting out charts" with uncouth, disgusting images; but God was in the publishment of the chart by Brother Nichols, and there was a prophecy of this chart in the Bible.

- **Hab. 2:2** — The truth should be made plain upon tables; the earth and its fullness are the Lord's, and necessary means should not be spared to make it plain. The 1843 and 1850 charts are the only two that fulfill Habakkuk 2. 1EGWLM 243.2.
- **Ezek. 37:1-10** — The valley of dry bones: prophesy upon these bones; the charts prophesied to God's people and the bones came together, and the prophesying to the four winds brings the sealing and a shaking that sifts the true from the false.
- **Rev. 7** — The four winds of Ezekiel's prophecy are tied to the sealing of Revelation 7; the breath enters the slain and they stand up an exceeding great army.

### Do Not Make the Spirit of Prophecy of None Effect

> "The very last deception of Satan will be to make of none effect the testimony of the Spirit of God" — done by those who claim to believe the prophet yet disregard her visions.

- **Rev. 12:17** — The dragon makes war with the remnant who keep the commandments of God and have the testimony of Jesus; to deny the chart is to make that testimony of none effect.
- **Rev. 19:10** — "The testimony of Jesus is the spirit of prophecy"; the remnant has both the commandments and the Spirit of Prophecy, and must not make them of none effect.

### Question — Where Was Jesus Before 1844?

> The 144,000 follow the Lamb whithersoever He goeth; we should be able to follow Him into the holy place where He ministered from 1840 to 1844.

- **Rev. 14:4** — The 144,000 follow the Lamb wherever He goes; therefore we can follow Him into the holy place before He entered the most holy.
- **Rev. 8:2-6** — The angel stands at the golden altar in the holy place with the censer of incense; the seven trumpets show where and when Christ ministered, exactly like the yearly tabernacle service, the courtyard being the earth and the holy place in heaven.

### Question — The Eastern Question and Armageddon

> "The last plagues, the preparing of the way of the kings of the East, and the gathering of these kings to the world-battle of Armageddon, is the final meaning and the only culmination of the Eastern Question." A. T. Jones, Medical Missionary, Aug. 21, 1906.

- **Rev. 16** — The Eastern question — the involvement of the powers of the East and the Ottoman Empire (the woes), not Islam as a religion — culminates in the seven last plagues, the way of the kings of the East, and the battle of Armageddon; this is part of the third angel's message and is on Ellen White's chart.

## Books of a New Order — Week 10

_Judgment Day Ten-Week Study. An in-depth look at Revelation 14 and the Three Angels' Messages in their order — and a warning about the "books of a new order" that would change our foundation._

### Daniel and the Revelation: The Book for This Time

> "When the books of Daniel and Revelation are better understood, believers will have an entirely different religious experience." — Testimonies to Ministers 114.3

- **Dan. 12** — Studying Daniel and Revelation gives believers an entirely different religious experience, impressing heart and mind with the character all must develop. TM 114.3
- **Rev. 1:3** — The book of Revelation, opened and unsealed in the time of the end, contains the message which all need to read and understand and is to be brought before the people as the very book for this time.
- **Matt. 24:43** — Students must carefully study Daniel and the Revelation so the day of Christ does not overtake them as a thief in the night; Satan seeks to divert minds from the truths of the first, second, and third angels' messages. Ms 174, 1899 (1MR 63.4)
- **Dan. 7; Dan. 8; Dan. 11; Rev. 8–13** — These are the great, essential questions which God would have presented to the people; in Daniel and the Revelation is found solid, eternal truth for this time.

### The Old Landmarks and the Foundation

> "I am instructed to say that these words we may use as appropriate for this time; for the time has come when sin must be called by its right name." — Letter 329, 1905, par. 24

- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — The first angel's message (1837), coupled with the Eastern question of August 11, 1840, gave the movement its power.
- **Rev. 14:8** — The second angel's message (1843), supported by the 1843 chart and the Midnight Cry, forms part of the platform of truth.
- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — The third angel's message (1844), made plain by the 1850 chart, completes the platform; the supporting messages are as important as the angels' messages themselves.
- **Hab. 2:2, 3** — The vision is to be made plain on tables; the "original faith" was founded on these charts and waymarks, and those who turn from it are lost in the fog of error. Christ in His Sanctuary 24.3; Loma Linda Messages 144.1
- **Mark 12:24** — Learned men who, with a pretense of great wisdom, teach a mystical, secret, spiritual meaning not apparent in the language are false teachers; "Ye know not the scriptures, neither the power of God." Darkness Before Dawn 38.4

### Books of a New Order — A New System of Religion

> "Were this reformation to take place, what would result? … Books of a new order would be written. A system of intellectual philosophy would be introduced." — Selected Messages vol. 1, 204.2

- **The counterfeit reformation** — The enemy seeks a counterfeit "reformation" that discards the pillars of faith, changes our religion, and accounts the foundations of the last fifty years as error. 1SM 204.2
- **Isa. 8:20** — "To the law and to the testimony": false dreams and visions contain some truth but lead away from the original faith; if men belittle God's law and trust impressions above the inspired word, there is no light in them. 2SM 98.3

### A New Set of Rules vs. Miller's Rules

> Louis F. Were's "The Certainty of the Third Angel's Message" (1945) laid out hermeneutical principles defending "a more symbolical and spiritual interpretation of prophecy." (Andrews paper, _From Clear Fulfillment to Complex Prophecy_)

- **2 Tim. 2:15** — Were's rule of "double" and "triple" application of prophecy misuses "rightly dividing the word of truth," allowing many contradictory versions of the same prophecy.
- **Rev. 9:20, 21** — Adventists once unanimously agreed that Revelation 9 was a very important prophecy; under the new hermeneutic it is now regarded as complex and non-vital — a negative shift away from the plainness God intends.
- **Rev. 13:11** — The two-horned lamblike beast that speaks as a dragon is America, the enemy of God's people; the papacy need not be the punchline of every prophecy for the interpretation to be sound.

_Supporting note: Were's book and his "Middle East Ferments and the Antichrist" (1950) charge that Uriah Smith "substituted Turkey for the Papacy as the King of the North," calling this Jesuit-fostered futurism — a charge the study answers by appeal to inspiration and Miller's literal rules._

### The Eastern Question — Turkey in Prophecy

> "The eleventh chapter of Daniel entire is a literal historical prophecy, the longest and most remarkable of its kind in all the Scriptures." — Bible Readings for the Home Circle, p. 391

- **Rev. 9:1–11** — The first woe (the fifth trumpet) depicts the Ottoman power; on Ellen White's chart, "Firearms first used on horseback by the Turks," with the woe commencing 1299.
- **Rev. 9:13–15** — The sixth trumpet (second woe) measures Ottoman supremacy to August 11, 1840.
- **Rev. 9:20, 21** — These trumpet-woes are plagues upon the idol-worshiping, persecuting papal power, with Turkey acting as a scourge against those who persecute God's people.
- **Dan. 11:45** — The King of the North (Turkey) plants the tabernacles of his palace at the glorious holy mountain — Turkey goes to Jerusalem — yet comes to his end, and none shall help him; this is what connects the prophecy with the Eastern Question. (Bible Readings for the Home Circle 391)
- **Rev. 16:12** — The sixth vial dries up the river Euphrates — which rises in Turkey, where the symbol began in Revelation 9 — meaning the nation of Turkey is finally removed before probation closes.
- **Rev. 8:13** — The three woes (woe, woe, woe) announced by the angel; the third woe "cometh quickly" and is still future, meaning Turkey is yet to come back upon the scene.
- **Great Controversy on the 1840 fulfillment** — Josiah Litch's exposition of Revelation 9 predicted the very day the Turkish government would surrender its independence; the exact fulfillment proved the correctness of Miller's principles of prophetic interpretation. GC 334
- **Uriah Smith and the Eastern question** — Ellen White rejoiced that Elder Smith, preaching the Eastern question, was "fully and thoroughly united with us; seems like Brother Smith of old," closing with a solemn address to those not preparing for "these great events in the near future." LT 55, 1884, par. 6, 7
- **The Eastern question is truth** — Elder Smith spoke upon the Eastern question to earnest attention; it was "just the subject the people wished to hear," and the prayer was that the Holy Spirit would inspire hearts "to make the truth plain." 4T 279.1; LT 10a, 1877, par. 9; MS 189, 1898, par. 9

### The Image, the Feet, and the Toes

- **Dan. 2:41–43** — God's sacred work is now represented by the feet of the image, iron mixed with miry clay — the mingling of church craft and statecraft to enforce a Sunday law. 4BC 1168
- **Dan. 7:8, 24** — In Daniel 7 three horns are plucked up by the little horn; but in the image of Daniel 2 the ten divisions are not toes cut off — the Germanic tribes (Franks, Vandals, Ostrogoths, etc.) arise in the feet and the same material runs down into the ten toes.

### The Three Unclean Spirits and Armageddon

- **Rev. 16:13** — Three unclean spirits like frogs come out of the mouth of the dragon (Pagan Rome / paganism), the beast (the papacy), and the false prophet (the image to the beast — apostate Protestant America).
- **Rev. 13:15–17** — The final stage makes void God's law: a Sunday law enforces the mark of the beast, and none may buy or sell — hence the call to prepare by country living and growing one's own food.
- **Rev. 12:3** — The great red dragon is paganism, Pagan Rome, the power that gave the beast his seat and authority.
- **Rev. 16:14, 16** — The kings of the whole world are gathered to the battle of that great day of God Almighty, to the place called in the Hebrew tongue Armageddon — a literal world-battle, not a spiritual one.
- **Rev. 16:15** — "Behold, I come as a thief: blessed is he that watcheth, and keepeth his garments" — appearing in the sixth plague, pointing to the importance of the garments at that hour.
- **The culmination of the Eastern Question** — "the last plagues, the preparing of the way of the kings of the East, and the gathering of these kings to the world-war, and the world-battle of Armageddon, is the final meaning and the only culmination of the Eastern Question." (A. T. Jones, Medical Missionary, Aug. 21, 1906)

### Daniel 11 Is Literal — A Crash Course

> "Daniel 11 doesn't deal with a kingdom based on religion but on geographic location." (on-screen note)

- **Dan. 7:16** — Daniel asks "the truth of all this," and the angel makes known "the interpretation" — truth equals interpretation.
- **Dan. 7:17** — "These great beasts, which are four, are four kings" — the beast is the symbol, but "kings" is the literal interpretation; truth = interpretation = literal = kings.
- **Dan. 11:2** — "Now will I shew thee the truth… three kings in Persia": because "kings" is never a symbol, Daniel 11 must be read literally, not spiritualized.
- **Dan. 11:3** — A mighty king (Alexander the Great) stands up and rules with great dominion.
- **Dan. 11:4** — His kingdom is broken and divided toward the four winds — Greece literally split among four generals (Ptolemy/south, Seleucus/east, Lysimachus/north toward Turkey, Cassander/west), narrowing to the King of the North and King of the South relative to Jerusalem.
- **Dan. 11:40–45** — Whoever rules the northern geographical area of the old Grecian Empire is "the King of the North"; Turkey now occupies that location, so the King of the North of verses 40–45 is Turkey.

### The Sequence of Final Events

- **Dan. 11:45** — Turkey goes to Jerusalem and comes to its end before probation closes; this happens after a Sunday law begins.
- **Dan. 12:1** — At that time — when Turkey is removed from Jerusalem — Michael stands up, and a time of trouble such as never was begins as the plagues fall.
- **Rev. 16:12** — The kings of the earth first annihilate Turkey (the Euphrates dried up), clearing the way for the kings of the East.
- **Rev. 16:16** — The wicked gather to Armageddon in the sixth plague, contending over Jerusalem, but God stops the battle at the very end — this is the literal close of the Eastern question.

## The Shaking is Here — Week 11

### The Shaking and the Falling Away

> This bonus study moves from the opening of the judgment to its closing, and begins the conversation on the judgment of the living. The shaking is the start of that work.

- **2 Thess. 2:1-3** — Before the day of Christ there must come a shaking and a falling away first; let no man deceive you. The remnant are not prepared for what is coming; the cry is "Get ready, get ready, get ready," for the fierce anger of the Lord is soon to come, His wrath poured out unmixed with mercy. EW 119.1
- **Rev. 16:1** — God's seven angels pour out the vials of His wrath upon the earth — the fierce anger unmixed with mercy, the close of probation.

### The Straight Testimony to Laodicea

> The shaking is caused by the straight testimony called forth by the counsel of the True Witness.

- **Rev. 3:17** — Laodicea says "I am rich and increased with goods," not knowing she is wretched, miserable, poor, blind, and naked. CET 176.1
- The shaking results when some will not bear the straight testimony but rise up against it; the True Witness is Jesus, who calls it forth. CET 176.1
- The solemn testimony has been lightly esteemed, if not entirely disregarded; it must work deep repentance, and all who receive it will obey and be purified. CET 176.2

### The Harvest, the Wheat, and the Tares

- **Matt. 13:28-30** — Let both grow together until the harvest; the tares are bound and burned, the wheat gathered into the barn. The tares look like wheat, not like obvious weeds.
- **4T 306.3** — God's people trace down the roll of fast-fulfilling prophecy and purify their souls by obeying the truth, that they not be found without the wedding garment.
- **Rev. 16:15** — "Behold, I come as a thief. Blessed is he that watcheth, and keepeth his garments, lest he walk naked." Watching requires clean eyes; clothing requires the garment.

### The Mighty Shaking Has Commenced

> The mighty shaking has commenced and will go on. CET 107.3

- All will be shaken out who are not willing to take a bold, unyielding stand for the truth; it must be a freewill offering. CET 107.3
- **Hab. 2:2** — Write the vision and make it plain; the truth made plain. The lightnings of Sinai will not move those unmoved by the plain truths of the word.

### The Fourth Angel and the Light Despised

> There is to be in the churches a wonderful manifestation of the power of God, but it will not move those who have not humbled themselves. Mar 219.2

- **Rev. 18:1** — Another angel comes down having great power, and the earth is lightened with his glory — the straight truth from the True Witness given to Laodicea.
- In their blindness they see the light as something dangerous that arouses their fears, and brace themselves to resist it, opposing the work. Mar 219.2
- **TM 112.1** — When the shaking comes by the introduction of false theories, surface readers anchored nowhere are like shifting sand; Daniel and Revelation must be studied, as well as the other prophecies of the Old and New Testaments.

### Refusing the Light: The Babylon Parallel

- **DAR 612.2 (Uriah Smith)** — When the churches refused the second-advent message, loss of spirituality and a moral fall followed; rejecting truth forges the shackles of error. The same can happen in our church.
- **Way Marks and High Heaps p. 64** — When the message was first introduced in 1843, the churches treated it with scorn; some looked favorably until it was presented in clear scriptural light, then withdrew and declared it the doctrine of the devil.

### Ezekiel's Vision: Prophesying to the Dry Bones

- **Ezek. 37:1-3** — Ezekiel is set in a valley full of very dry bones and asked, "Son of man, can these bones live?"
- **Ezek. 37:4-7** — He is commanded to prophesy to the bones; as he prophesies there is a noise and a shaking, and the bones come together. The preaching of prophecy to Laodicea causes the shaking.
- **Ezek. 37:8-10** — He prophesies to the wind, and breath from the four winds enters the slain; they live and stand up an exceeding great army.
- **Rev. 14:6, 7** — Prophesying that gives the bones flesh, skin, and the knowledge that He is God echoes the first angel's message.

### The Latter Rain and the Harvest

- **Deut. 11:14** — God gives the first rain and the latter rain, that you may gather your corn, wine, and oil.
- **Jer. 5:24** — God gives both the former and latter rain in its season, reserving the appointed weeks of the harvest; the latter rain prepares for the harvest.
- **Joel 2:23** — Rejoice, children of Zion, for He gives the former rain and the latter rain in the first month — a prophecy for right now.
- **Joel 2:1-4** — Blow the trumpet in Zion; the day of the Lord is at hand, a day of darkness and gloominess.
- **Rev. 16:14, 15** — The spirits of devils gather the kings to the battle of the great day of God Almighty; again the warning about the garments.

### The Rain Is Doctrine — and the Foundation

> The rain is doctrine, the true prophecies of the end of the world coming from the True Witness.

- **Deut. 32:2** — "My doctrine shall drop as the rain, my speech shall distil as the dew" — the latter rain is the outpouring of true prophetic truth.
- **1 Tim. 4:1** — In the latter times some shall depart from the faith, giving heed to seducing spirits and doctrines of devils; spiritualizing the scripture is spiritualism.
- **Mar 28.3** — In the absence of persecution some appear sound, but in the crisis, when the law of God is made void, the church is sifted and many give heed to seducing spirits and doctrines of devils.
- **4T 89.2** — The refining process proves some to be dross; the shaking of God blows away multitudes like dry leaves, for they are not steadfast with God.
- **Mar 219.4** — There must be no neglect of the former rain; only those living up to the light they have will recognize the manifestation of the Holy Spirit in the latter rain.

### Everything That Can Be Shaken

- **Rev. 6:13** — The stars fall as a fig tree casts her untimely figs when shaken of a mighty wind — the wind aids the shaking.
- **Matt. 11:7** — "What went ye out into the wilderness to see? A reed shaken with the wind?" The wind sifts out those who will not aid the fourth angel's message.
- **Heb. 12:27** — "Yet once more" signifies the removing of those things that are shaken, that the things which cannot be shaken may remain.

### The Image of the Beast (Q&A — The 1850 Chart)

> Q&A on the 1850 chart: how can the beast's number be 666 if the chart places that name on the lamb-like-horned beast?

- **Rev. 13:11** — A beast comes up out of the earth with two horns like a lamb but speaks as a dragon — America, exercising all the power of the first beast.
- **Rev. 13:14** — This beast deceives those on the earth and bids them make an image to the first beast which had the deadly wound. An image looks much like the original.
- **Rev. 17:5** — Upon her forehead is written "Mystery, Babylon the Great, the Mother of Harlots"; the lamb-horned beast (Protestant America) makes an image that bears the same number 666 because it acts exactly like the mother.

## Has the Judgment of the Living Begun? Part 1 — Week 12

### The Shaking: The Straight Testimony to Laodicea

> The truth of prophecy is the straight testimony that wakes God's people; some will not bear it, and that is what causes the shaking.

- **Deut. 32:2** — God's doctrine drops as the rain; the latter rain is not a strange outpouring but the doctrine itself, the prophetic truth of the end times.
- **Ezek. 37:1-10** — The valley of very dry bones must be shaken and breathed upon; God commands His servant to prophesy to the bones, and prophecy is what wakes the dead.
- **Ezek. 37:7-10** — As the prophet prophesied there was a noise and a shaking; he prophesies to the wind to come from the four winds, and breath enters the slain so they live as an exceeding great army.

> "I asked the meaning of the shaking... it would be caused by the straight testimony called forth by the counsel of the True Witness to the Laodiceans... Some will not bear this straight testimony. They will rise up against it." CET 176.1

### The Sealing: Holding the Four Winds

- **Rev. 7:1-4** — Four angels stand on the four corners of the earth holding the four winds until the servants of God are sealed in their foreheads; earth, sea, and trees represent peoples, and the angel from the East brings the light of truth.
- **Rev. 7:2** — The sealing angel ascends from the East, as the sun rises bringing light; light represents truth, and this message seals.
- **Rev. 7:3, 4** — The winds are held back until the 144,000 of the tribes of Israel are sealed in their foreheads.

### Ezekiel 9: The Mark and the Judgment Beginning at the House of God

- **Ezek. 9:1-3** — A loud cry calls the men with destroying weapons; one man among them is clothed in linen with a writer's inkhorn, like a pen by his side.
- **Ezek. 9:4** — The man with the inkhorn goes through the midst of Jerusalem (the final church movement) and sets a mark — a seal — upon the foreheads of those who sigh and cry for the abominations.
- **Ezek. 9:5, 6** — The slaughter begins at the sanctuary, at the ancient men before the house; God's people of position are judged first, then the laity, then the world.

### The Vineyard, the Fruit, and the Slaughter Wind

- **Isa. 27:2, 3** — God's people are a vineyard which He waters every moment — watered with the latter rain.
- **Isa. 27:6** — Jacob takes root, and Israel blossoms and buds and fills the face of the world with fruit; this is the time of harvest.
- **Isa. 27:7, 8** — The day of fruit is also the day of slaughter; in the time of the latter rain He debates with His people, staying His rough wind in the day of the east wind.
- **Hos. 8:7** — They have sown the wind and shall reap the whirlwind — the four winds from all directions bringing destruction in their path.
- **Rev. 7:1** — Back to the four angels holding the four winds: the winds would blow on God's people, so He must first seal His servants.

### What the Seal Is: A King's Signet on His People

- **Rev. 9:4** — The locusts are commanded not to hurt the grass, green things, or trees, but only those men who lack the seal of God — a past historical event, yet showing the seal protects God's people.
- **Gen. 41:40-42** — Pharaoh sets Joseph over all Egypt and gives him his ring; the ring carries the king's authority.
- **Esther 3:10-12** — The king gives Haman his ring; the decree is written in the king's name and sealed with the king's ring, so it carries the king's authority and cannot be questioned.
- **Esther 8:8** — The writing sealed with the king's ring may no man reverse; this is why the seal matters — it is given in the king's name and cannot be reversed.
- **Rev. 7** — The 144,000 have the seal of the living God and the Father's name written in their foreheads; they come proclaiming the loud cry in the Father's name, stamped with the seal of the King.

### The Sabbath: The Seal of the Living God

- **Ex. 31:13** — God's sabbaths are a sign between Him and His people, that they may know He is the Lord that sanctifies them; the same thing that woke the bones makes them know the Lord.
- **Rom. 4:15** — Where no law is, there is no transgression; Satan would remove the law to remove sin, but to remove the law he must remove its seal.
- **Ex. 20:8-11** — The fourth commandment contains the seal of God: His name (the Lord thy God), His territory (heaven and earth), and His title (Creator); remove the seventh day and you remove the seal.
- **Isa. 8:16** — Bind up the testimony, seal the law among my disciples — the Sabbath is that seal of the law.
- **Rev. 14:12** — Here is the patience of the saints who keep the commandments of God (including the fourth, which contains the seal) and the faith of Jesus — the doctrine of the latter rain and the Three Angels' messages.

### The Seal Tested: The Mark of the Beast in Opposition

> The seal must be tested through observance or disregard of the Sabbath; the three Hebrews were sealed in the test on the plains of Dura, willing to die rather than bow.

- **Rev. 13:16, 17** — The beast causes all to receive a mark in the right hand or forehead, that no man might buy or sell without it — a counterfeit seal set in direct opposition to God's.
- **Rev. 13:11, 12** — Another beast (Protestant America) comes up out of the earth with two lamblike horns but speaks as a dragon, causing the world to worship the first beast whose deadly wound was healed; the mark therefore has to do with worship.
- **Dan. 7:25** — This power speaks great words against the Most High and thinks to change times and laws — the times and laws of God; the only commandment dealing with time is the fourth, so the mark is the transfer of the Sabbath from the seventh day to the first.
- **2SM 80.4** — "The Lord has shown me clearly that the image of the beast will be formed before probation closes; for it is to be the great test for the people of God, by which their eternal destiny will be decided." (the great test that decides eternal destiny, i.e. the close of probation)
- **Ev 234.2** — "No one has yet received the mark of the beast. The testing time has not yet come... None are condemned until they have had the light and have seen the obligation of the fourth commandment. But when the decree shall go forth enforcing the counterfeit sabbath, and the loud cry of the third angel shall warn men against the worship of the beast and his image, the line will be clearly drawn." (defines the loud cry as the warning against the mark)
- **Mar 164.4** — "This is the test that the people of God must have before they are sealed... All who prove their loyalty to God by observing His law, and refusing to accept a spurious sabbath... will receive the seal of the living God. Those who yield the truth... and accept the Sunday sabbath, will receive the mark of the beast."

### The Loud Cry: The Fourth Angel's Message

- **Rev. 18:1-4** — Another angel comes down with great power, and the earth is lightened with his glory; he cries "Babylon is fallen," and a voice says "Come out of her, my people," that ye be not partakers of her sins and receive not of her plagues — the final loud cry call.
- **Rev. 14:9, 10** — The third angel warns with a loud voice that whoever worships the beast and his image and receives his mark shall drink the wine of the wrath of God, poured out without mixture.
- **Rev. 16:2** — The first vial is poured upon the earth, and a grievous sore falls upon those who have the mark of the beast and worship his image; the plagues fall on those who take the mark, so the seal protects God's people from them.
- **Mar 200.2** — "Just as soon as the people of God are sealed in their foreheads — it is not any seal or mark that can be seen, but a settling into the truth, both intellectually and spiritually... it will come. Indeed, it has begun already; the judgments of God are now upon the land, to give us warning." (the sealing is a settling into the truth; the shaking has begun)

### Standing on the Platform: The 1842-1844 Foundation

> Nothing is to disturb the foundation of faith laid since the message came in 1842, 1843, and 1844; this truth is to be as the Rock of Ages.

- **Rev. 10** — Time is no longer; there are no more day-for-a-year time prophecies to predict these events — beware those who set times of three-and-a-half or seven years; we know the events, not the dates.
- **CIHS 188.1** — "Solemn are the scenes connected with the closing work of the atonement... The judgment is now passing in the sanctuary above... Soon — none know how soon — it will pass to the cases of the living." (Quotes Mark 13:33 and Rev. 3:3 — watch, lest He come as a thief.)

### Q&A: The 144,000 and the Great Multitude

- **Rev. 9:4** — The locusts hurt only those men without the seal; the grass, green things, and trees represent people, so the locusts devour people who lack the seal of God, not literal vegetation.
- **Rev. 17:15** — The waters represent peoples, multitudes, nations, and tongues — the Bible interprets its own symbols.
- **Rev. 7:9** — The great multitude no man can number, of all nations and tongues, stands before the throne with white robes and palms in their hands — distinct from the 144,000.
- **EW 39.3** — Enoch, translated alive and not an Israelite, bears a glorious palm on his right arm; like the great multitude with palms, a type of those who go to heaven without seeing death.
- **EW 16.2** — The 144,000 stand on the sea of glass clothed with a glorious white mantle (a priestly garment) from shoulders to feet — distinct from the great multitude's white robes and palms.
- **Rev. 7:9, 14** — The great multitude come out of great tribulation, alive through the plagues until Jesus appears, so they cannot be the risen dead; both they and the 144,000 are living when Jesus comes.
